# Output-Optimal Algorithms for Join-Aggregate Queries

连接-聚合查询的输出最优算法

XIAO HU, University of Waterloo, Canada

胡晓，加拿大滑铁卢大学

One of the most celebrated results of computing join-aggregate queries defined over commutative semi-rings is the classic Yannakakis algorithm proposed in 1981. It is known that the runtime of the Yannakakis algorithm is $O\left( {N + \text{OUT}}\right)$ for any free-connex query,where $N$ is the input size of the database and OUT is the output size of the query result. This is already output-optimal. However,only an upper bound $O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ on the runtime is known for the large remaining class of acyclic but non-free-connex queries. Alternatively, one can convert a non-free-connex query into a free-connex one using tree decomposition techniques and then run the Yannakakis algorithm. This approach takes $O\left( {{N}^{\text{#fn-subw }} + \text{OUT}}\right)$ time,where #fn-subw is the free-connex sub-modular width of the query. But, none of these results is known to be output-optimal.

在可交换半环上定义的连接-聚合查询计算领域，最著名的成果是1981年提出的经典Yannakakis算法。该算法对于任何自由连接查询（free-connex query）的时间复杂度为$O\left( {N + \text{OUT}}\right)$，其中$N$表示数据库输入规模，OUT表示查询结果的输出规模。这已是输出最优的算法。然而，对于更大一类无环但非自由连接的查询（acyclic but non-free-connex queries），目前仅知运行时上限为$O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$。另一种方法是通过树分解技术将非自由连接查询转换为自由连接查询后执行Yannakakis算法，该方法耗时$O\left( {{N}^{\text{#fn-subw }} + \text{OUT}}\right)$（其中#fn-subw表示查询的自由连接子模宽度）。但上述方法均未达到输出最优。

In this paper,we show a matching lower and upper bound $\Theta \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \mathrm{{fhtw}}}} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ for computing general acyclic join-aggregate queries by semiring algorithms, where fn-fhtw is the free-connex fractional hypertree width of the query. For example, fn-fhtw = 1 for free-connex queries, fn-fhtw = 2 for line queries (a.k.a. chain matrix multiplication),and fn-fhtw $= k$ for star queries (a.k.a. star matrix multiplication) with $k$ relations. Although free-connex fractional hypertree width is a natural and well-established measure of how far a join-aggregate query is from being free-connex, we demonstrate that it precisely captures the output-optimal complexity of these queries. To our knowledge, this has been the first polynomial improvement over the Yannakakis algorithm in the last 40 years and completely resolves the open question of computing acyclic join-aggregate queries in an output-optimal way. As a by-product, our output-optimal algorithm for acyclic queries also yields new output-sensitive algorithms for cyclic queries via tree decomposition techniques.

本文通过半环算法证明了无环连接-聚合查询计算的下界与上界匹配为$\Theta \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \mathrm{{fhtw}}}} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$，其中fn-fhtw表示查询的自由连接分数超树宽度。例如：自由连接查询的fn-fhtw=1，线性查询（即链式矩阵乘法）的fn-fhtw=2，含$k$个关系的星型查询（即星型矩阵乘法）的fn-fhtw$= k$。尽管自由连接分数超树宽度是衡量连接-聚合查询偏离自由连接程度的经典指标，但我们证明它精确刻画了这类查询的输出最优复杂度。这是40年来首次对Yannakakis算法做出的多项式改进，彻底解决了无环连接-聚合查询输出最优计算这一开放性问题。作为副产品，我们的无环查询输出最优算法还通过树分解技术为循环查询提供了新的输出敏感算法。

## 1 INTRODUCTION

## 1 引言

Join-aggregate queries defined over commutative semi-rings have wide applications in data analytical tasks. For example, join-aggregate queries over Boolean semiring can capture the CNF satisfiability problem,the $k$ -colorability problem on graphs,the Boolean conjunctive query [2],the constraint satisfaction problem, and the list recovery problem in coding theory [28]. As another example, join-aggregate queries over sum-product semiring have been widely used in complex network analysis (such as clustering coefficients and transitivity ratio), discrete Fourier transforms, graph analysis (such as homomorphism and Holant problem [17]), counting quantified conjunctive query, and permanent computation of matrices. Finally, join-aggregate queries over max-product semiring can capture the maximum a posteriori problem in probabilistic graph models and maximum likelihood decoding in linear codes [5]. We refer interested readers to $\left\lbrack  {3,5,{23},{41}}\right\rbrack$ for many more applications.

基于可交换半环的连接-聚合查询在数据分析任务中应用广泛。例如：布尔半环上的连接-聚合查询可建模CNF可满足性问题、图的$k$着色问题、布尔连接查询[2]、约束满足问题及编码理论中的列表恢复问题[28]；和-积半环上的查询广泛应用于复杂网络分析（如聚类系数和传递比率）、离散傅里叶变换、图分析（如同态问题和Holant问题[17]）、计数量化连接查询及矩阵积和式计算；而最大-积半环上的查询可建模概率图模型中的最大后验问题及线性码的最大似然解码[5]。更多应用详见$\left\lbrack  {3,5,{23},{41}}\right\rbrack$。

Finding efficient algorithms for computing join-aggregate queries has been a holy grail in database theory since 1981. Previous results have achieved two flavors of runtimes: worst-case optimal [8, 39, ${46},{56},{63}\rbrack$ and output-sensitive $\left\lbrack  {{39},{63}}\right\rbrack$ . Worst-case optimal bounds are tight only on pathological instances with huge outputs, which are rare in practice. In contrast, output-sensitive bounds express the runtime as a function of the input size $N$ and output size OUT,which are more practically meaningful, especially for queries where the aggregation may significantly reduce the output size. In addition, output-sensitive algorithms can imply worst-case optimal ones: the classical Yannakakis algorithm [63] is the best-known example, which achieves an output-optimal bound of $O\left( {N + \text{OUT}}\right)$ for free-connex queries (including acyclic joins as special cases). Although output-optimal algorithms are practically desirable, they are much more difficult to design. Figure 1 illustrates the relationships between different classes of acyclic join-aggregate queries. For the largest class of acyclic but non-free-connex queries (in shadow area in Figure 1), prior works have yet to discover an output-optimal algorithm. Currently, there are only two approaches for computing these queries: (1) run the Yannakakis algorithm; (2) convert the query into a free-connex one using the tree decomposition technique and the worst-case optimal join algorithm [45, 47], and then run the Yannakakis algorithm on the tree decomposition. For (1),Yannakakis only gave an upper bound $O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ on its runtime. Later,this bound has been tightened to $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$ for star queries with $k$ relations (the matrix multiplication query is the special case with two relations), which is already output-optimal [51]. For (2),Khamis et al. showed an upper bound $O\left( {{N}^{\text{#fn-subw }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ on its runtime, where #fn-subw is the #free-connex submodular width of the query [39]. Both algorithms are worst-case optimal (see Appendix B), but neither is output-optimal.

自1981年以来，寻找计算连接聚合查询的高效算法一直是数据库理论界的圣杯。现有成果已实现两种时间复杂度：最坏情况最优[8,39,${46},{56},{63}\rbrack$]和输出敏感$\left\lbrack  {{39},{63}}\right\rbrack$。最坏情况最优边界仅在输出量巨大的病态实例中成立，这类实例在实践中罕见。相比之下，输出敏感边界将时间复杂度表示为输入规模$N$与输出规模OUT的函数，更具实践意义——尤其当聚合操作可能大幅缩减输出规模时。此外，输出敏感算法可隐含最坏情况最优性：经典Yannakakis算法[63]就是著名例证，它为自由连接查询（包含无环连接作为特例）实现了$O\left( {N + \text{OUT}}\right)$的输出最优边界。虽然输出最优算法极具实用价值，但其设计难度更高。图1展示了各类无环连接聚合查询的关系。对于最大类的无环非自由连接查询（图1阴影区），现有研究尚未发现输出最优算法。目前仅有两种计算方案：(1)运行Yannakakis算法；(2)通过树分解技术[45,47]将查询转换为自由连接形式后执行Yannakakis算法。方案(1)中，Yannakakis仅给出$O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$的时间上界。后续研究将该边界压缩至$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$（针对含$k$个关系的星型查询，其中两关系矩阵乘法查询为特例），已达输出最优[51]。方案(2)中，Khamis等学者证明其时间上界为$O\left( {{N}^{\text{#fn-subw }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$（#fn-subw表示查询的#自由连接子模宽度[39]）。两种算法均属最坏情况最优（见附录B），但均非输出最优。

In this work, we identify the free-connex fractional hypertree width (fn-fhtw) for join-aggregate queries to characterize the output-optimal complexity. We develop a matching lower and upper bound $\Theta \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \mathrm{{fhtw}}}} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ for computing general acyclic join-aggregate queries. Note that fn-fhtw $= 1$ for the free-connex queries and fn-fhtw $= k$ for star queries with $k$ relations, thereby generalizing the previous results on these two special cases. Furthermore, since this bound is output-optimal, it unifies and improves previously mentioned incomparable approaches for acyclic queries. As a by-product, our output-optimal algorithm for acyclic queries also yields new output-sensitive algorithms for cyclic queries, although their optimality remains unclear. In addition to our algorithmic contribution, we prove that several important notions of width identified in the literature, such as #free-connex submodular width [40] and free-connex submodular width [40], collapse to free-connex fractional hypertree width fn-fhtw on acyclic queries. This surprising finding further verifies our intuition that this is the right notion to capture the output-optimal complexity of acyclic join-aggregate queries.

本研究提出用自由连接分数超树宽度(fn-fhtw)刻画连接聚合查询的输出最优复杂度。我们为一般无环连接聚合查询建立了匹配的上下界$\Theta \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \mathrm{{fhtw}}}} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$。值得注意的是，自由连接查询满足fn-fhtw$= 1$，含$k$个关系的星型查询满足fn-fhtw$= k$，从而推广了这两类特例的现有结论。由于该边界具备输出最优性，它统一并改进了前述针对无环查询的不可比方案。作为副产品，我们的无环查询输出最优算法还为循环查询提供了新的输出敏感算法（尽管其最优性尚待验证）。除算法贡献外，我们证明文献中多个重要宽度概念（如#自由连接子模宽度[40]和自由连接子模宽度[40]）在无环查询中均坍缩为fn-fhtw。这一惊人发现进一步验证了我们的直觉：fn-fhtw是捕捉无环连接聚合查询输出最优复杂度的正确维度。

### 1.1 Problem Definition

### 1.1 问题定义

Join Queries. A (natural) join is defined as a hypergraph $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,where the set of vertices $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{\ell }}\right\}$ model the attributes and the set of hyperedges $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},\ldots ,{e}_{k}}\right\}   \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$ model the relations. Let $\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$ be the domain of attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ . Let $\operatorname{dom}\left( X\right)  = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{x \in  X}}\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$ be the domain of a subset $X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ of attributes. An instance of $q$ is associated a set of relations $\mathcal{R} = \left\{  {{R}_{e} : e \in  \mathcal{E}}\right\}$ . Each relation ${R}_{e}$ consists of a set of tuples,where each tuple $t \in  {R}_{e}$ is an assignment that assigns a value from $\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$ to $x$ for every attribute $x \in  e$ . The hypergraph $q$ is called self-join-free if every relation ${R}_{e}$ is distinct. In this work,we focus on self-join-free queries. More specifically, our lower bounds assume self-join-free queries, but our algorithm can be applied to the case when self-join exists. The full join result of $q$ on $\mathcal{R}$ ,denoted as $q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ ,is defined as $q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = \left\{  {t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  : \forall e \in  \mathcal{E},{\pi }_{e}t \in  {R}_{e}}\right\}$ ,i.e.,all combinations of tuples,one from each relation, such that they share the same values on their common attributes.

连接查询。一个(自然)连接被定义为一个超图$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，其中顶点集$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{\ell }}\right\}$建模属性，超边集$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},\ldots ,{e}_{k}}\right\}   \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$建模关系。设$\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$为属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$的域。设$\operatorname{dom}\left( X\right)  = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{x \in  X}}\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$为属性子集$X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$的域。$q$的实例关联着一组关系$\mathcal{R} = \left\{  {{R}_{e} : e \in  \mathcal{E}}\right\}$。每个关系${R}_{e}$由一组元组构成，其中每个元组$t \in  {R}_{e}$是一个赋值，为每个属性$x \in  e$从$\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$分配一个值给$x$。若每个关系${R}_{e}$都互不相同，则称超图$q$为无自连接。本研究中我们聚焦无自连接查询。具体而言，我们的下界假设查询无自连接，但算法可应用于存在自连接的情况。$q$在$\mathcal{R}$上的完全连接结果记为$q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$，其定义为$q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = \left\{  {t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  : \forall e \in  \mathcal{E},{\pi }_{e}t \in  {R}_{e}}\right\}$，即来自各关系的元组所有可能组合，且这些组合在共有属性上取值相同。

Given a join query $q$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ ,the effective domain of a subset of attributes $X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ is defined as the collection of tuples in $\operatorname{dom}\left( X\right)$ that appears in at least one full join result of $q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ , i.e.,the projection of $q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ onto $X$ .

给定连接查询$q$和实例$\mathcal{R}$，属性子集$X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$的有效域定义为：在$q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的至少一个完全连接结果中出现过的$\operatorname{dom}\left( X\right)$中元组的集合，即$q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$在$X$上的投影。

Join-Aggregate Queries. A join-aggregate query is defined as a triple $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,where $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is a (natural) join,and $\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ is the set of output attributes. Let $\left( {\mathbf{D},\oplus ,\otimes ,\mathbf{0},\mathbf{1}}\right)$ be a commutative semi-ring. We consider an instance $\mathcal{R}$ for $Q$ with annotated relations [27,38]. Every tuple $t$ is associated with an annotation $w\left( t\right)  \in  \mathbf{D}$ . The annotation of a full join result $t \in  q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ is $w\left( t\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bigotimes }_{e \in  \mathcal{E}}w\left( {{\pi }_{e}t}\right)$ . The query result of $\mathcal{Q}$ on $\mathcal{R}$ is defined as

连接-聚合查询。连接-聚合查询定义为一个三元组$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，其中$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$是(自然)连接，$\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$为输出属性集。设$\left( {\mathbf{D},\oplus ,\otimes ,\mathbf{0},\mathbf{1}}\right)$为交换半环。我们考虑带标注关系[27,38]的$Q$实例$\mathcal{R}$。每个元组$t$关联一个标注$w\left( t\right)  \in  \mathbf{D}$。完全连接结果$t \in  q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的标注是$w\left( t\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bigotimes }_{e \in  \mathcal{E}}w\left( {{\pi }_{e}t}\right)$。查询$\mathcal{Q}$在$\mathcal{R}$上的结果定义为

$$
\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {{t}_{\mathbf{y}},w\left( {t}_{\mathbf{y}}\right) }\right)  : {t}_{\mathbf{y}} \in  {\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}q\left( \mathcal{R}\right) ,w\left( {t}_{\mathbf{y}}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{t \in  q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  : {\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}t = {t}_{\mathbf{y}}}w\left( t\right) }\right\}  .
$$

In plain language,a join-aggregate query (semantically) first computes the full join result $q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ and the annotation of each result,which is the $\otimes$ -aggregate of the tuples comprising the join result. Then it partitions $q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ into groups by the attributes in $\mathbf{y}$ . Finally,for each group,it computes the $\oplus$ -aggregate of the annotations of the join result in that group. As mentioned,join-aggregate queries include many common database queries as special cases. For example, if we ignore the annotations,it becomes a join-project query ${\pi }_{\mathrm{y}}q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ ,also known as a conjunctive query. If we take D be the domain of integers and set $w\left( t\right)  = 1$ for every tuple $t$ ,it becomes the COUNT (*) GROUP BY $\mathbf{y}$ query; in particular,if $\mathbf{y} = \varnothing$ ,the query computes the full join size $\left| {q\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$ . If we take $\mathcal{V} = \{ A,B,C\}$ with $\mathbf{y} = \{ A,C\}$ ,and $\mathcal{E} = \{ \{ A,B\} ,\{ B,C\} \}$ ,it becomes the matrix multiplication query. If we take $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{k},B}\right\}$ with $\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}\right\}$ ,and $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {\left\{  {{A}_{1},B}\right\}  ,\cdots \left\{  {{A}_{k},B}\right\}  }\right\}$ ,it becomes a star query. If we take $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{k + 1}}\right\}$ for $k \geq  3$ with $\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{k + 1}}\right\}$ ,and $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {\left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {{A}_{k},{A}_{k + 1}}\right\}  }\right\}$ ,it becomes a line query.

通俗地说，连接-聚合查询（语义上）首先计算完整的连接结果$q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$及每个结果的注解值——该值是构成连接结果的元组$\otimes$聚合运算所得。接着按$\mathbf{y}$中的属性将$q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$分组。最后对每个组，计算该组内连接结果注解值的$\oplus$聚合值。如前所述，连接-聚合查询涵盖多种常见数据库查询特例：若忽略注解值，则退化为连接-投影查询${\pi }_{\mathrm{y}}q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$（即合取查询）；若设定D为整数域且为每个元组$t$设置$w\left( t\right)  = 1$，则转化为COUNT(*) GROUP BY $\mathbf{y}$查询——特别当$\mathbf{y} = \varnothing$时，该查询计算的是全连接规模$\left| {q\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$；若采用$\mathcal{V} = \{ A,B,C\}$且$\mathbf{y} = \{ A,C\}$与$\mathcal{E} = \{ \{ A,B\} ,\{ B,C\} \}$，则形成矩阵乘法查询；若采用$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{k},B}\right\}$且$\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}\right\}$与$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {\left\{  {{A}_{1},B}\right\}  ,\cdots \left\{  {{A}_{k},B}\right\}  }\right\}$，则构成星型查询；若对$k \geq  3$采用$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{k + 1}}\right\}$且$\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{k + 1}}\right\}$与$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {\left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {{A}_{k},{A}_{k + 1}}\right\}  }\right\}$，则转化为线性查询。

Below,if not specified,a query always refers to a join-aggregate query. We use $N = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\left| {R}_{e}\right|$ to denote the input size of $\mathcal{R}$ and OUT $= \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$ to denote the output size of $\mathcal{Q}$ over $\mathcal{R}$ . We study the data complexity of this problem by assuming the query size (i.e., $\left| \mathcal{V}\right|$ and $\left| \mathcal{E}\right|$ ) as constants.

下文若无特别说明，"查询"均指连接-聚合查询。我们用$N = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\left| {R}_{e}\right|$表示$\mathcal{R}$的输入规模，用OUT$= \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$表示$\mathcal{R}$上$\mathcal{Q}$的输出规模。通过假设查询规模（即$\left| \mathcal{V}\right|$和$\left| \mathcal{E}\right|$）为常量，我们研究该问题的数据复杂度。

Model of Computation. We use the standard RAM model with uniform cost measures. A tuple or a semiring element is stored in a word. Copying one semiring element or combining two semiring elements via a semiring operation $\left( {\oplus \text{and} \otimes  }\right)$ can be done in $O\left( 1\right)$ time. Inheriting from [50],we confine ourselves to semiring algorithms that work with semiring elements as an abstract type and can only copy them from existing semiring elements or combine them using $\oplus$ or $\otimes$ . No other operations on semi-ring elements are allowed, such as division, subtraction, or equality check.

计算模型。我们采用具有统一成本度量的标准RAM模型。一个元组或半环元素存储于一个字中。复制半环元素或通过半环运算$\left( {\oplus \text{and} \otimes  }\right)$合并两个半环元素可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内完成。继承自[50]的约束，我们限定半环算法仅能对半环元素进行抽象类型操作：从现有元素复制，或使用$\oplus$与$\otimes$进行组合。禁止对半环元素执行除法、减法或相等性检查等其他操作。

Output-Optimality. To establish output-optimality for algorithm design, we consider a unified output-sensitive upper and lower bound in Definition 1.1.

输出最优性。为确立算法设计的输出最优性，我们在定义1.1中考虑了一个统一的输出敏感上界与下界。

Definition 1.1 (Output-sensitive Bound). For a self-join-free query $Q$ ,let $f\left( Q\right)$ be the smallest exponent such that for any parameters $1 \leq  N$ and OUT $\leq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime } \in  \mathfrak{R}\left( N\right) }}\left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|$ ,a semi-ring algorithm exists that can compute $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ for any instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT within $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{f\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time,where $\Re \left( N\right)$ is the set of all instances over $Q$ of input size $N$ .

定义1.1（输出敏感界）。对于无自连接查询$Q$，令$f\left( Q\right)$为最小指数，使得对于任意参数$1 \leq  N$和输出量OUT$\leq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime } \in  \mathfrak{R}\left( N\right) }}\left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|$，存在一个半环算法能在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{f\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间内，为输入规模为$N$、输出规模为OUT的任何实例$\mathcal{R}$计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$，其中$\Re \left( N\right)$表示所有输入规模为$N$的$Q$实例集合。

<!-- Media -->

This bound is a monotonic function of $f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ . A smaller $f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ implies a smaller runtime,i.e., a better upper bound. For example, $f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  1$ implies $O\left( {N + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ ; and $f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq   + \infty$ implies $O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ . This definition can also express lower bounds. For example, $f\left( Q\right)  \geq  1$ implies $\Omega \left( {N + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ . Below,we characterize $f\left( \cdot \right)$ for acyclic queries with both lower and upper bounds.

该上界是$f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$的单调函数。较小的$f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$意味着更短运行时间，即更优上界。例如，$f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  1$推导出$O\left( {N + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$；$f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq   + \infty$则推导出$O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$。此定义亦可表达下界，如$f\left( Q\right)  \geq  1$蕴含$\Omega \left( {N + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$。下文我们将通过上下界共同刻画$f\left( \cdot \right)$无环查询的特性。

### 1.2 Our New Lower Bound for Acyclic Queries

### 1.2 无环查询的新下界

Prior work has provided lower bounds of $f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ . First, $f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  1$ for all queries since any algorithm must read the input data and output all query results. Pagh et al. showed $f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  k$ for star queries with $k$ relations [50]. Hu identified the free-width for an acyclic query $Q$ ,denoted as freew(Q),and showed that $f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  \operatorname{freew}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ [31]. In this paper,we prove:

前人研究已给出$f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$的下界。首先，$f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  1$适用于所有查询，因算法必须读取输入数据并输出全部结果。Pagh等学者证明了含$k$个关系的星型查询满足$f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  k$[50]。Hu提出了无环查询的自由宽度freew(Q)，并证明$f\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  \operatorname{freew}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$[31]。本文中我们进一步证明：

THEOREM 1.2. For any acyclic query $Q,f\left( Q\right)  \geq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)$ .

定理1.2. 对于任意无环查询$Q,f\left( Q\right)  \geq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)$。

We next give some simple observations to understand our significant improvement over [31] and defer the detailed comparison between fn-fhtw(Q)and freew(Q)to Section 3. First,fn-fhtw $\left( Q\right)  \geq$ freew $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  1$ for all queries. In some cases, $\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{freew}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ ,such as free-connex queries, line queries,and star queries. But,for many other cases, $\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  > \operatorname{freew}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

接下来通过简要分析阐明我们对[31]的显著改进，fn-fhtw(Q)与freew(Q)的详细对比将在第3节展开。首先，fn-fhtw$\left( Q\right)  \geq$freew$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  1$对所有查询成立。某些情况下（如自由连接查询、链式查询和星型查询）$\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{freew}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$，但更多情形下$\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  > \operatorname{freew}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

This fn-fhtw-dependent lower bound can be broken beyond semiring algorithms. For example, some works use fast matrix multiplication techniques to speed up conjunctive queries (as a special case of join-aggregate queries defined over Boolean semiring) processing [1,7,24,31] or graph pattern search (as a special case of self-joins) [16, 21, 36]. However, we cannot apply these techniques to arbitrary join-aggregate queries, since a general semiring does not necessarily have an additive inverse (such as the tropical semi-ring), so we won't pursue this dimension further in this paper.

这种基于fn-fhtw的下界在半环算法之外可能被突破。例如部分研究采用快速矩阵乘法技术加速合取查询（作为定义在布尔半环上的连接-聚合查询特例）处理[1,7,24,31]或图模式搜索（作为自连接特例）[16,21,36]。但由于通用半环未必存在加法逆元（如热带半环），这些技术无法推广至任意连接-聚合查询，故本文不再深入探讨该方向。

### 1.3 Our New Upper Bound for Acyclic Queries

### 1.3 无环查询的新上界

In this paper, we propose a new algorithm by exploring a hybrid version of the Yannakakis algorithm for computing acyclic queries, and therefore we can prove:

本文通过改进Yannakakis无环查询算法的混合版本提出新算法，由此可证：

THEOREM 1.3. For any acyclic query $Q,f\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)$ .

定理1.3. 对于任何无环查询$Q,f\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)$。

Combine Theorem 1.2 and Theorem 1.3,we obtain a full understanding of $f\left( \cdot \right)$ for acyclic queries:

结合定理1.2与定理1.3，我们获得了对无环查询$f\left( \cdot \right)$的完整理解：

Corollary 1.4. For any acyclic query $Q,f\left( Q\right)  =$ fn-fhtw(Q).

推论1.4. 对于任意无环查询$Q,f\left( Q\right)  =$，fn-fhtw(Q)。

Comparison with [63]. One question remains for the Yannakakis algorithm: Is the unsatisfactory upper bound $O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ due to a fundamental limitation of the algorithm itself or just because we do not have a tight analysis of its runtime? The runtime bound of the Yannakakis algorithm has been tightened on free-connex queries and star queries [51]. Recently, Hu showed that the Yannakakis algorithm indeed requires $\Theta \left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time for line queries [31],which has first demonstrated the limitation of the Yannakakis algorithm. We provide a tight analysis for all acyclic queries. More specifically,the runtime bound can be tightened to $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ for $a$ - hierarchical queries. In contrast, $O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ is tight for all non-a-hierarchical queries. As shown in Figure 1, a-hierarchical queries include free-connex and star queries, but not line queries. Hence, our new algorithm strictly outperforms the Yannakakis algorithm on all non-a-hierarchical queries.

与文献[63]的对比。Yannakakis算法仍存在一个问题：不理想的上界$O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$究竟源于算法本身的固有局限，还是仅因我们未对其运行时间进行严密分析？该算法的运行时边界已在自由连接查询和星型查询[51]中得到优化。近期，胡的研究证明Yannakakis算法在线性查询[31]中确实需要$\Theta \left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间，这首次揭示了该算法的局限性。我们对所有无环查询给出了严密分析，具体而言：对于$a$层次化查询，运行时边界可优化至$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$；而$O\left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$对所有非层次化查询都是紧确的。如图1所示，层次化查询包含自由连接查询和星型查询，但不含线性查询。因此，我们的新算法在所有非层次化查询上严格优于Yannakakis算法。

Comparison with [39]. Another approach (even applying for cyclic queries) that converts a query into a free-connex one and then runs the Yannakakis algorithm takes $O\left( {{N}^{\# \mathrm{{fn}}\text{-subw(}Q)} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time,where $\# \mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ is the #free-connex sub-modular width of query $\mathcal{Q}$ [39]. As shown in Lemma 3.10,both notions of width collapse on all acyclic queries,i.e.,#fn-subw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ for any acyclic query $Q$ . It is not hard to see that this result is always worse (or at least not better) than our new result due to $N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}} = O\left( {{N}^{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) } + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ . Moreover,this result is worse than our new result by a factor of ${\left( \frac{{N}^{\text{fn-fhtw }\left( Q\right) }}{\text{OUT }}\right) }^{1 - \frac{1}{\text{fn-fhtw }\left( Q\right) }}$ when OUT $< {N}^{\text{fn-fhtw }\left( Q\right) }$ .

与文献[39]的对比。另一种方法（甚至适用于循环查询）将查询转换为自由连接形式后执行Yannakakis算法，耗时$O\left( {{N}^{\# \mathrm{{fn}}\text{-subw(}Q)} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$，其中$\# \mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$表示查询$\mathcal{Q}$的#自由连接子模宽度[39]。如引理3.10所示，这两种宽度概念在所有无环查询上等价，即对任意无环查询$Q$，#fn-subw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。显然由于$N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}} = O\left( {{N}^{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) } + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$的存在，该结果始终逊于（或至多不优于）我们的新成果。此外当OUT$< {N}^{\text{fn-fhtw }\left( Q\right) }$时，该结果比我们的新成果差${\left( \frac{{N}^{\text{fn-fhtw }\left( Q\right) }}{\text{OUT }}\right) }^{1 - \frac{1}{\text{fn-fhtw }\left( Q\right) }}$倍。

Comparison with [25]. In an independent work from ours, Deep et al. identified the project-width for a query $Q$ ,denoted as projw(Q),and showed that $f\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{projw}\left( Q\right)$ [25]. Again,we give some observations to understand our advantages over this upper bound and defer a detailed comparison to Section 3. First,fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ for all queries. For a-hierarchical queries, fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ . For line queries with $k$ relations, $\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 2 < \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = k$ . Also, for many other cases,fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  < \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

与文献[25]的对比。在另一项独立研究中，Deep等人提出了查询$Q$的投影宽度（记为projw(Q)），并证明$f\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{projw}\left( Q\right)$[25]。我们通过几点观察说明本研究的优势，详细对比将在第3节展开。首先，对于所有查询，fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$；对于a-层次查询，fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$；对于含$k$关系的线性查询，$\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 2 < \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = k$；多数其他情况下，fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  < \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

We summarize the runtime of our new algorithm and comparable algorithms in Figure 2.

图2汇总了我们新算法与同类算法的运行时间对比。

### 1.4 Implications to Cyclic Queries

### 1.4 循环查询的启示

The common approach that converts a query into an acyclic one using tree decomposition techniques and then runs the Yannakakis algorithm takes $O\left( {{N}^{\# \text{subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time,where $\# \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ is the #sub-modular width of the query $Q$ [43]. If restricting tree decompositions to be free-connex,this takes $O\left( {{N}^{\# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time. Note that $\# \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ for all queries. These two results are incomparable unless the value of OUT is known. By replacing the Yannakakis algorithm with our new output-optimal algorithm, we can get new output-sensitive algorithms for cyclic queries. However, their optimality is unclear, which we leave as an open question.

传统方法通过树分解技术将查询转换为无环形式后运行Yannakakis算法，耗时$O\left( {{N}^{\# \text{subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$（其中$\# \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$表示查询$Q$的#次模宽度[43]）。若限制树分解为自由连接式，则需$O\left( {{N}^{\# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间。注意$\# \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$适用于所有查询。除非已知OUT值，否则这两个结果不可直接比较。用我们的输出优化算法替代Yannakakis算法后，可得到新的循环查询敏感算法，但其最优性尚不明确，这将成为待解问题。

### 1.5 Organization of This Paper

### 1.5 本文结构

Our paper is organized as follows. In Section 2, we introduce the preliminaries. In Section 3, we define the free-connex fractional hypertree width and investigate its properties for acyclic queries. In Section 4, we review the Yannakakis algorithm and introduce our algorithm for line queries as an introductory example. We present our algorithm for general acyclic queries in Section 5. In Section 6, we show output-sensitive algorithms for cyclic queries. Finally, we review other related works in Section 8 and conclude in Section 9.

本文结构如下：第2章介绍基础知识；第3章定义自由连接分数超树宽度并研究其在无环查询中的特性；第4章回顾Yannakakis算法，并以线性查询为例引入新算法；第5章提出通用无环查询算法；第6章展示循环查询的敏感算法；最后在第8章综述相关研究，第9章总结全文。

## 2 PRELIMINARIES

## 2 基础知识

### 2.1 Fractional Edge Covering and AGM Bound

### 2.1 分数边覆盖与AGM界

For a join query $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,we use ${\mathcal{E}}_{x} = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : x \in  e\}$ to denote the set of relations containing attribute $x$ . An attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ is unique if $\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{x}\right|  = 1$ ,and joint otherwise. For a subset of attributes $S \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ ,we use $q\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack   = \left( {S,\mathcal{E}\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }\right)$ to denote the sub-query induced by $S$ ,where $\mathcal{E}\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack   = \{ e \cap  S : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$ . A fractional edge covering is a function $\rho  : \mathcal{E} \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ such that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e : A \in  e}}\rho \left( e\right)  \geq  1$ for each attribute $A \in  \mathcal{V}$ . The fractional edge covering number of $q$ ,denoted as ${\rho }^{ * }\left( q\right)$ ,is defined as the minimum sum of weight over all possible fractional edge coverings $\rho$ for $q$ ,i.e., ${\rho }^{ * }\left( q\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{\rho }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\rho \left( e\right)$ . For a join query $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ and any parameter $N \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$ ,the AGM bound [8] states that the maximum number of join results produced by any instance of input size $N$ is $\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( q\right) }\right)$ . For a join-aggregate query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ with $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ and any parameter $N \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$ ,the maximum number of join results produced by any instance of input size $N$ is $\Theta \left( {{N}^{\rho  * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack  }\right) }\right)$ .

对于连接查询$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，我们使用${\mathcal{E}}_{x} = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : x \in  e\}$表示包含属性$x$的关系集合。若$\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{x}\right|  = 1$，则称属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$是唯一的，否则为联合属性。对于属性子集$S \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$，用$q\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack   = \left( {S,\mathcal{E}\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }\right)$表示由$S$导出的子查询，其中$\mathcal{E}\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack   = \{ e \cap  S : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$。分数边覆盖是一个函数$\rho  : \mathcal{E} \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，满足对每个属性$A \in  \mathcal{V}$都有$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e : A \in  e}}\rho \left( e\right)  \geq  1$。$q$的分数边覆盖数记作${\rho }^{ * }\left( q\right)$，定义为所有可能分数边覆盖$\rho$的最小权重和，即${\rho }^{ * }\left( q\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{\rho }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\rho \left( e\right)$。对于连接查询$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$和任意参数$N \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$，AGM界[8]指出：输入规模为$N$的任何实例产生的最大连接结果数为$\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( q\right) }\right)$。对于包含$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的连接聚合查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$及任意参数$N \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$，输入规模为$N$的任何实例产生的最大连接结果数为$\Theta \left( {{N}^{\rho  * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack  }\right) }\right)$。

### 2.2 Tree Decompositions

### 2.2 树分解

A tree decomposition (TD) of $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is a pair $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{T}$ is a tree and $\chi  :$ nodes $\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow$ ${2}^{\mathcal{V}}$ is a mapping from the nodes of $\mathcal{T}$ to subsets of $\mathcal{V}$ ,that satisfies the following properties:

$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$的树分解(TD)是一个二元组$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，其中$\mathcal{T}$是树结构，$\chi  :$节点$\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow$${2}^{\mathcal{V}}$是将$\mathcal{T}$的节点映射到$\mathcal{V}$子集的函数，且满足以下性质：

- For each relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,there is a node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ such that $e \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$ .

- 对于每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，存在节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$使得$e \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$。

- For each attribute $A \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,the set $\{ u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  : A \in  \chi \left( u\right) \}$ forms a connected sub-tree of $\mathcal{T}$ .

- 对于每个属性$A \in  \mathcal{V}$，集合$\{ u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  : A \in  \chi \left( u\right) \}$构成$\mathcal{T}$的连通子树。

Each set $\chi \left( u\right)$ is called a bag of the TD. Wlog,we assume $\chi \left( u\right)  \neq  \chi \left( {u}^{\prime }\right)$ for any pair of nodes $e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ . The width of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ noted as width $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is defined as

每个集合$\chi \left( u\right)$称为TD的一个包。不失一般性，我们假设$\chi \left( u\right)  \neq  \chi \left( {u}^{\prime }\right)$对于任意节点对$e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$。$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的宽度记作width$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，其定义为

$$
\operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right) 
$$

i.e.,the maximum fractional edge covering number of the subqueries induced by all bags in $\mathcal{T}$ . A TD is non-redundant if no bag is a subset of another. Below, we can only consider non-redundant TDs. A TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is free-connex if there is a connected subtree $S$ of $\mathcal{T}$ such that $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right) }}\chi \left( u\right)  = \mathbf{y}$ , i.e.,the union of attributes appearing in $S$ is exactly the output attributes. $S$ is called a connex of $\mathcal{T}$ .

即，由$\mathcal{T}$中所有包诱导的子查询的最大分数边覆盖数。若不存在一个包是另一个的子集，则该TD是非冗余的。下文我们仅考虑非冗余TD。当存在$\mathcal{T}$的连通子树$S$使得$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right) }}\chi \left( u\right)  = \mathbf{y}$时（即$S$中出现的属性并集恰好是输出属性），TD$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$称为自由共轭的。$S$称为$\mathcal{T}$的共轭子树。

### 2.3 Classification of Queries

### 2.3 查询分类

Acyclic [12, 35]. A query is acyclic if and only if it has a width-1 TD.

无环查询[12,35]。当且仅当查询存在宽度为1的TD时，该查询是无环的。

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: decompose cleanse 2 - non-output attribute o output attribute -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01962f9c-a7ec-7caa-abca-be50bafc00b3_5.jpg?x=1094&y=1184&w=348&h=818&r=0"/>

Fig. 3. An illustration of the decompose and cleanse process.

图3. 分解清洗过程示意图。

<!-- Media -->

Free-connex [9]. A query is free-connex if and only if it has a width-1 free-connex TD.

自由共轭查询[9]。当且仅当查询存在宽度为1的自由共轭TD时，该查询是自由共轭的。

Hierarchical [54]. A query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is hierarchical if for any pair of attributes $A,B \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,either ${\mathcal{E}}_{A} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{B}$ ,or ${\mathcal{E}}_{B} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{A}$ ,or ${\mathcal{E}}_{A} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{B} = \varnothing$ .

层次化查询[54]。对于任意属性对$A,B \in  \mathcal{V}$，若查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$满足${\mathcal{E}}_{A} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{B}$或${\mathcal{E}}_{B} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{A}$或${\mathcal{E}}_{A} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{B} = \varnothing$，则该查询是层次化的。

$\exists$ -Connected. The existential connectivity of a query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is defined on a graph ${G}_{Q}^{\exists }$ ,where each relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ is a vertex,and an edge exists between $e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ if $e \cap  {e}^{\prime } - \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing .Q$ is $\exists$ -connected if ${G}_{Q}^{\exists }$ is connected,and $\exists$ -disconnected otherwise. If $Q$ is $\exists$ -disconnected,we can decompose it as follows. We find all connected components of ${G}_{Q}^{\exists }$ , in which the set of relations corresponding to the set of vertices in one connected component of ${G}_{Q}^{\exists }$ form a connected subquery of $Q$ . There are four 3-connected subqueries of $Q : 1$ is a complicated acyclic query, 2 is a line query, 3 is a star query, and 4 is a single relation.

$\exists$连通性。查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$的存在连通性定义在图${G}_{Q}^{\exists }$上：每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$作为顶点，当$e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$时顶点间存在边。若${G}_{Q}^{\exists }$连通则称$e \cap  {e}^{\prime } - \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing .Q$是$\exists$连通的，否则为$\exists$非连通的。对于$\exists$非连通的$Q$，可如下分解：找出${G}_{Q}^{\exists }$的所有连通分量，每个分量顶点对应的关系集合构成$Q$的连通子查询。$Q : 1$包含四个3连通子查询：1是复杂无环查询，2是线性查询，3是星型查询，4是单一关系。

Cleansed. A query $Q$ is cleansed if every unique attribute is an output attribute, and there exist no relations whose attributes are fully contained by another one. If $Q$ is not cleansed,we cleanse it by iteratively removing a unique non-output attribute or a relation whose attributes are fully contained by another. The resulting query is the cleansed version of $Q$ .

净化查询。当查询$Q$中每个唯一属性都是输出属性，且不存在属性集被其他关系完全包含的关系时，该查询是净化的。若非净化状态，则通过迭代移除唯一的非输出属性或被其他关系完全包含的关系来实现净化，最终得到$Q$的净化版本。

Separated. A query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is separated if every output attribute is unique, every unique attribute is an output attribute, and for each $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ with $e \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ ,there exists some ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ e\}$ with $e - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ . Among these 4 queries,all of 2,3,4 are separated,but 1 is not. Agg-hierarchical. A query $Q$ is agg-hierarchical if every $\exists$ -connected subquery in ${G}_{{Q}^{\prime }}^{\exists }$ is hierarchical,where ${Q}^{\prime }$ is the cleansed version of $Q$ .

分离性。一个查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$若满足每个输出属性唯一、每个唯一属性都是输出属性，且对于每个$e \in  \mathcal{E}$与$e \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$都存在某个${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ e\}$与$e - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$，则该查询具有分离性。在这4个查询中，2、3、4号查询均满足分离性，但1号不满足。聚合层次性。若查询$Q$中每个$\exists$连通子查询都是层次化的（其中${Q}^{\prime }$是$Q$的净化版本），则该查询具有聚合层次性。

## 3 FREE-CONNEX FRACTIONAL HYPERTREE WIDTH

## 3 自由连接分数超树宽度

In this section, we give a structural definition of free-connex fractional hypertree width for general queries. For acyclic queries, we characterize an equivalent procedural definition, which is fundamental in facilitating comparisons between free-connex fractional hypertree width with other notions of width in Section 3.2, and inspiring our output-optimal algorithm in Section 5.

本节给出通用查询的自由连接分数超树宽度的结构定义。对于无环查询，我们阐述了等价的过程式定义，该定义是3.2节中比较自由连接分数超树宽度与其他宽度概念的基础，并为第5节输出最优算法提供理论支撑。

### 3.1 Definition

### 3.1 定义

The structural definition of free-connex fractional hypertree width is defined on free-connex tree decompositions. Let $\operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ denote the set of all free-connex TDs for a query $\mathcal{Q}$ .

自由连接分数超树宽度的结构定义基于自由连接树分解。用$\operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$表示查询$\mathcal{Q}$的所有自由连接树分解集合。

Definition 3.1 (Free-connex Fractional Hypertree Width (fn-fhtw)). For a query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,its free-connex fractional hypertree width fn-fhtw(Q)is defined as:

定义3.1（自由连接分数超树宽度（fn-fhtw））。对于查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，其自由连接分数超树宽度fn-fhtw(Q)定义为：

$$
\text{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}\operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \tag{1}
$$

i.e., the minimum width of all possible free-connex TDs.

即所有可能自由连接树分解的最小宽度。

It is easy to see fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  1$ for all queries and fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 1$ for free-connex queries. Furthermore, fn-fhtw is preserved in the cleanse process (see Lemma 3.2). When restricting our scope to acyclic queries, we prove two important properties of fn-fhtw in Lemma 3.3 and Lemma 3.4, which serves as a procedural definition of free-connex fractional hypertree width. Their proofs are rather technical and deferred to Appendix A.

易见所有查询都满足fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  1$，自由连接查询满足fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 1$。此外，fn-fhtw在净化过程中保持不变（见引理3.2）。当限定于无环查询时，我们在引理3.3和3.4中证明了fn-fhtw的两个重要性质，这构成了自由连接分数超树宽度的过程式定义。其证明技术性较强，详见附录A。

LEMMA 3.2. For any query $Q$ ,fn-fhtw $\left( Q\right)  =$ fn-fhtw $\left( {Q}^{\prime }\right)$ ,where ${Q}^{\prime }$ is cleansed version of $Q$ .

引理3.2。对任意查询$Q$，fn-fhtw$\left( Q\right)  =$等于fn-fhtw$\left( {Q}^{\prime }\right)$，其中${Q}^{\prime }$是$Q$的净化版本。

LEMMA 3.3. For any acyclic query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,if $\mathcal{Q}$ is $\exists$ -disconnected with $\exists$ -connected subqueries ${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\cdots ,{Q}_{h}$ ,then fn-fhtw $\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}$ fn-fhtw $\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$ .

引理3.3。对任意无环查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，若$\mathcal{Q}$是$\exists$不连通且具有$\exists$连通子查询${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\cdots ,{Q}_{h}$，则fn-fhtw$\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}$等于fn-fhtw$\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$。

LEMMA 3.4. For any acyclic query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,if $\mathcal{Q}$ is $\exists$ -connected,then $\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ for $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,i.e.,the fractional edge covering number of the sub-query induced by output attributes.

引理3.4. 对于任何无环查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，若$\mathcal{Q}$是$\exists$-连通的，则$\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$对于$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$成立，即由输出属性导出的子查询的分数边覆盖数。

COROLLARY 3.5. For any acyclic query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,its free-connex fractional hypertree width fn-fhtw(Q)is recursively defined as:

推论3.5. 对于任何无环查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，其自由连接分数超树宽度fn-fhtw(Q)递归定义为：

- If $\mathcal{Q}$ is $\exists$ -disconnected with $\exists$ -connected subqueries ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{Q}}_{h}$ ,fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$ .

- 若$\mathcal{Q}$相对于$\exists$不连通且具有$\exists$-连通子查询${\mathcal{Q}}_{1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{Q}}_{h}$，则fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}$等于fn-fhtw$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$。

- If $\mathcal{Q}$ is $\exists$ -connected, $\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ for $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,i.e.,the fractional edge covering number of the sub-query induced by output attributes.

- 若$\mathcal{Q}$是$\exists$-连通的，则$\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$对于$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$成立，即由输出属性导出的子查询的分数边覆盖数。

### 3.2 Comparison with Other Notions of Width

### 3.2 与其他宽度概念的比较

Free-width and Project-width. We first review the definitions for free-width and project-width:

自由宽度与投影宽度. 我们首先回顾自由宽度和投影宽度的定义：

Definition 3.6 (Free-width [31] and Project-width [25]). For any acyclic query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,its free-width freew(Q)and project-width projw(Q)are defined as follows:

定义3.6（自由宽度[31]与投影宽度[25]). 对于任何无环查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，其自由宽度freew(Q)和投影宽度projw(Q)定义如下：

- If $\mathcal{Q}$ is $\exists$ -disconnected with $\exists$ -connected subqueries ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{Q}}_{h}$ ,free $\mathrm{w}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{freew}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$ , and $\operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{projw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$ .

- 若$\mathcal{Q}$相对于$\exists$不连通且具有$\exists$-连通子查询${\mathcal{Q}}_{1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{Q}}_{h}$，则自由宽度$\mathrm{w}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{freew}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$，且$\operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{projw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$。

- If $\mathcal{Q}$ is $\exists$ -connected but not cleansed,freew $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{freew}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ and $\operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{projw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ ,where ${Q}^{\prime }$ is the cleansed version of $Q$ .

- 若$\mathcal{Q}$是$\exists$-连通但未净化，则freew$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{freew}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$且$\operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{projw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$，其中${Q}^{\prime }$是$Q$的净化版本。

- If $\mathcal{Q}$ is $\exists$ -connected and cleansed,free $w\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \left| {\{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing \} }\right|$ ,where ${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ is the set of unique (output) attributes in $Q$ ; and projw $\left( Q\right)  = \left| \mathcal{E}\right|$ .

- 若$\mathcal{Q}$是$\exists$连通且经过净化处理的，自由$w\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \left| {\{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing \} }\right|$，其中${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$表示$Q$中唯一（输出）属性的集合；且投影$\left( Q\right)  = \left| \mathcal{E}\right|$。

LEMMA 3.7. For any acyclic query $Q$ ,free $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{projw}\left( Q\right)$ .

引理3.7. 对于任何无环查询$Q$，自由$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{projw}\left( Q\right)$。

These three notions of width share the exact definition if $Q$ is $\exists$ -disconnected or not cleansed. The only difference comes when $Q$ is $\exists$ -connected and cleansed. In this case,it is not hard to see that freew $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq$ fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ . First,every relation containing a unique attribute (which must be an output attribute because $Q$ is cleansed) should be assigned a weight of 1 in any fractional edge covering of $q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack$ . This is why $\operatorname{freew}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)$ . Moreover,assigning all relations with weight 1 forms a trivial fractional edge covering for $q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack$ ,hence fn-fhtw $\left( Q\right)  \leq  \left| \mathcal{E}\right|  = \operatorname{projw}\left( Q\right)$ . In Example 3.8,we show a query $Q$ with free $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  <$ fn-fhtw $\left( Q\right)  < \operatorname{projw}\left( Q\right)$ .

当$Q$是$\exists$非连通或未净化时，这三种宽度概念的定义完全一致。唯一差异出现在$Q$为$\exists$连通且净化的情况。此时不难看出自由宽度$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq$≤分数无超树宽度$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{projw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。首先，在$q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack$的任何分数边覆盖中，包含唯一属性（因查询已净化，该属性必为输出属性）的关系都应被赋予权重1，这解释了$\operatorname{freew}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)$。此外，为所有关系分配权重1即构成$q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack$的平凡分数边覆盖，故分数无超树宽度$\left( Q\right)  \leq  \left| \mathcal{E}\right|  = \operatorname{projw}\left( Q\right)$。在示例3.8中，我们展示了一个自由$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  <$严格小于分数无超树宽度$\left( Q\right)  < \operatorname{projw}\left( Q\right)$的查询$Q$。

Example 3.8. Consider an $\exists$ -connected and cleansed query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ in Figure 4,where $\mathcal{V} =$ $\left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  ,\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right\}  ,{e}_{2} = \left\{  {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,{e}_{3} = \left\{  {{A}_{3},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,{e}_{5} = \left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  }\right\}  ,$ $\left. {{e}_{6} = \left\{  {{B}_{3},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  }\right\}$ ,and $\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{C}_{2}}\right\}$ . Note that ${A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3}$ are unique output attributes. All of ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ contain some unique output attribute(s),so freew $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 3$ . It has the fractional edge covering number as 4,so fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 4$ . It has 5 relations,so projw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 5$ .

示例3.8. 考虑图4中一个$\exists$连通的净化查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，其中$\mathcal{V} =$$\left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  ,\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right\}  ,{e}_{2} = \left\{  {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,{e}_{3} = \left\{  {{A}_{3},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,{e}_{5} = \left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  }\right\}  ,$$\left. {{e}_{6} = \left\{  {{B}_{3},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  }\right\}$，且$\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{C}_{2}}\right\}$。注意${A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3}$是唯一输出属性。所有${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$都包含某些唯一输出属性，因此自由宽度$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 3$。其分数边覆盖数为4，故分数超树宽$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 4$。该查询含5个关系，故投影宽度$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = 5$。

#Free-connex submodular width. The #free-connex submodular width (#fn-subw) is also defined based on tree decompositions but in a rather complicated formula. First,we can show #fn-subw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ for all queries. From [39], we can further draw a clear ordering by additionally involving submodular width (subw), free-connex submodular width (fn-subw), and #submodular width (#subw) as follows:

#自由连接子模宽度。#自由连接子模宽度(#fn-subw)同样基于树分解定义，但公式较为复杂。首先可证#fn-subw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$对所有查询成立。根据[39]，通过引入子模宽度(subw)、自由连接子模宽度(fn-subw)和#子模宽度(#subw)，可进一步建立明确排序关系：

LEMMA 3.9. For any query $Q$ ,subw $\left( Q\right)  \leq  \{ \# \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)$ ,fn-subw $\left( Q\right) \}  \leq$ $\# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

引理3.9. 对任意查询$Q$，子模宽度$\left( Q\right)  \leq  \{ \# \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)$，自由连接子模宽度$\left( Q\right) \}  \leq$$\# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

Surprisingly, some widths collapse when we restrict our scope to acyclic queries!

令人惊讶的是，当我们将研究范围限定在无环查询时，某些宽度指标会坍缩！

LEMMA 3.10. For any acyclic query $Q$ ,fn-subw $\left( Q\right)  = \#$ fn-subw $\left( Q\right)  =$ fn-fhtw(Q).

引理3.10. 对任意无环查询$Q$，自由连接子模宽度$\left( Q\right)  = \#$等于自由连接子模宽度$\left( Q\right)  =$即分数超树宽Q。

All missing details are deferred to Appendix A. This result also implies the fundamental difference between existing algorithms and our new algorithm. On acyclic queries, the algorithm in [39] always picks one free-connex TD and performs computation according to it; in contrast, our algorithm partitions the input instance into multiple sub-instances, picks a set of free-connex TDs, and applies them to different sub-instances. The power of this hybrid strategy will become much clearer in our next section.

所有缺失细节详见附录A。该结果也揭示了现有算法与我们新算法的本质差异：对于无环查询，文献[39]算法总是选取一个自由连接树分解并按之计算；而我们的算法将输入实例划分为多个子实例，选取一组自由连接树分解并应用于不同子实例。这种混合策略的优势将在下节得到更清晰的展现。

## 4 WARM UP: YANNAKAKIS REVISITED AND LINE QUERY

## 4 预热：重访亚纳卡基斯算法与线型查询

In this section, we first review the Yannakakis algorithm [38, 63] over acyclic join-aggregate queries. To illustrate some of the high-level ideas behind our general algorithm, we examine line queries (a.k.a. chain matrix multiplication), which represent the simplest scenario where the Yannakakis algorithm is not optimal:

本节首先回顾无环连接聚合查询上的亚纳卡基斯算法[38,63]。为阐明通用算法背后的高层思想，我们研究线型查询（又称链式矩阵乘法）——这是亚纳卡基斯算法非最优的最简单场景：

$$
{\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{line }} = {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}{R}_{1}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  \cdots  \bowtie  {R}_{k}\left( {{A}_{k},{A}_{k + 1}}\right) 
$$

Notably, chain matrix multiplication has independently garnered significant attention in previous research $\left\lbrack  {{11},{26},{42},{48}}\right\rbrack$ .

值得注意的是，链式矩阵乘法在先前研究中已独立获得显著关注$\left\lbrack  {{11},{26},{42},{48}}\right\rbrack$。

### 4.1 Yannakakis Algorithm Revisited

### 4.1 重温Yannakakis算法

<!-- Media -->

can preprocess ${}^{1}$ these relations within $O\left( N\right)$ time. Let(T,X)be a width-1 (non-redundant) TD for $Q$ . There is a one-to-one correspondence between relations in $\mathcal{E}$ and nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ . For simplicity,we also use $e$ to denote the node $u$ in $\mathcal{T}$ that corresponds to relation $e$ ,i.e., $\chi \left( u\right)  = e$ . The algorithm consists of two phases:

可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内预处理这些关系${}^{1}$。设(T,X)是$Q$的宽度1（非冗余）TD，$\mathcal{E}$中的关系与$\mathcal{T}$中的节点存在一一对应关系。为简化表述，我们亦用$e$表示$\mathcal{T}$中对应关系$e$的节点$u$，即$\chi \left( u\right)  = e$。该算法包含两个阶段：

Semi-Joins. A tuple is dangling if it does not participate in any full join result. The first phase removes all dangling tuples via a bottom-up and top-down pass of semi-joins along $\mathcal{T}$ .

半连接。若元组未参与任何完全连接结果，则称其为悬挂元组。第一阶段通过沿$\mathcal{T}$自底向上和自顶向下的半连接传递来移除所有悬挂元组。

Pairwise Join-Aggregation. The second phase performs joins and aggregations in a bottom-up way along $\mathcal{T}$ . Specifically,it takes two nodes ${R}_{e}$ and ${R}_{{p}_{u}}$ such that $u$ is a leaf and ${p}_{u}$ is the parent of $u$ ,aggregate over non-output attributes that do not appear in $\chi \left( {p}_{u}\right)$ by replacing ${R}_{e}$ with ${ \oplus  }_{\chi \left( u\right)  - \chi \left( {p}_{u}\right)  - \mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}$ ,and replaces ${R}_{{p}_{u}}$ with ${R}_{e} \bowtie  {R}_{{p}_{u}}$ . Then ${R}_{e}$ is removed,and the step repeats until only one node remains,i.e.,the root node $r$ . Hence,it aggregates over all remaining non-output attributes in $r$ ,and outputs ${ \oplus  }_{r - \mathrm{y}}{R}_{r}$ as the final result. See an example in Figure 5.

成对连接-聚合。第二阶段沿$\mathcal{T}$自底向上执行连接与聚合操作。具体而言，算法选取两个节点${R}_{e}$和${R}_{{p}_{u}}$，其中$u$为叶节点且${p}_{u}$是$u$的父节点，通过将${R}_{e}$替换为${ \oplus  }_{\chi \left( u\right)  - \chi \left( {p}_{u}\right)  - \mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}$来聚合未出现在$\chi \left( {p}_{u}\right)$中的非输出属性，并将${R}_{{p}_{u}}$替换为${R}_{e} \bowtie  {R}_{{p}_{u}}$。随后移除${R}_{e}$，循环此步骤直至仅剩根节点$r$。最终算法在$r$上聚合所有剩余非输出属性，并输出${ \oplus  }_{r - \mathrm{y}}{R}_{r}$作为结果。示例见图5。

The runtime is proportional to the largest number of intermediate results materialized (after dangling tuples are removed). But, this number can vary significantly depending on the sepecific query plans chosen. Each query plan corresponds to one width-1 TD together with a particular sequence of pairwise joins and aggregations. So, the runtime of the Yannakakis algorithm refers to the runtime of the fastest available query plan.

运行时间与物化的最大中间结果数量（剔除悬挂元组后）成正比。但该数值会因所选查询计划的不同而显著波动。每个查询计划对应一个宽度为1的树分解（TD）及特定的成对连接-聚合序列。因此Yannakakis算法的运行时间是指可用最快查询计划的执行时长。

${}^{1}$ For any pair of $e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ with $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ,we simply replace ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$ by ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \bowtie  {R}_{e}$ and remove ${R}_{e}$ . Note that the annotations of tuples in ${R}_{e}$ are "passed" to tuples in ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$ via the join. As $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ,the join can be done within $O\left( N\right)$ time.

${}^{1}$对于任意$e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$与$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$的组合，我们直接将${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$替换为${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \bowtie  {R}_{e}$并移除${R}_{e}$。注意${R}_{e}$中元组的注解会通过连接操作"传递"到${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$的元组。当$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$时，连接操作可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内完成。

foreach $i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ do

遍历$i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$执行

		${Q}_{i} \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{i}^{\text{heavy }} \bowtie  \left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j = i + 1}^{k}{R}_{j}}\right) ;$

${\mathcal{Q}}_{ * } \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{k}$

	eturn ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1} \oplus  {\mathcal{Q}}_{2} \oplus  \cdots  \oplus  {\mathcal{Q}}_{k - 1} \oplus  {\mathcal{Q}}_{ * }$ ;

	返回${\mathcal{Q}}_{1} \oplus  {\mathcal{Q}}_{2} \oplus  \cdots  \oplus  {\mathcal{Q}}_{k - 1} \oplus  {\mathcal{Q}}_{ * }$；

---

<!-- Media -->

### 4.2 Line Queries

### 4.2 线性查询

As pointed out by [31], there exists some instance for line queries such that every query plan of the Yannakakis algorithm must materialize $\Omega \left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ intermediate results. From their hard instance, we are inspired to leverage the power of multiple query plans to overcome the fundamental limitation of the Yannakakis algorithm. Given the wide variety of TDsfor a line query, one natural question arises: which query plans should we select? For line queries, we only consider two query plans that correspond to two width-1 TDs: (a) one is rooted at $\left( {{A}_{1}{A}_{2}}\right)$ ; (b) one is rooted at $\left( {{A}_{k}{A}_{k + 1}}\right)$ . See Figure 5 and 6. Behind our hybrid strategy, the idea is to partition the input instance into a set of sub-instances and then choose one of two plans for each sub-instance. To determine which plan to use, we need a deeper analysis of data statistics. For example,if the effective domain of ${A}_{k + 1}$ is small,we choose the one rooted at $\left( {{A}_{1}{A}_{2}}\right)$ ; and if that of ${A}_{1}$ is small,we choose the one rooted at $\left( {{A}_{k}{A}_{k + 1}}\right)$ .

如文献[31]指出，对于线性查询存在某些实例，使得Yannakakis算法的每个查询计划都必须物化$\Omega \left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$个中间结果。受其困难实例启发，我们尝试利用多查询计划的协同效应来突破Yannakakis算法的固有局限。鉴于线性查询存在多种树分解(TDs)，一个核心问题随之产生：应如何选择查询计划？针对线性查询，我们仅考虑对应两种宽度为1的树分解的查询计划：(a)以$\left( {{A}_{1}{A}_{2}}\right)$为根节点的方案；(b)以$\left( {{A}_{k}{A}_{k + 1}}\right)$为根节点的方案（见图5和图6）。混合策略的核心思想是将输入实例划分为若干子实例，再为每个子实例选择两种计划之一。具体选择需基于数据统计的深入分析，例如：当${A}_{k + 1}$的有效域较小时选用$\left( {{A}_{1}{A}_{2}}\right)$根节点方案；当${A}_{1}$的有效域较小时则选用$\left( {{A}_{k}{A}_{k + 1}}\right)$根节点方案。

Algorithm. As described in Algorithm 2, our new approach consists of two stages. In Stage I, we partition the input instance. In Stage II, we choose different query plans for each sub-instance, apply the Yannakakis algorithm, and aggregate all subqueries. See an example in Figure 7. We now assume a parameter OUT is known such that OUT $\leq$ OUT $\leq  c \cdot$ OUT for some constant $c$ . This assumption can be removed without increasing the complexity asymptotically [30].

算法. 如算法2所述，我们的新方法包含两个阶段：第一阶段划分输入实例，第二阶段为每个子实例选择不同查询计划、应用Yannakakis算法并聚合所有子查询（示例见图7）。现假设已知参数OUT满足OUT$\leq$OUT$\leq  c \cdot$OUT（其中$c$为常数），该假设可在不增加渐进复杂度的情况下消除[30]。

Stage I: Partition. For each value $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{2}\right)$ ,we define its degree as the number of tuples from ${R}_{1}$ displaying $a$ in ${A}_{2}$ ,i.e., $\bigtriangleup \left( a\right)  = \left| {{\pi }_{{A}_{1}}{\sigma }_{{A}_{2} = a}{R}_{1}}\right|$ . A value $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{2}\right)$ is heavy if $\bigtriangleup \left( a\right)  > \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$ ,and light otherwise. Let ${A}_{2}^{\text{heavy }},{A}_{2}^{\text{light }}$ be the set of heavy and light values in ${A}_{2}$ . Let ${R}_{1}^{\text{heavy }} = {R}_{1} \ltimes  {A}_{2}^{\text{heavy }}$ and ${R}_{1}^{\text{light }} = {R}_{1} \ltimes  {A}_{2}^{\text{light }}$ be the set of heavy and light tuples in ${R}_{1}$ respectively. Below,we partition relations by ordering ${R}_{2},{R}_{3},\cdots ,{R}_{k}$ . Suppose we are done with ${R}_{1},{R}_{2},\cdots ,{R}_{i - 1}$ . We next partition values in $\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i + 1}\right)$ that can be joined with any value in $\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)$ via ${R}_{1}^{\text{light }},{R}_{2}^{\text{light }},\cdots ,{R}_{i - 1}^{\text{light }}$ . Let $\Delta \left( a\right)  = \left| {{\pi }_{{A}_{1}}\left\{  {\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  \left( {{\sigma }_{{A}_{i + 1} = a}{R}_{i}}\right) }\right\}  }\right|$ be the degree of each value $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i + 1}\right)$ . A

第一阶段：分区。对于每个值$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{2}\right)$，我们将其度数定义为在${A}_{2}$中显示$a$的${R}_{1}$元组数量，即$\bigtriangleup \left( a\right)  = \left| {{\pi }_{{A}_{1}}{\sigma }_{{A}_{2} = a}{R}_{1}}\right|$。若$\bigtriangleup \left( a\right)  > \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$，则该值$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{2}\right)$为重度值，否则为轻度值。设${A}_{2}^{\text{heavy }},{A}_{2}^{\text{light }}$为${A}_{2}$中重度值与轻度值的集合，${R}_{1}^{\text{heavy }} = {R}_{1} \ltimes  {A}_{2}^{\text{heavy }}$和${R}_{1}^{\text{light }} = {R}_{1} \ltimes  {A}_{2}^{\text{light }}$分别表示${R}_{1}$中重度元组和轻度元组的集合。以下通过排序${R}_{2},{R}_{3},\cdots ,{R}_{k}$对关系进行分区。假设已完成${R}_{1},{R}_{2},\cdots ,{R}_{i - 1}$的处理，接下来对<b11></b1>中可通过${R}_{1}^{\text{light }},{R}_{2}^{\text{light }},\cdots ,{R}_{i - 1}^{\text{light }}$与$\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)$内任意值连接的值进行分区。设$\Delta \left( a\right)  = \left| {{\pi }_{{A}_{1}}\left\{  {\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  \left( {{\sigma }_{{A}_{i + 1} = a}{R}_{i}}\right) }\right\}  }\right|$为各值$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i + 1}\right)$的度数。

value $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i + 1}\right)$ is heavy if $\Delta \left( a\right)  > \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$ ,and light otherwise. Let ${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }},{A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$ be the set of heavy,light values in ${A}_{i}$ . Then, ${R}_{i}^{\text{heavy }} = {R}_{i} \ltimes  {A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }}$ and ${R}_{i}^{\text{light }} = {R}_{i} \ltimes  {A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$ . Note that some values in ${A}_{i + 1}$ may be undefined,as well as some tuples in ${R}_{i}$ .

若$\Delta \left( a\right)  > \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$，则值$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i + 1}\right)$为重度值，否则为轻度值。设${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }},{A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$为${A}_{i}$中重度值与轻度值的集合，则${R}_{i}^{\text{heavy }} = {R}_{i} \ltimes  {A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }}$且${R}_{i}^{\text{light }} = {R}_{i} \ltimes  {A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$。需注意${A}_{i + 1}$中某些值可能未定义，${R}_{i}$中部分元组亦然。

Instead of computing $\Delta \left( \cdot \right)$ directly,we introduce the following intermediate relations:

我们不直接计算$\Delta \left( \cdot \right)$，而是引入以下中间关系：

$$
{T}_{i}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{i + 1}}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{i}}\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{i}\text{,and}{S}_{i}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{i + 1}}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{i}}\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right) 
$$

and recursively compute them as follows (with ${T}_{1} = {R}_{1}$ in line 2):

并通过以下方式递归计算（第2行含${T}_{1} = {R}_{1}$）：

$$
{T}_{i}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{i + 1}}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{i}}{S}_{i - 1}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{i}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{i}\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{i + 1}}\right) ;\;\text{ (line 3) }
$$

$$
{S}_{i}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{i + 1}}\right)  = {T}_{i}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{i + 1}}\right)  \ltimes  {A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }};\;\text{(line 7)}
$$

Once we have computed ${T}_{i}$ ,we can identify the heavy and light values in ${A}_{i + 1}$ ,i.e., ${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }}$ and ${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$ (lines 4-5). We can use ${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }},{A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$ to partition ${R}_{i}$ into ${R}_{i}^{\text{heavy }},{R}_{i}^{\text{light }}$ (line 6). Then, ${S}_{i}$ can be computed based on ${T}_{i}$ and ${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$ . Furthermore, ${T}_{i + 1}$ can be computed based on ${S}_{i}$ and ${R}_{i + 1}$ .

一旦计算出${T}_{i}$，我们就能识别${A}_{i + 1}$中的重值与轻值，即${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }}$和${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$（第4-5行）。利用${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }},{A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$可将${R}_{i}$划分为${R}_{i}^{\text{heavy }},{R}_{i}^{\text{light }}$（第6行）。接着，${S}_{i}$可基于${T}_{i}$和${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{light }}$计算得出。此外，${T}_{i + 1}$能根据${S}_{i}$和${R}_{i + 1}$进行推算。

We partition ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{line }}$ into $k$ sub-instances: ${\mathcal{Q}}_{i} = {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{i}^{\text{heavy }} \bowtie  \left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j = i + 1}^{k}{R}_{j}}\right)$ for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ ,and ${\mathcal{Q}}_{ * } = {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{k}$ .

我们将${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{line }}$划分为$k$个子实例：为每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$分配${\mathcal{Q}}_{i} = {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{i}^{\text{heavy }} \bowtie  \left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j = i + 1}^{k}{R}_{j}}\right)$，以及${\mathcal{Q}}_{ * } = {\bigoplus }_{{A}_{2},{A}_{3},\cdots ,{A}_{k}}\left( {{ \bowtie  }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }{R}_{j}^{\text{light }}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{k}$。

Stage II: Hybrid Yannakakis. We invoke the Yannakakis algorithm to compute ${Q}_{i}$ for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ using the TD (a),and ${Q}_{ * }$ using the TD (b). In the latter case,it is equivalent to computing ${\bigoplus }_{{A}_{k}}{S}_{k - 1}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{k}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{k}\left( {{A}_{k},{A}_{k + 1}}\right)$ . Finally,we aggregate the results of all subqueries.

阶段二：混合Yannakakis算法。调用Yannakakis算法，分别使用树分解(a)计算每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$对应的${Q}_{i}$，使用树分解(b)计算${Q}_{ * }$。后者等效于计算${\bigoplus }_{{A}_{k}}{S}_{k - 1}\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{k}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{k}\left( {{A}_{k},{A}_{k + 1}}\right)$。最终汇总所有子查询结果。

Analysis. In Stage I,consider any $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   - \{ 1\}$ . As there are $N$ tuples in ${R}_{i}$ ,and each of them can be joined with at most $\sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$ tuples in ${S}_{i - 1},{T}_{i}$ can be computed in $O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$ time. Also, $\left| {T}_{i}\right|  = O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$ . The cost of computing ${S}_{i}$ is $O\left( \left| {T}_{i}\right| \right)  = O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$ . In Stage II,for ${Q}_{i}$ ,each value in the effective domain of ${A}_{k + 1}$ can be joined with at least $\sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$ values in ${A}_{1}$ , implied by ${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }}$ . As there are OUT results in total,the effective domain size of ${A}_{k + 1}$ is $O\left( \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ .

分析。阶段一中，考虑任意$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   - \{ 1\}$。由于${R}_{i}$中存在$N$个元组，每个最多可与${S}_{i - 1},{T}_{i}$中的$\sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$个元组连接，故可在$O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$时间内完成计算。此外，$\left| {T}_{i}\right|  = O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$。计算${S}_{i}$的代价为$O\left( \left| {T}_{i}\right| \right)  = O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$。阶段二中，对于${Q}_{i}$，${A}_{k + 1}$有效域中的每个值至少能与${A}_{1}$中的$\sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$个值连接，此性质由${A}_{i + 1}^{\text{heavy }}$隐含。由于总共有OUT个结果，${A}_{k + 1}$的有效域大小为$O\left( \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$。

8 return ${ \bowtie  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }{\mathcal{S}}_{i}$ by Yannakakis algorithm [63]; - Algorithm 1;

8 通过Yannakakis算法[63]返回${ \bowtie  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }{\mathcal{S}}_{i}$； - 算法1；

while(V,E,y)is not cleansed do

当(V,E,y)未被清理时循环执行

	if $\exists B \in  \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}$ s.t. $\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{B}\right|  = 1$ ,say ${\mathcal{E}}_{B} = \{ e\}$ then

	若存在$\exists B \in  \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}$使得$\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{B}\right|  = 1$，例如${\mathcal{E}}_{B} = \{ e\}$则

Hence,the number of intermediate join results materialized for each node is at most $O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$ . For ${Q}_{ * }$ ,the total number of intermediate join results is $O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$ ,as there are $N$ tuples in ${R}_{k}$ and each of them can be joined with at most $\sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$ tuples in ${S}_{k - 1}$ . Hence,this step takes $O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$ time. Finally,as each sub-query produces at most OUT results,and there are $O\left( 1\right)$ subqueries,the aggregation step takes $O\left( \mathrm{{OUT}}\right)$ time. Putting everything together,we obtain:

因此，每个节点物化的中间连接结果数量最多为$O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$。对于${Q}_{ * }$，中间连接结果总数为$O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$，因为${R}_{k}$中存在$N$个元组，每个元组最多可与${S}_{k - 1}$中的$\sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$个元组连接。故此步骤耗时$O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$。最终，由于每个子查询最多产生OUT个结果，且存在$O\left( 1\right)$个子查询，聚合步骤耗时$O\left( \mathrm{{OUT}}\right)$。综合可得：

THEOREM 4.1. For ${Q}_{\text{line }}$ and an arbitrary instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT,the query result $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed in $O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$ time.

定理4.1. 对于${Q}_{\text{line }}$及输入规模为$N$、输出规模为OUT的任意实例$\mathcal{R}$，查询结果$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$时间内计算得出。

## 5 ACYCLIC QUERIES

## 5 无环查询

After obtaining some high-level ideas behind our hybrid strategy, we are now ready to move to general acyclic queries. But, there are several challenging questions in front of us:

在理解了混合策略背后的高层理念后，我们现在可以转向一般无环查询。但面临几个关键挑战：

- How to relate free-connex fractional hypertree width to the structure of an acyclic query?

- 如何将自由连接分数超树宽度与无环查询的结构关联？

- What optimal condition is required to run the Yannakakis algorithm within the targeted time?

- 在目标时间内运行Yannakakis算法需要满足什么最优条件？

- How to partition the input instance into a set of sub-instances satisfying the optimal condition? We will answer these questions step by step.

- 如何将输入实例分割成满足最优条件的子实例集？我们将逐步解答这些问题。

### 5.1 Outline of Our Algorithm

### 5.1 算法概览

The procedural definition of free-connex fractional hypertree width in Corollary 3.5 essentially outlines our algorithm. As described in Algorithm 3,given an acyclic query $Q$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT,we first remove dangling tuples (line 1),decompose $Q$ into a set of connected subqueries (line 2-3), compute the results of each 3-connected subquery separately (line 4-6) and combine their results via join (line 7).

推论3.5中自由连接分数超树宽度的过程性定义本质上勾勒了我们的算法。如算法3所述，给定无环查询$Q$和输入规模$N$、输出规模OUT的实例$\mathcal{R}$，我们首先移除悬空元组（第1行），将$Q$分解为连通子查询集（第2-3行），分别计算每个3连通子查询结果（第4-6行），最后通过连接合并结果（第7行）。

From now on,we focus exclusively on $\exists$ -connected queries. Given an arbitrary subquery $Q$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ ,if $Q$ is not cleansed,we apply Algorithm 4 to obtain a cleanse version ${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }$ and an updated instance ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ of input size $O\left( N\right)$ such that $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ . Algorithm 4 iteratively applies the following two steps: (lines 2-3) removes a unique non-output attribute $B \in  \mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y}$ (suppose ${\mathcal{E}}_{B} = \{ e\}$ ) and aggregate ${R}_{e}$ over $B$ ; or (lines 4-5) removes relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ if there exists another relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ with $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ and update ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$ by ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \bowtie  {R}_{e}$ ,i.e.,update the annotation of each tuple $t \in  {R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$ by $w\left( t\right)  \otimes  w\left( {{\pi }_{e}t}\right)$ . This step only takes $O\left( N\right)$ time.

接下来我们专注$\exists$连通查询。给定任意子查询$Q$和输入规模$N$的实例$\mathcal{R}$，若$Q$未净化，则应用算法4获得净化版本${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }$及更新后的输入规模$O\left( N\right)$的实例${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$，满足$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$。算法4迭代执行以下步骤：（第2-3行）移除唯一非输出属性$B \in  \mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y}$（假设${\mathcal{E}}_{B} = \{ e\}$）并对$B$聚合${R}_{e}$；或（第4-5行）当存在满足$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$的关系${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$时移除关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，并通过${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \bowtie  {R}_{e}$更新${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$，即用$w\left( t\right)  \otimes  w\left( {{\pi }_{e}t}\right)$更新每个元组$t \in  {R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$的注解。此步骤仅耗时$O\left( N\right)$。
