Algorithm 5: Separatte $\left( {\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right) ,\mathcal{R}}\right)$

算法5：分离$\left( {\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right) ,\mathcal{R}}\right)$

---

1 ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \leftarrow$ a set of fn-fhtw relations with $\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}e$ ;

1 ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \leftarrow$ 一组满足$\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}e$的fn-fhtw关系；

	$\kappa  \leftarrow$ assign each output attribute $A \in  \mathbf{y}$ to an arbitrary relation $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ with $A \in  e$ ;

	$\kappa  \leftarrow$ 将每个输出属性$A \in  \mathbf{y}$分配给任意满足$A \in  e$的关系$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$；

	foreach $A \in  \mathbf{y}$ with $\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{A}\right|  > 1$ do

	对每个满足$\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{A}\right|  > 1$的$A \in  \mathbf{y}$执行

			${x}_{A} \leftarrow$ an attribute not appearing $\mathcal{V}$ ;

			${x}_{A} \leftarrow$ 未出现的属性$\mathcal{V}$；

			$\mathcal{V} \leftarrow  \mathcal{V} \cup  \left\{  {x}_{A}\right\}  ,\kappa \left( A\right)  \leftarrow  \kappa \left( A\right)  \cup  \left\{  {x}_{A}\right\}  ,\mathbf{y} \leftarrow  \mathbf{y} \cup  \left\{  {x}_{A}\right\}   - \{ A\} ;$

			foreach tuple $t \in  {R}_{\kappa \left( A\right) }$ do extend $t$ with value ${\pi }_{A}t$ in attribute ${x}_{A}$ ;

			对每个元组$t \in  {R}_{\kappa \left( A\right) }$，用值${\pi }_{A}t$扩展属性${x}_{A}$中的$t$；

	foreach $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ s.t. $\nexists {e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ with $e - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ do

	对每个满足$e - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$的$\nexists {e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$的$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$执行

			${x}_{e} \leftarrow$ an attribute not appearing in $\mathcal{V}$ ;

			${x}_{e} \leftarrow$ 未出现在$\mathcal{V}$中的属性；

			${e}^{\prime \prime } \leftarrow  \left\{  {x}_{e}\right\}   \cup  \left( {e \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right)$ ;

			$\mathcal{V} \leftarrow  \mathcal{V} \cup  \left\{  {x}_{e}\right\}  ,\mathbf{y} \leftarrow  \mathbf{y} \cup  \left\{  {x}_{e}\right\}   - \left( {e \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right) ,\mathcal{E} \leftarrow  \mathcal{E} \cup  \left\{  {e}^{\prime \prime }\right\}  ;$

			${R}_{{e}^{\prime \prime }} \leftarrow  \varnothing$ ;

			foreach $t \in  {\pi }_{e \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime }}{R}_{e}$ do

			foreach $t \in  {\pi }_{e \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime }}{R}_{e}$ 执行

					${t}^{\prime } \leftarrow$ a tuple over attributes ${e}^{\prime \prime }$ with ${\pi }_{e \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime }}{t}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{{x}_{e}}{t}^{\prime } = t$ and $w\left( {t}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$ ;

					${t}^{\prime } \leftarrow$ 关于属性${e}^{\prime \prime }$的元组，其中包含${\pi }_{e \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime }}{t}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{{x}_{e}}{t}^{\prime } = t$和$w\left( {t}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$；

					${R}_{{e}^{\prime \prime }} \leftarrow  {R}_{{e}^{\prime \prime }} \cup  \left\{  {t}^{\prime }\right\}$

			$\mathcal{R} \leftarrow  \mathcal{R} \cup  \left\{  {R}_{{e}^{\prime \prime }}\right\}$

	return Updated $Q$ and $\mathcal{R}$ ;

	返回更新后的$Q$和$\mathcal{R}$；

<!-- Media -->

THEOREM 5.1. Any $\exists$ -connected query $Q$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ ,can be transformed into a cleansed query ${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }$ and an instance ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ of input size $O\left( N\right)$ within $O\left( N\right)$ time,such that $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ .

定理5.1. 任何$\exists$连通的查询$Q$及输入规模为$N$的实例$\mathcal{R}$，均可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内转换为净化查询${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }$及输入规模为$O\left( N\right)$的实例${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$，使得$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$。

Next,we narrow our scope to $\exists$ -connected and cleansed queries. Given an arbitrary query $Q$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ ,if $\mathcal{Q}$ is not separated,we apply the Algorithm 5 to obtain a separated version ${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }$ and an updated instance ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ of input size $O\left( N\right)$ such that $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ and fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ . Algorithm 5 begins by handling joint output attributes (lines 1-6). As shown in Lemma 5.2, it is always feasible to find a subset ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ of fn-fhtw relations that contain all output attributes (line 1),and to assign each output attribute $A \in  \mathbf{y}$ to an arbitrary relation $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ with $A \in  e$ . Let $\kappa$ be such an assignment (line 2). For each joint output attribute $A \in  \mathbf{y}$ ,we introduce a unique output attribute ${x}_{A}$ to relation $\kappa \left( A\right)$ and convert $A$ into a non-output attribute (line 5). To preserve the equivalence of query results,we force a one-to-one mapping between $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$ and $\operatorname{dom}\left( {x}_{A}\right)$ (line 6). The annotations of all tuples remain unchanged. By applying this procedure to every joint output attribute, we ultimately obtain a query in which the set of unique attributes is exactly the set of output attributes. Algorithm 5 then examines every relation $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\diamond }$ with $e \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ ,for which there is no other relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that $e - \mathrm{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ (lines 7-15). For each such relation,we add another relation ${e}^{\prime \prime }$ that includes a unique output attribute ${x}_{e}$ along with all output attributes present in $e$ ,and then convert all output attributes in $e$ into non-output attributes (line 10). To preserve the equivalence of the query results,we force a one-to-one mapping between $\operatorname{dom}\left( {e \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime }}\right)$ and $\operatorname{dom}\left( {x}_{e}\right)$ ,and set the annotation of each tuple in ${R}_{{e}^{\prime \prime }}$ as 1 (lines 12-14).

接下来，我们将研究范围缩小至$\exists$连通且净化后的查询。给定任意查询$Q$和实例$\mathcal{R}$，若$\mathcal{Q}$未分离，则应用算法5获得分离版本${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }$及更新后的输入规模为$O\left( N\right)$的实例${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$，使得$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$且fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$ fn-fhtw$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$。算法5首先处理联合输出属性（第1-6行）。如引理5.2所示，总能找到包含所有输出属性的fn-fhtw关系子集${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$（第1行），并将每个输出属性$A \in  \mathbf{y}$分配给满足$A \in  e$的任意关系$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$。设$\kappa$为此分配方案（第2行）。对每个联合输出属性$A \in  \mathbf{y}$，我们向关系$\kappa \left( A\right)$引入唯一输出属性${x}_{A}$，并将$A$转换为非输出属性（第5行）。为保持查询结果等价性，强制建立$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$与$\operatorname{dom}\left( {x}_{A}\right)$间的一一映射（第6行）。所有元组的标注保持不变。通过对每个联合输出属性执行此过程，最终获得唯一属性集恰好为输出属性集的查询。算法5随后检查每个满足$e \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$且不存在其他关系${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$使$e - \mathrm{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$成立的关系$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\diamond }$（第7-15行）。对此类关系，添加包含唯一输出属性${x}_{e}$及$e$中所有输出属性的新关系${e}^{\prime \prime }$，并将$e$中所有输出属性转为非输出属性（第10行）。为保持查询结果等价性，强制建立$\operatorname{dom}\left( {e \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime }}\right)$与$\operatorname{dom}\left( {x}_{e}\right)$间的一一映射，并将${R}_{{e}^{\prime \prime }}$中每个元组的标注设为1（第12-14行）。

LEMMA 5.2. There exists a subset ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ of fn-fhtw relations such that $\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }}}e$ .

引理5.2. 存在fn-fhtw关系的子集${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$使得$\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }}}e$成立。

Proof of Lemma 5.2. As $Q$ is acyclic, $q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack$ is also acyclic. Every acyclic query has an optimal fractional edge covering ${\rho }^{ * }$ that is also integral [29],i.e., ${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$ or ${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0$ for any $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ . Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ be the set of relations for which ${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$ holds for every $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ . For every attribute $A \in  \mathbf{y}$ , there must exist a relation $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ such that $A \in  e$ . Implied by Corollary 3.5, $\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }\right|  =$ fn-fhtw.

引理5.2的证明。由于$Q$是非循环的，$q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack$也是非循环的。每个非循环查询都存在一个最优分数边覆盖${\rho }^{ * }$，该覆盖同时也是整数的[29]，即对于任何$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$或${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0$。设${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$为满足对每个$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$都有${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$成立的关系集合。对于每个属性$A \in  \mathbf{y}$，必存在一个关系$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$使得$A \in  e$。根据推论3.5，$\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }\right|  =$ fn-fhtw。

Example 5.3. We continue with Example 3.8 in which $Q$ is not separated. Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{6}}\right\}$ be the chosen subset of relations in line 1. Recall that $\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{C}_{2}}\right\}$ . There is only one way to assign: ${A}_{1}$ to ${e}_{1},{A}_{2}$ to ${e}_{2},{A}_{3}$ to ${e}_{3}$ ,and ${C}_{2}$ to ${e}_{6}$ . As ${C}_{2}$ is not unique,we introduce a unique output attribute ${B}_{4}$ to ${e}_{6}$ and convert ${C}_{2}$ into a non-output attribute. As no relation contains all non-output attributes of ${e}_{6}$ ,we add ${e}_{4} = \left\{  {{A}_{4},{B}_{4}}\right\}$ with a unique output attribute ${A}_{4}$ to $\mathcal{E}$ and convert ${B}_{4}$ into a non-output attribute. The resulting query $\left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime }}\right)$ is separated,where ${\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{A}_{4},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3},{B}_{4},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  ,{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {{e}_{1} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right\}  ,{e}_{2} = \left\{  {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,{e}_{3} = }\right\}$ $\left\{  {{A}_{3},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,{e}_{4} = \left\{  {{A}_{4},{B}_{4}}\right\}  ,{e}_{5} = \left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  ,{e}_{6} = \left\{  {{B}_{3},{B}_{4},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  \}$ and ${\mathbf{y}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{A}_{4}}\right\}$ .

示例5.3。我们继续讨论示例3.8，其中$Q$未被分离。设${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{6}}\right\}$为第1行选定的关系子集。回顾$\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{C}_{2}}\right\}$。仅有一种赋值方式：将${A}_{1}$赋给${e}_{1},{A}_{2}$，${e}_{2},{A}_{3}$赋给${e}_{3}$，以及${C}_{2}$赋给${e}_{6}$。由于${C}_{2}$不唯一，我们为${e}_{6}$引入唯一输出属性${B}_{4}$，并将${C}_{2}$转换为非输出属性。由于没有关系包含${e}_{6}$的所有非输出属性，我们添加${e}_{4} = \left\{  {{A}_{4},{B}_{4}}\right\}$（带唯一输出属性${A}_{4}$）到$\mathcal{E}$，并将${B}_{4}$转换为非输出属性。最终查询$\left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime }}\right)$被分离，其中${\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{A}_{4},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3},{B}_{4},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  ,{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {{e}_{1} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right\}  ,{e}_{2} = \left\{  {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,{e}_{3} = }\right\}$、$\left\{  {{A}_{3},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,{e}_{4} = \left\{  {{A}_{4},{B}_{4}}\right\}  ,{e}_{5} = \left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  ,{e}_{6} = \left\{  {{B}_{3},{B}_{4},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right\}  \}$和${\mathbf{y}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{A}_{4}}\right\}$。

THEOREM 5.4. Any 3-connected and cleansed acyclic query $Q$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ , can be transformed into a separated acyclic query ${Q}^{\prime }$ and an instance ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ of input size $O\left( N\right)$ within $O\left( N\right)$ time,such that $\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ and $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ .

定理5.4. 任何3-连通且净化后的无环查询$Q$及输入规模为$N$的实例$\mathcal{R}$，都可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内转化为分离无环查询${Q}^{\prime }$及输入规模为$O\left( N\right)$的实例${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$，使得$\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$且$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$。

Thanks to these helper procedures, we can ultimately focus on separated acyclic queries at last, which constitute the most technical part of this section. We delve into the structural properties of separated acyclic queries in Section 5.2 and present our output-optimal algorithm in Section 5.3. The main result achieved is summarised in Theorem 5.4.

得益于这些辅助流程，我们最终得以聚焦于分离无环查询——这构成了本节最具技术性的部分。我们将在5.2节深入探讨分离无环查询的结构特性，并在5.3节提出输出最优算法。取得的主要成果总结于定理5.4中。

THEOREM 5.5. For any separated acyclic query $Q$ ,and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT,the query result $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed in $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$ time.

定理5.5. 对于任何分离无环查询$Q$，以及输入规模为$N$、输出规模为OUT的实例$\mathcal{R}$，查询结果$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$时间内计算得出。

We now briefly analyze the complexity of Algorithm 3. Both CLEANSE and SEPARATE procedures take $O\left( N\right)$ time. From Theorem 5.5,each invocation of the HybriDYANNAKAKIs procedure takes $O\left( {N \cdot  {\left| {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}^{\prime }\right) \right| }^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}^{\prime }\right) }}}\right)$ time. Implied by Lemma 3.2 and Theorem 5.4,fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}^{\prime }\right)  =$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)  \leq$ fn-fhtw(Q). Moreover,when there are no dangling tuples, $\left| {{\mathcal{Q}}_{i}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}^{\prime }\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$ . Putting everything together, we obtain:

现简要分析算法3的复杂度：CLEANSE与SEPARATE流程均耗时$O\left( N\right)$。根据定理5.5，每次HybriDYANNAKAKIs流程调用耗时$O\left( {N \cdot  {\left| {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}^{\prime }\right) \right| }^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}^{\prime }\right) }}}\right)$。由引理3.2和定理5.4可知，fn-fhtw$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}^{\prime }\right)  =$ fn-fhtw$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)  \leq$ fn-fhtw(Q)。此外，当不存在悬空元组时，$\left| {{\mathcal{Q}}_{i}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}^{\prime }\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$。综合以上可得：

THEOREM 5.6. For any acyclic query $Q$ ,and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT, the query result $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed in $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time,where $\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ is the free-connex fractional hypertree width of $Q$ .

定理5.6. 对于任何无环查询$Q$，及输入规模为$N$、输出规模为OUT的实例$\mathcal{R}$，查询结果$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间内计算完成，其中$\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$是$Q$的自由连接分数超树宽度。

### 5.2 Structural Properties of Separated Acyclic Queries

### 5.2 分离无环查询的结构特性

In the remainder of this section, we focus on separated acyclic queries. We discover a nice structural property of separated acyclic queries regarding free-connex fractional hypertree width. Recall that in a separated query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,every output attribute is unique,every unique attribute is an output attribute,and for each relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ with $e \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ ,there exists some other relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ e\}$ with $e - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ . In an arbitrary TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,a node is said to be incident to another node if an edge exists between them. For a node $e$ ,let ${\mathcal{N}}_{e}$ denote the set of nodes incident to it. A node $e$ is a leaf if it is only incident to another node,i.e., $\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{e}\right|  = 1$ .

本节剩余部分将聚焦于分离无环查询。我们发现分离无环查询关于自由连接分数超树宽度的优美结构特性：在分离查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$中，每个输出属性都是唯一的，每个唯一属性都是输出属性，且对于每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$与$e \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$，总存在另一个关系${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ e\}$与$e - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$。在任意树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$中，若两节点间存在边则称其相邻。对于节点$e$，用${\mathcal{N}}_{e}$表示其相邻节点集合。当节点$e$仅与单个节点相邻时（即$\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{e}\right|  = 1$），该节点称为叶节点。

LEMMA 5.7. Any separated acyclic query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ has a width-1 TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ such that there is a one-to-one correspondence between the set of nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ and the set of relations containing output attribute(s) in $\mathcal{T}$ . $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is called a separated TD for $Q$ .

引理5.7. 任何分离无环查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$都存在宽度为1的树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，其节点集与包含输出属性的关系集之间具有一一对应关系。该树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$称为$Q$的分离树分解。

Proof of Lemma 5.7. Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : e \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing \}$ be the set of relations without non-output attributes. Implied by the GYO reduction [2],the join query $\left( {\mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y},{\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)$ derived by relations in ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ is also acyclic since all (unique) output attributes can be removed first and then all relations containing output attributes. Let $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{ \bullet  },{\chi }_{ \bullet  }}\right)$ a width-1 TD for $\left( {\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y},{\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)$ . Note that there is a one-to-one correspondence between nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{ \bullet  }$ and relations in ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ . For each $e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ ,we identify an arbitrary ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ e\}$ such that $e - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ,and add $e$ as a child node of ${e}^{\prime }$ . It can be checked that the resulting $\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is a valid width-1 $\operatorname{TD}$ for $Q$ ,and each relation containing an output attribute is a leaf node. It remains to show that each leaf node of $\mathcal{T}$ also contains (unique) output attributes. By contradiction,we assume that there exists some leaf node $e$ of $\mathcal{T}$ such that $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ . Let ${e}^{\prime }$ be the unique node incident to $e$ . As $Q$ is cleansed, $e - {e}^{\prime } \neq  \varnothing$ . Together with $e \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing ,e$ must contain some unique non-output attribute, leading to a contradiction.

引理5.7证明. 设${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : e \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing \}$为不含非输出属性的关系集合。根据GYO归约[2]可知，由${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$中关系导出的连接查询$\left( {\mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y},{\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)$也是无环的——因为所有（唯一）输出属性可先被移除，随后处理含输出属性的关系。设$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{ \bullet  },{\chi }_{ \bullet  }}\right)$为$\left( {\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y},{\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)$的宽度1树分解，注意${\mathcal{T}}_{ \bullet  }$中节点与${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$中关系存在一一对应。对于每个$e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$，我们任选满足$e - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$的${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ e\}$，将$e$添加为${e}^{\prime }$的子节点。可验证所得$\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$构成$Q$的有效宽度1$\operatorname{TD}$，且每个含输出属性的关系都是叶节点。现需证明$\mathcal{T}$的每个叶节点也包含（唯一）输出属性。反设存在叶节点$e$满足$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$，令${e}^{\prime }$为其唯一相邻节点。由于$Q$已被净化，$e - {e}^{\prime } \neq  \varnothing$。结合$e \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing ,e$必含某个唯一非输出属性，导致矛盾。

See an example in Figure 9. From Lemma 5.7, the number of leaf nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ is exactly fn-fhtw(Q). For a pair of incident nodes ${e}_{1},{e}_{2}$ ,we use $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}$ to denote the undirected edge between them,use $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ (resp. $\left. \left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right) \right)$ to denote the directed edge from ${e}_{1}$ to ${e}_{2}$ (resp. from ${e}_{2}$ to ${e}_{1}$ ). Removing edge $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}$ separates $\mathcal{T}$ into two connected subtrees ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ and ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}$ ,which contains ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{2}$ separately. Let ${\mathcal{L}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ be the set of leaf nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ . For simplicity,we use ${\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}} = \frac{\left| {\mathcal{L}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right| }{\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( Q\right) }$ to measure the fraction of the number of nodes containing output attributes, or equivalently the number of leaf nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ . These parameters will be frequently used for partitioning the input instance in the next subsection. For each edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ ,the subtree ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ derives a sub-query ${Q}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \bigoplus \; \boxtimes  \;{R}_{e}$ ,i.e., aggregates over all non-output attributes except the join attributes between ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{2}$ . Note that these join attributes will appear in some nodes of $\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ ,and should be kept in the subsequent computation.

参见图9中的示例。根据引理5.7，$\mathcal{T}$中的叶节点数恰好等于fn-fhtw(Q)。对于一对邻接节点${e}_{1},{e}_{2}$，我们用$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}$表示它们之间的无向边，用$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$（或$\left. \left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right) \right)$）表示从${e}_{1}$指向${e}_{2}$（或从${e}_{2}$指向${e}_{1}$）的有向边。移除边$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}$会将$\mathcal{T}$分割为两个连通子树${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$和${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}$，分别包含${e}_{1}$和${e}_{2}$。设${\mathcal{L}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$为${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$中的叶节点集合。为简化表述，我们使用${\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}} = \frac{\left| {\mathcal{L}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right| }{\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( Q\right) }$来衡量包含输出属性的节点比例，即${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$中叶节点的数量。这些参数将在下一小节中频繁用于划分输入实例。对于每条边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$，子树${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$派生出一个子查询${Q}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \bigoplus \; \boxtimes  \;{R}_{e}$，即对${e}_{1}$与${e}_{2}$之间除连接属性外所有非输出属性的聚合运算。注意这些连接属性将出现在$\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$的某些节点中，并应在后续计算中保留。

Fig. 9. A separated width-1 TD for query in Figure 8. ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ and ${e}_{4}$ are leaf nodes. ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } = \left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right\}$ . All (unique) output attributes ${A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{A}_{4}$ are highlighted in red. Leaf nodes ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}$ correspond to relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}$ . Removing edge $\left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right\}$ leads to two sub-trees, where ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}$ contains nodes ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{5}$ and ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}$ contains nodes ${e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{6}$ . ${Q}_{{e}_{5},{e}_{6}} = { \oplus  }_{{B}_{1},{B}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right)  \boxtimes$ ${R}_{2}\left( {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right)$ . And, $\phi \left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right)  = \phi \left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}\right)  = \frac{1}{2}.$

图9. 图8查询对应的宽度为1的分离树分解。${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$和${e}_{4}$是叶节点。${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } = \left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right\}$。所有（唯一）输出属性${A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3},{A}_{4}$以红色高亮显示。叶节点${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}$对应关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}$。移除边$\left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right\}$会生成两棵子树，其中${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}$包含节点${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{5}$，${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}$包含节点${e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{6}$。${Q}_{{e}_{5},{e}_{6}} = { \oplus  }_{{B}_{1},{B}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right)  \boxtimes$${R}_{2}\left( {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{C}_{1},{C}_{2}}\right)$。并且，$\phi \left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right)  = \phi \left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}\right)  = \frac{1}{2}.$

### 5.3 Our Algorithm for Separated Acyclic Queries

### 5.3 面向分离无环查询的算法

Given an arbitrary separated acyclic query, we next characterize an optimal condition on the input instances for which the runtime of the Yannakakis algorithm can be bounded as stated in Theorem 5.5. In other words, the Yannakakis algorithm is already output-optimal in these cases. We then explore how to partition an arbitrary input instance into a collection of sub-instances, each of which satisfies this optimal condition individually. Likewise, we assume that a parameter OUT is known such that OUT $\leq$ OUT $\leq  c \cdot$ OUT for some constant $c$ . This assumption can be removed without increasing the complexity asymptotically [30]. Finally, we introduce the notion of edge label, which encapsulates both data statistics and query structure.

给定任意分离无环查询，我们接下来刻画输入实例的最优条件，在该条件下Yannakakis算法的时间复杂度可如定理5.5所述界定。换言之，Yannakakis算法在这些情况下已是输出最优的。我们继而探讨如何将任意输入实例划分为若干子实例集合，其中每个子实例都单独满足该最优条件。同样地，我们假设已知参数OUT满足OUT$\leq$OUT$\leq  c \cdot$OUT（其中$c$为常数）。该假设可在不渐近增加复杂度的情况下移除[30]。最后，我们引入边标签概念，其同时封装了数据统计信息和查询结构。

Definition 5.8 (Edge Label). For a separated acyclic query $Q$ and a separated width-1 TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ , an instance $\mathcal{R}$ and parameter $\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$ ,an edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ in $\mathcal{T}$ is

定义5.8（边标签）。对于分离无环查询$Q$、宽度为1的分离树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$、实例$\mathcal{R}$及参数$\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}$，$\mathcal{T}$中的边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$被定义为：

- large if $\left| {{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  \ltimes  t}\right|  > {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$ holds for every tuple $t \in  {\pi }_{{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}{R}_{{e}_{1}}$ ; and

- 大型边，若对每个元组$t \in  {\pi }_{{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}{R}_{{e}_{1}}$都满足$\left| {{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  \ltimes  t}\right|  > {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$；且

- small if $\left| {{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  \ltimes  t}\right|  \leq  {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$ holds for every tuple $t \in  {\pi }_{{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}{R}_{{e}_{1}}$ ; and

- 小型边，若对每个元组$t \in  {\pi }_{{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}{R}_{{e}_{1}}$都满足$\left| {{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  \ltimes  t}\right|  \leq  {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$；且

- unlabeled otherwise.

- 否则为未标记边。

Furthermore,a small edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is limited if $\left| {{\bigoplus }_{{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|  \leq  {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$ .

此外，若$\left| {{\bigoplus }_{{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|  \leq  {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$，则小边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$会受到限制。

#### 5.3.1 Optimal Condition of Yannakakis Algorithm

#### 5.3.1 Yannakakis算法的最优条件

Now, we can see a natural connection between the Yannakakis algorithm and edge labels:

现在，我们可以看出Yannakakis算法与边标签之间的自然关联：

LEMMA 5.9. For a separated acyclic query $Q$ with a separated width-1 TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT,if edge $\left( {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right)$ is small,the intermediate join result ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \bowtie  {Q}_{e,{e}^{\prime }}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed in $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}}}\right)$ time.

引理5.9. 对于具有分离宽度1树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的分离无环查询$Q$，输入规模为$N$、输出规模为OUT的实例$\mathcal{R}$，若边$\left( {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right)$较小，则中间连接结果${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \bowtie  {Q}_{e,{e}^{\prime }}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}}}\right)$时间内计算完成。

Proof of Lemma 5.9. Consider an arbitrary node ${e}_{1} \in$ nodes $\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{e,{e}^{\prime }}\right)$ . Let ${p}_{{e}_{1}}$ be the parent node of ${e}_{1}$ . The intermediate join result materialized at ${e}_{1}$ is ${R}_{{e}_{1}} \bowtie  \left( {{ \bowtie  }_{{e}_{2}}{Q}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}}\right)$ ,where ${e}_{2}$ is over all child nodes of ${e}_{1}$ . Without dangling tuples,every tuple $t \in  {R}_{{e}_{1}}$ participates in at most ${\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}}$ query result in ${Q}_{{e}_{1},{p}_{{e}_{1}}}$ ; otherwise,there must exist some tuple $t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {e \cap  {e}^{\prime }}\right)$ that can participate in more than ${\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}}$ query result in ${Q}_{e,{e}^{\prime }}$ ,contradicting the fact that edge $\left( {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right)$ is small. As there are at most $N$ tuples in ${R}_{{e}_{1}}$ ,the number of intermediate join results materialized at ${e}_{1}$ is at most $O\left( {N \cdot  {\operatorname{OUT}}^{{\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}}}\right)$ . When we move to ${e}^{\prime }$ ,the size of ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \bowtie  {Q}_{e,{e}^{\prime }}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be bounded similarly.

引理5.9证明. 考虑任意节点${e}_{1} \in$的节点$\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{e,{e}^{\prime }}\right)$。设${p}_{{e}_{1}}$为${e}_{1}$的父节点。在${e}_{1}$处物化的中间连接结果为${R}_{{e}_{1}} \bowtie  \left( {{ \bowtie  }_{{e}_{2}}{Q}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}}\right)$，其中${e}_{2}$遍历${e}_{1}$的所有子节点。若无悬挂元组，每个元组$t \in  {R}_{{e}_{1}}$最多参与${Q}_{{e}_{1},{p}_{{e}_{1}}}$中的${\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}}$个查询结果；否则必存在某个元组$t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {e \cap  {e}^{\prime }}\right)$能参与${Q}_{e,{e}^{\prime }}$中超过${\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}}$个查询结果，这与边$\left( {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right)$较小的事实矛盾。由于${R}_{{e}_{1}}$中最多有$N$个元组，在${e}_{1}$处物化的中间连接结果数量最多为$O\left( {N \cdot  {\operatorname{OUT}}^{{\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}}}\right)$。当转移至${e}^{\prime }$时，${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \bowtie  {Q}_{e,{e}^{\prime }}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的规模可类似地界定。

Recall that ${\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }} = 1 - \frac{1}{\text{ fn-fhtw }}$ if ${e}^{\prime }$ is a leaf node. Hence,we come to the optimal condition below:

回顾当${e}^{\prime }$为叶节点时${\phi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }} = 1 - \frac{1}{\text{ fn-fhtw }}$。因此，我们得出以下最优条件：

COROLLARY 5.10 (OPTIMAL CONDITION FOR YANNAKAKIS). For a separated acyclic query $Q =$ (V,E,y)with a separated width-1 TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT,if there is a leaf node ${e}^{\prime }$ of $\mathcal{T}$ such that edge $\left( {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right)$ is small for the only node ${e}^{\prime }$ incident to $e$ , then $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed in $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}}\right)$ time.

推论5.10（YANNAKAKIS最优条件）。对于具有分离宽度1树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的分离无环查询$Q =$(V,E,y)，若输入规模为$N$、输出规模为OUT的实例$\mathcal{R}$存在一个叶节点${e}^{\prime }$使得边$\left( {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right)$对于唯一与$e$关联的节点${e}^{\prime }$是小的，则$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}}\right)$时间内计算完成。

#### 5.3.2 Partition Separated Acyclic Queries

#### 5.3.2 分区分离无环查询

The input instance does not necessarily meet the optimal condition above, so we cannot apply the Yannakakis algorithm directly. Even worse, it is unknown how to efficiently decide the label of each edge since the definition of edge labeling is based on the sub-query, which may be too expensive to compute. Consequently, the most technically challenging step is to identify an efficient ordering for labeling the edges and to iteratively isolate a sub-instance once it meets the optimal condition specified in Corollary 5.10.

输入实例未必满足上述最优条件，因此无法直接应用Yannakakis算法。更棘手的是，由于边标记的定义基于子查询（其计算代价可能过高），目前尚无法高效判定各边的标记。因此，技术层面最具挑战性的步骤在于：确定边标记的高效排序方案，并在子实例满足推论5.10所述最优条件时，逐步将其隔离处理。

As described in Algorithm 6,let $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ be a separated width-1 TD for $Q$ (line 1),with all edges unlabeled initially. Again,there is a one-to-one correspondence between relations in $\mathcal{E}$ and nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ . Hence,we also use $e$ to denote the node in $\mathcal{T}$ that corresponds to relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ . We put(T,R) into a candidate set $\mathcal{P}$ of instances to be partitioned (line 2). In general,consider an arbitrary pair $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \mathcal{P}$ . From Lemma 5.11,we apply limited-imply-limited rule to infer edge labels (lines 5-6). If it meets the optimal condition, we compute the result immediately (lines 7-8) and remove it from $\mathcal{P}$ (line 16). Otherwise,we further partition it (lines 9-15). More specially,we pick a non-labeled edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ such that every other incoming edge to ${e}_{1}$ is small,i.e.,edge $\left( {{e}_{3},{t}_{1}}\right)$ is small for each node ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$ (line 10). We compute ${\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ using the Yannakakis algorithm along ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ rooted at ${e}_{1}$ (line 11). A tuple $t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}\right)$ is heavy if $\left| {{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)  \ltimes  t}\right|  > {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$ ,and light otherwise. Now,we construct two sub-instances for ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ ,which contain heavy and light tuples in ${R}_{{e}_{1}}$ separately (line 15),and two copies of ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ in which edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is further labeled as large and small separately (line 13-14). By Lemma 5.12,we can apply large-reverse-limited rule to infer $\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$ as limited when $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is labeled as large (line 14). We add these two sub-instances into $\mathcal{P}$ (line 15) and also remove $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right)$ from $\mathcal{P}$ (line 16). We continue applying this procedure to every remaining instance in $\mathcal{P}$ until $\mathcal{P}$ becomes empty (line 3).

如算法6所述，设$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$为$Q$的宽度为1的独立树分解（第1行），初始时所有边均未标记。关系$\mathcal{E}$与节点$\mathcal{T}$之间存在一一对应关系，因此我们同样用$e$表示对应于关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$的$\mathcal{T}$节点。将(T,R)放入待划分实例的候选集$\mathcal{P}$中（第2行）。对于任意节点对$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \mathcal{P}$，根据引理5.11，我们应用有限蕴含有限规则推导边标签（第5-6行）。若满足最优条件则立即计算结果（第7-8行）并从$\mathcal{P}$移除（第16行）；否则继续划分（第9-15行）。具体而言，选取未标记边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$使得通往${e}_{1}$的其他入边均为小边（即对每个节点${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$，边$\left( {{e}_{3},{t}_{1}}\right)$都是小边）（第10行）。沿以${e}_{1}$为根的${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$路径，使用Yannakakis算法计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$（第11行）。当$\left| {{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)  \ltimes  t}\right|  > {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$时元组$t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}\right)$为重元组，否则为轻元组。为${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$构建两个子实例：分别包含${R}_{{e}_{1}}$中的重元组和轻元组（第15行），以及两个${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$副本——其中边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$分别被标记为大边和小边（第13-14行）。根据引理5.12，当$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$标记为大边时，可应用大反向有限规则推导$\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$为有限边（第14行）。将这两个子实例加入$\mathcal{P}$（第15行）并移除$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right)$（第16行）。重复该过程直至$\mathcal{P}$为空集（第3行）。

LEMMA 5.11 (LIMITED-IMPLY-LIMITED). For any node ${e}_{1}$ ,if there exists a node ${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}$ such that edge $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$ is limited for every node ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$ ,then edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ must be limited.

引理5.11（有限蕴含有限规则）。对于任意节点${e}_{1}$，若存在节点${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}$使得对每个节点${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$边$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$均为有限边，则边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$必为有限边。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 6: HYBRIDYANNAKAKIS $\left( {\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right) ,\mathcal{R}}\right)$

算法6：混合式Yannakakis算法 $\left( {\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right) ,\mathcal{R}}\right)$

---

1 $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \leftarrow$ a separated width-1 TD of $\mathcal{Q}$ with all edges of $\mathcal{T}$ non-labeled;

1 $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \leftarrow$ 生成$\mathcal{Q}$的分离宽度为1的树分解，其中$\mathcal{T}$的所有边均未标记；

	$\mathcal{P} \leftarrow  \{ (\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R}\} ,\mathcal{S} \leftarrow  \mathbf{0};$

	while $\mathcal{P} \neq  \varnothing$ do

	while $\mathcal{P} \neq  \varnothing$ 执行

				$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right)  \leftarrow$ an arbitrary pair in $\mathcal{P}$ ;

				$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right)  \leftarrow$ 在$\mathcal{P}$中任选一个节点对；

				while $\exists$ non-labeled $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ in ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ such that $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$ is limited for each ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$ do

				当$\exists$存在未标记边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$位于${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$中，且对每个${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$而言$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$受限时，执行

						Label edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ as limited;

						将边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$标记为受限；

				if there is a leaf node ${e}_{1}$ with small (or limited) edge $\left( {*,{e}_{1}}\right)$ in ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ then

				若存在叶节点${e}_{1}$，其在${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$中的边$\left( {*,{e}_{1}}\right)$为小型（或受限）边，则

						$\mathcal{S} \leftarrow  \mathcal{S} \oplus  \mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ for computing $\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ via Yannakakis algorithm on $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at ${e}_{1}$ ;

						$\mathcal{S} \leftarrow  \mathcal{S} \oplus  \mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ 通过以${e}_{1}$为根的$\mathcal{T}$上运行Yannakakis算法计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$；

				else

				否则

							$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)  \leftarrow$ a non-labeled edge in ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ s.t. $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$ is small for each ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$ ;

							$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)  \leftarrow$ 在${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$中选择未标记边，要求对每个${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$而言$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$为小型边；

							Compute ${\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ by the Yannakakis algorithm on ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ rooted at ${e}_{1}$ ;

							以${e}_{1}$为根在${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$上运行Yannakakis算法计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$；

							$\mathcal{H} \leftarrow  \left\{  {t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}\right)  : \left| {{\sigma }_{{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2} = t}{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|  > {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}}\right\}  ;$

							${\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime } \leftarrow  {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ with $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$ labeled as large,limited separately;

							${\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime } \leftarrow  {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ 将$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$分别标记为大型边和受限边；

							${\mathcal{T}}_{2}^{\prime } \leftarrow  {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ with $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ labeled as small;

							${\mathcal{T}}_{2}^{\prime } \leftarrow  {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ 将$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$标记为小型边；

						$\mathcal{P} \leftarrow  \mathcal{P} \cup  \left\{  {\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime } - \left\{  {R}_{{e}_{1}}\right\}   \cup  \left\{  {{R}_{{e}_{1}} \ltimes  \mathcal{H}}\right\}  }\right) ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{2}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime } - \left\{  {R}_{{e}_{1}}\right\}   \cup  \left\{  {{R}_{{e}_{1}} \vartriangleright  \mathcal{H}}\right\}  }\right) }\right\}  ;$

				$\mathcal{P} \leftarrow  \mathcal{P} - \left\{  \left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right) \right\}$

	return $\mathcal{S}$ ;

	返回 $\mathcal{S}$ ；

---

Proof of Lemma 5.11. For ${Q}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ ,we observe the following:

引理5.11的证明。对于${Q}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$，我们观察到以下现象：

$$
\left| {\mathop{\bigoplus }\limits_{{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}\right|  \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}-\{ {e}_{2}\} }}\left| {\mathop{\bigoplus }\limits_{{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}}\right|  \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}-\{ {e}_{2}\} }}{\operatorname{OUT}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}} \leq  {\operatorname{OUT}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}
$$

where the first inequality follows that $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}}\left( {e \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}-\{ {e}_{2}\} }}\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}}}\left( {e \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right)$ and the second inequality follows that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  }}{\phi }_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}} = {\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ . By definition, $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ must be limited.

第一个不等式成立源于$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}}\left( {e \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}-\{ {e}_{2}\} }}\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}}}\left( {e \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right)$，第二个不等式成立源于$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  }}{\phi }_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}} = {\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$。根据定义，$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$必须是有界的。

LEMMA 5.12 (LARGE-REVERSE-LIMITED). If edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is large,then edge $\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$ must be limited.

引理5.12（大边反向有界性）。若边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$是大边，则边$\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$必须是有界的。

Proof of Lemma 5.12. Consider an arbitrary tuple $t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}\right)$ . As $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is large, $t$ can be joined with at least OUT ${\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ query result of ${Q}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ . Without dangling tuples,every tuple ${t}^{\prime } \in  {\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}{Q}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}$ appears together with at least OUT ${}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$ tuples from ${\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}{Q}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$ in the final query result. This way, $\left| {{\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}}\right|  \leq  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - {\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}} = {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}}$ . Hence, $\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$ is limited.

引理5.12的证明。考虑任意元组$t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}}\right)$。由于$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$是大边，$t$可与至少OUT${\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$个${Q}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$的查询结果连接。在没有悬挂元组的情况下，每个元组${t}^{\prime } \in  {\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}{Q}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}$都会在最终查询结果中与至少OUT${}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$个来自${\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}{Q}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}$的元组共同出现。由此，$\left| {{\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}}\right|  \leq  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - {\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}} = {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}}$。因此，$\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$是有界的。

Example 5.13. We continue the example in Figure 9. Initially, all edges are unlabeled in (9.1). We start with applying line 13 to label edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right)$ since ${\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}   = \varnothing$ . In (9.2),the instance with large edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right)$ and limited edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ already meets the optimal condition. The remaining instance has a small edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ . We can apply a similar argument to edges $\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{5}}\right) ,\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{6}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{6}}\right)$ . In (9.3),we are left with the remaining instance with small edges $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right) ,\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{5}}\right) ,\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{6}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{6}}\right)$ . Then,we can apply line 13 to label edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right)$ .

例5.13. 我们继续图9中的示例。初始状态下，(9.1)中所有边均未标注。由于${\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}   = \varnothing$，我们首先应用第13行对边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right)$进行标注。在(9.2)中，具有大边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right)$和受限边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$的实例已满足最优条件。剩余实例则包含小边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$。对于边$\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{5}}\right) ,\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{6}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{6}}\right)$可采用类似论证。在(9.3)中，我们处理带有小边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right) ,\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{5}}\right) ,\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{6}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{6}}\right)$的剩余实例，随后应用第13行标注边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right)$。

In (9.4),for the instance with large edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right)$ and limited edge $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}\right)$ ,we can apply line 13 to partition both edges $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{2}}\right)$ . Suppose we partition edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ wlog. In (9.6),the instance with small edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ already meets the optimal condition. In (9.7),we are left with an instance with large edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ and limited edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right)$ . We can apply the limited-imply-limited rule to infer edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{2}}\right)$ as limited. This instance also meets the optimal condition.

在(9.4)中，对于具有大边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right)$和受限边$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}\right)$的实例，可应用第13行同时对边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{2}}\right)$进行划分。假设我们选择划分边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$。在(9.6)中，含小边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$的实例已满足最优条件。而在(9.7)中，我们得到具有大边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$和受限边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right)$的实例，应用"受限蕴含受限"规则可推断边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{2}}\right)$为受限状态，该实例同样满足最优条件。

In (9.5),for the other instance with small edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right)$ ,we can apply line 13 to label both edges $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{4}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}\right)$ . Suppose we label edge $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{4}}\right)$ wlog. In (9.8),the instance with small edge $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{4}}\right)$ meets the optimal condition. We can apply a similar argument to edge $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{3}}\right)$ . In (9.9),the remaining instance has large edges $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{3}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{4}}\right)$ ,as well as limited edges $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{6}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{6}}\right)$ . We can apply the limited-imply-limited rule to infer edge $\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}\right)$ as limited. Now,we can apply line 13 to label edges $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{2}}\right)$ . Suppose we label edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ . In (9.10),the instance with small edge $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$ already meets the optimal condition. In (9.11),we are left with the instance with large edge $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$ and limited edge $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{2}}\right)$ . Then,we can apply the limited-imply-limited rule to infer edge $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{2}}\right)$ as limited. This instance also meets the optimal condition. Now, $\mathcal{P} = \varnothing$ ,and we are done with the partition procedure.

在(9.5)中，对于另一条含小边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{6}}\right)$的实例，可应用第13行规则标记边$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{4}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}\right)$。假设我们优先标记边$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{4}}\right)$。在(9.8)中，含小边$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{4}}\right)$的实例满足最优条件。类似论证可应用于边$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{3}}\right)$。在(9.9)中，剩余实例包含大边$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{3}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{4}}\right)$，以及受限边$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{6}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{6}}\right)$。应用受限传导规则可推导边$\left( {{e}_{6},{e}_{5}}\right)$为受限。此时可应用第13行规则标记边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{2}}\right)$。假设我们标记边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$。在(9.10)中，含小边$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{1}}\right)$的实例已满足最优条件。在(9.11)中，剩余实例含大边$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$和受限边$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{2}}\right)$。应用受限传导规则可推导边$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{2}}\right)$为受限。该实例亦满足最优条件。至此$\mathcal{P} = \varnothing$，划分流程完成。

#### 5.3.3 Analysis

#### 5.3.3 分析

To show the correctness of Algorithm 6,we first need to prove the following result:

为证明算法6的正确性，需先验证以下结论：

LEMMA 5.14. In Algorithm 6,for any $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \mathcal{P}$ ,either line 8,line 10,or line 13 can be applied.

引理5.14. 在算法6中，对于任意$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \mathcal{P}$，必可应用第8行、第10行或第13行规则。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 7: IDENTIFYLEAF $\left( {Q,{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }}\right)$

算法7：IDENTIFYLEAF $\left( {Q,{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }}\right)$

---

while ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ is not a single node do

while ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$非单节点时执行

	Root ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ at an arbitrary node $r$ such that $r \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ ;

	将${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$根植于满足$r \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$的任意节点$r$；

	if $\exists$ node $e$ with its parent ${p}_{e}$ such that edge $\left( {e,{p}_{e}}\right)$ is large then ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime } \leftarrow  {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{T}}_{e,{p}_{e}}^{\prime }$ ;

	if $\exists$中存在节点$e$其父边$\left( {e,{p}_{e}}\right)$为大边 then ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime } \leftarrow  {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{T}}_{e,{p}_{e}}^{\prime }$；

	else return $r$ ;

	else 返回 $r$；

return the single node in ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ ;

返回${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$中的单节点；

Proof of Lemma 5.14. Consider an arbitrary instance ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ with a partially labeled separated width- 1 TD $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$ . By contradiction,we assume that neither line 8,line 10,nor line 13 of Algorithm 6 can be applied. We next show how to identify a leaf node $e$ of ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ such that $\left( {*,e}\right)$ is small; hence, ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ already meets the optimal condition, leading to a contradiction.

引理5.14证明. 设任意实例${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$具有部分标记的宽度-1树分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$。反设算法6第8、10、13行规则均不适用。下文将识别${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$的叶节点$e$使得$\left( {*,e}\right)$为小边，从而${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$已满足最优条件，形成矛盾。

As shown in Algorithm 7,the high-level idea is to conceptually remove nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ ,until a single node is left,which is exactly the leaf node as desired. We root ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ at an arbitrary node $r \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$ such that $r \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ . Let ${p}_{e}$ be the parent of $e$ in this rooted tree. If there exists a node $e$ such that $\left( {e,{p}_{e}}\right)$ is large,we simply remove the whole subtree ${\mathcal{T}}_{e,{p}_{e}}^{\prime }$ and continue the process. Otherwise, every edge $\left( {e,{p}_{e}}\right)$ is small,including the edge $\left( {*,r}\right)$ incident to $r$ ,hence $r$ is returned.

如算法7所示，高层级思路是在概念上移除${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$中的节点，直至仅剩单一节点，该节点恰好是所需的叶节点。我们将${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$以任意节点$r \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$为根进行定位，使得$r \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$。设${p}_{e}$为该有根树中$e$的父节点。若存在节点$e$使得$\left( {e,{p}_{e}}\right)$较大，则直接移除整个子树${\mathcal{T}}_{e,{p}_{e}}^{\prime }$并继续该过程。否则，每条边$\left( {e,{p}_{e}}\right)$均较小，包括与$r$关联的边$\left( {*,r}\right)$，因此返回$r$。

It remains to show that a node $r \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$ with $r \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ can always be found at line 2 . By contradiction,we assume that such a node does not exist. In the execution process above, ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ is always a connected subtree. For clarity,let ${\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$ be the state of ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ at some point and let $\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$ be the subtree(s) removed by the process above. As ${\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$ is connected and ${\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$ does not contain any leaf nodes of $\mathcal{T},\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$ must contain all leaf nodes and hence be disconnected. Moreover,for each pair of nodes ${e}_{1} \in$ nodes $\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }\right) ,{e}_{2} \in$ nodes $\left( {\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,the subtree ${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}^{\prime }$ has been removed due to the fact that edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is large at line 3 . This way,Lemma 5.15 is violated on ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ ,leading to a contradiction. Hence,it is always feasible to find a node $r \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$ with $r \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ .

仍需证明第2行总能找到满足$r \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$的节点$r \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$。采用反证法，假设该节点不存在。在上述执行过程中，${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$始终为连通子树。为明确起见，设${\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$为某时刻${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$的状态，$\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$为上述过程移除的子树。由于${\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$连通且不包含$\mathcal{T},\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$的任何叶节点，则$\mathcal{T},\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }$必包含所有叶节点而导致不连通。此外，对于每对节点${e}_{1} \in$节点$\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }\right) ,{e}_{2} \in$节点$\left( {\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}^{\prime }}\right)$，子树${\mathcal{T}}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}^{\prime }$因第3行中边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$较大而被移除。这导致${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$违反引理5.15，产生矛盾。因此总能找到满足$r \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$的节点$r \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$。

LEMMA 5.15 (NOT-ALL-LARGE). Consider a connected subtree ${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$ of $\mathcal{T}$ that does not contain any leaf node of $\mathcal{T}$ . For any instance ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ with non-empty query result,there must exist a pair of nodes ${e}_{1} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{1}\right) ,{e}_{2} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}}\right)$ such that $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is an edge in $\mathcal{T}$ but not large.

引理5.15（非全大边准则）。考虑$\mathcal{T}$的连通子树${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$，其不包含$\mathcal{T}$的任何叶节点。对于查询结果非空的任意实例${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$，必存在节点对${e}_{1} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{1}\right) ,{e}_{2} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}}\right)$使得$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$是$\mathcal{T}$中的边但非大边。

Proof of Lemma 5.15. Let ${U}_{1}$ be the set of nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$ that are incident to some nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{2}$ . Let ${U}_{2}$ be the set of nodes in $\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}$ that are incident to some nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$ . Note that there is a one-to-one correspondence between ${U}_{1}$ and ${U}_{2}$ . Let ${e}_{2} \in  {U}_{2}$ be the corresponding node for ${e}_{1} \in  {U}_{1}$ , i.e., $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is an edge. By contradiction,suppose every such edge $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ is large for every ${e}_{1} \in  {U}_{1}$ . Implied by Lemma 5.12,every such edge $\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$ is limited. Consider an arbitrary node ${e}_{1}^{ * } \in  {U}_{1}$ with its corresponding node ${e}_{2}^{ * } \in  {U}_{2}$ . We note that for every tuple in $t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {{e}_{1}^{ * } \cap  {e}_{2}^{ * }}\right)$ ,

引理5.15的证明。设${U}_{1}$为${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$中与${\mathcal{T}}_{2}$某些节点相邻的节点集合，${U}_{2}$为$\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{T}}_{1}$中与${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$某些节点相邻的节点集合。注意${U}_{1}$与${U}_{2}$之间存在一一对应关系。设${e}_{2} \in  {U}_{2}$为${e}_{1} \in  {U}_{1}$的对应节点，即$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$构成一条边。采用反证法，假设每条这样的边$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$对于所有${e}_{1} \in  {U}_{1}$都是大边。根据引理5.12可知，每条这样的边$\left( {{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right)$都是受限的。考虑任意节点${e}_{1}^{ * } \in  {U}_{1}$及其对应节点${e}_{2}^{ * } \in  {U}_{2}$，我们注意到对于$t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {{e}_{1}^{ * } \cap  {e}_{2}^{ * }}\right)$中的每个元组，

$$
\left| {{\sigma }_{{e}_{1}^{ * } \frown  {e}_{2}^{ * } = t}{Q}_{{e}_{1}^{ * },{e}_{2}^{ * }}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{{e}_{1} \in  {U}_{1}}}\left| {\mathop{\bigoplus }\limits_{{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}}{Q}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{{e}_{1} \in  {U}_{1} - \left\{  {e}_{1}^{ * }\right\}  }}{\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}} \leq  {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1}^{ * },{e}_{2}^{ * }}}
$$

where the last inequality is implied by the fact that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}_{1} \in  {U}_{1} - \left\{  {e}_{1}^{ * }\right\}  }}\left| {\mathcal{L}}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right|  = \left| {\mathcal{L}}_{{e}_{1}^{ * },{e}_{2}^{ * }}\right|$ . Hence,edge $\left( {{e}_{1}^{ * },{e}_{2}^{ * }}\right)$ is not large,leading to a contradiction.

末项不等式成立的原因是$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}_{1} \in  {U}_{1} - \left\{  {e}_{1}^{ * }\right\}  }}\left| {\mathcal{L}}_{{e}_{2},{e}_{1}}\right|  = \left| {\mathcal{L}}_{{e}_{1}^{ * },{e}_{2}^{ * }}\right|$。因此边$\left( {{e}_{1}^{ * },{e}_{2}^{ * }}\right)$并非大边，由此导出矛盾。

LEMMA 5.16. Algorithm 6 will terminate after running the while-loop at most $O\left( {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }\right)$ iterations.

引理5.16。算法6执行while循环最多$O\left( {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }\right)$次迭代后必将终止。

Proof of LEMMA 5.16. As each edge will be labeled once, and labeling each edge can lead to at most 2 sub-instances,the total number of sub-instances in $\mathcal{P}$ is $O\left( {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }\right)$ ,where the number of edges in $\mathcal{T}$ is bounded by the number of relations in $Q$ . Each iteration removes at least one sub-instance from $\mathcal{P}$ . Moreover,when a sub-instance is removed,it won’t be added back to $\mathcal{P}$ . Hence, $\mathcal{P}$ will become $\varnothing$ after at most $O\left( {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }\right)$ iterations.

引理5.16的证明。由于每条边仅被标记一次，且每次标记至多生成2个子实例，$\mathcal{P}$中的子实例总数不超过$O\left( {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }\right)$，其中$\mathcal{T}$的边数受限于$Q$中的关系数量。每次迭代至少从$\mathcal{P}$移除一个子实例，且被移除的子实例不会重新加入$\mathcal{P}$。因此，$\mathcal{P}$在最多$O\left( {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }\right)$次迭代后将成为$\varnothing$。

To compute ${\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ ,the Yannakakis algorithm needs to materialize the following intermediate join result: ${R}_{{e}_{1}} \bowtie  \left( {{ \bowtie  }_{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}-\{ {e}_{2}\} }{Q}_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right) }\right)$ . As there are $N$ tuples in ${R}_{{e}_{1}}$ ,and each tuple in ${R}_{{e}_{1}}$ can be joined with at most $\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}-\{ {e}_{2}\} }}{\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}} = {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$ tuples in this

接下来我们分析算法6的运行时间。首先关注第14行：对于任意节点${e}_{3} \in$${\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}} - \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$，由于边$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\right)$规模较小，根据引理5.10可在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}}}\right)$时间内计算出查询结果${Q}_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$。为计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$，Yannakakis算法需物化以下中间连接结果：${R}_{{e}_{1}} \bowtie  \left( {{ \bowtie  }_{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}-\{ {e}_{2}\} }{Q}_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right) }\right)$。鉴于${R}_{{e}_{1}}$中存在$N$个元组，且${R}_{{e}_{1}}$中每个元组最多可与$\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{{e}_{1}}-\{ {e}_{2}\} }}{\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{3},{e}_{1}}} = {\widetilde{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}$个元组连接

intermediate result,its size can be bounded by $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}}\right)$ . The cost of line 14 is bounded by Lemma 5.10. The cost of lines 15-18 is bounded by $O\left( N\right)$ . All other lines take $O\left( 1\right)$ time. As analyzed above,the number of the while-loop iterations is $O\left( {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }\right)$ ,still a constant. So,the partitioning procedure takes $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$ time. Putting everything together,we obtain:

该中间结果的大小上限为$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{{\phi }_{{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}}}\right)$。第14行耗时由引理5.10界定，15-18行耗时上限为$O\left( N\right)$，其余行耗时$O\left( 1\right)$。如前所述，while循环迭代次数为$O\left( {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }\right)$（仍为常数），故分区过程总耗时为$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$。综合可得：

LEMMA 5.17. For a separated acyclic query $Q$ with a separated width-1 TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,an arbitrary instance $\mathcal{R}$ can be partitioned into $O\left( 1\right)$ sub-instances ${\mathcal{R}}_{1},{\mathcal{R}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{R}}_{h}$ within $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}}\right)$ time such that $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$ ,and each ${\mathcal{R}}_{i}$ satisfies Corollary 5.10 together with $\mathcal{T}$ .

引理5.17 对于具有分离宽度1树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的分离无环查询$Q$，任意实例$\mathcal{R}$可在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}}\right)$时间内被划分为$O\left( 1\right)$个子实例${\mathcal{R}}_{1},{\mathcal{R}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{R}}_{h}$，使得$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$，且每个${\mathcal{R}}_{i}$与$\mathcal{T}$共同满足推论5.10。

Combining Lemma 5.17 and Corollary 5.10, we complete the proof of Theorem 5.5.

结合引理5.17与推论5.10，我们完成了定理5.5的证明。

## 6 CYCLIC QUERIES

## 6 环状查询

In this section, we turn to cyclic queries. As mentioned, cyclic queries are usually tackled with tree decomposition techniques. Similarly, we can derive output-sensitive algorithms for cyclic queries by replacing the Yannakakis algorithm with our new output-optimal algorithm.

本节转向环状查询处理。如前所述，环状查询通常采用树分解技术解决。类似地，通过用我们新的输出最优算法替代Yannakakis算法，可推导出环状查询的输出敏感算法。

For an arbitrary query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and a $\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,we define another query ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathcal{T}} = (\mathcal{V},\{ \chi \left( u\right)$ : $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) \}$ ,y),i.e.,each node in the TD defines a relation. Given each instance $\mathcal{R}$ for $\mathcal{Q}$ ,we define another instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathcal{T}}$ for ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathcal{T}}$ as follows. For each node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ,we get a relation ${R}_{u} \mathrel{\text{:=}} { \bowtie  }_{e \in  \mathcal{E}}{\pi }_{\chi \left( u\right)  \cap  e}{R}_{e}$ as the result of the corresponding sub-query $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack$ . For each relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ , we assign it to one specific node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ such that $e \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$ . The annotations of tuples in ${R}_{e}$ are defined as follows: if no relation is assigned to $u$ ,every tuple $t$ has its annotation $w\left( t\right)  = 1$ ; otherwise,every tuple $t$ has its annotation $w\left( t\right)  = {\bigotimes }_{e}w\left( {{\pi }_{e}t}\right)$ over all relations $e$ assigned to $u$ . After getting the induced query ${Q}_{\mathcal{T}}$ and instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathcal{T}}$ ,we simply invoke our new algorithm for acyclic queries in Section 5 to compute the query result ${Q}_{\mathcal{T}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\mathcal{T}}\right)$ ,which is essentially $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ .

对于任意查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$和$\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，我们定义另一个查询${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathcal{T}} = (\mathcal{V},\{ \chi \left( u\right)$：$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) \}$，y)，即树分解(TD)中每个节点定义一个关系。给定$\mathcal{Q}$的每个实例$\mathcal{R}$，我们如下定义${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathcal{T}}$的另一个实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathcal{T}}$：对于每个节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$，通过执行对应子查询$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack$得到关系${R}_{u} \mathrel{\text{:=}} { \bowtie  }_{e \in  \mathcal{E}}{\pi }_{\chi \left( u\right)  \cap  e}{R}_{e}$；对于每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，将其分配给特定节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$以满足$e \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$。${R}_{e}$中元组的注释规则为：若未分配关系给$u$，则每个元组$t$的注释为$w\left( t\right)  = 1$；否则，每个元组$t$的注释是分配给$u$的所有关系$e$上$w\left( t\right)  = {\bigotimes }_{e}w\left( {{\pi }_{e}t}\right)$的聚合值。获得派生查询${Q}_{\mathcal{T}}$和实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathcal{T}}$后，我们直接调用第5章的无环查询新算法计算查询结果${Q}_{\mathcal{T}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\mathcal{T}}\right)$，其本质上是$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$。

Note that the input size of ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathcal{T}}$ is $O\left( {N}^{\text{width }\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right) }\right)$ ,and the output size is OUT. Plugging into Theorem 5.6,we obtain the time complexity as $O\left( {{N}^{\text{width }\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right) } \cdot  \max \left\{  {1,{\operatorname{OUT}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\text{fn-fhtw}\left( {Q}_{\mathcal{T}}\right) }}}\right\}   + \operatorname{OUT}}\right)$ . As the query size is constant,we can find within $O\left( 1\right)$ time the tree decomposition that minimizes the complexity formula above. Putting everything together, we obtain:

注意${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathcal{T}}$的输入规模为$O\left( {N}^{\text{width }\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right) }\right)$，输出规模为OUT。代入定理5.6可得时间复杂度为$O\left( {{N}^{\text{width }\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right) } \cdot  \max \left\{  {1,{\operatorname{OUT}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\text{fn-fhtw}\left( {Q}_{\mathcal{T}}\right) }}}\right\}   + \operatorname{OUT}}\right)$。由于查询规模恒定，我们能在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内找到使上述复杂度公式最小化的树分解。综合所有因素，我们得到：

THEOREM 6.1. For a query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT, the query result $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed in $O\left( {\mathop{\min }\limits_{\left( \mathcal{T},\chi \right) }{N}^{\text{width }\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right) } \cdot  \max \left\{  {1,{\operatorname{OUT}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\text{ fn-fitw }\left( {Q}_{\mathcal{T}}\right) }}}\right\}   + \operatorname{OUT}}\right)$ time,where $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is over all possible TDs of $\mathcal{Q}$ .

定理6.1 对于查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$及输入规模为$\mathcal{R}$、输出规模为OUT的实例，查询结果$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( {\mathop{\min }\limits_{\left( \mathcal{T},\chi \right) }{N}^{\text{width }\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right) } \cdot  \max \left\{  {1,{\operatorname{OUT}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\text{ fn-fitw }\left( {Q}_{\mathcal{T}}\right) }}}\right\}   + \operatorname{OUT}}\right)$时间内计算得出，其中$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$遍历$\mathcal{Q}$所有可能的树分解(TD)。

See Example 6.3. People have also exploited the power of hybrid TDs to speed up query processing in the literature. Inheriting the idea of [39], we first define the notion of TD-coverage as a set of TDs $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{h},{\chi }_{h}}\right)$ for $Q$ such that $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }{Q}_{{\mathcal{T}}_{i}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{\mathcal{T}}_{i}}\right)$ . Then,we can apply our new algorithm for acyclic queries to each $\operatorname{TD}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ and aggregate the results over all $\operatorname{TDs}$ .

参见示例6.3。现有研究已利用混合树分解(TD)来加速查询处理。基于文献[39]的思想，我们首先将TD覆盖定义为$Q$的一组树分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{h},{\chi }_{h}}\right)$，满足条件$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }{Q}_{{\mathcal{T}}_{i}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{\mathcal{T}}_{i}}\right)$。随后可对每个$\operatorname{TD}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$应用无环查询新算法，并聚合所有$\operatorname{TDs}$的结果。

THEOREM 6.2. For a query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT, the query result $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed in

定理6.2 对于查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$及输入规模为$\mathcal{R}$、输出规模为OUT的实例，查询结果$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在

$$
O\left( {\mathop{\min }\limits_{\left\{  \left( {\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}\right) ,\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\chi }_{2}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{n},{\chi }_{n}\right) \right\}  }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}{N}^{\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right) } \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{{\mathcal{T}}_{i}}\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) 
$$

time,where $\left\{  {\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{h},{\chi }_{h}}\right) }\right\}$ is over all possible TD-coverages of $Q$ .

时间内计算得出，其中$\left\{  {\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{h},{\chi }_{h}}\right) }\right\}$遍历$Q$所有可能的TD覆盖。

If we only consider the parameter width $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ ,the minimum TD -coverage can be identified, and the corresponding cost of is captured by the #submodular width [39]. However, we need to consider both width $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ and fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{{\mathcal{T}}_{i}}\right)$ to minimize the cost formula above. This question is interesting but very challenging, which we leave as future work.

若仅考虑参数宽度$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$，可确定最小TD覆盖，其对应代价由[39]提出的#次模宽度(#submodular width)描述。但需同时权衡宽度$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$与fn-fhtw$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{{\mathcal{T}}_{i}}\right)$以最小化上述代价公式。该问题极具挑战性，我们将留作未来研究方向。

Example 6.3. Consider a cyclic query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right.$ , $\left. {\left. {{A}_{3},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {\left\{  {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{A}_{3},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{B}_{2},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{3}}\right\}  }\right\}  }\right\}$ and $\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right\}$ . Figure 11 shows a $\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ for $Q : \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right\}$ , $\chi \left( {u}_{2}\right)  = \left\{  {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,\chi \left( {u}_{3}\right)  = \left\{  {{A}_{3},{B}_{2}}\right\}$ ,and $\chi \left( {u}_{4}\right)  = \left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3}}\right\}$ . Moreover, width $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \#$ subw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \frac{3}{2}$ and $\#$ fn-subw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathcal{T}}\right)  = 3$ . Our new algorithm can compute it in $O\left( {{N}^{\frac{3}{2}} \cdot  \max \left\{  {1,{\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{2}{3}}}\right\}   + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time, strictly improving the previous result $O\left( {N}^{3}\right)$ or $O\left( {{N}^{\frac{3}{2}} \cdot  \max \{ 1,\mathrm{{OUT}}\} }\right)$ .

例6.3. 考虑一个循环查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，其中$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right.$、$\left. {\left. {{A}_{3},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {\left\{  {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{A}_{3},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{B}_{2},{B}_{3}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{3}}\right\}  }\right\}  }\right\}$和$\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right\}$。图11展示了针对$Q : \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  = \left\{  {{A}_{1},{B}_{1}}\right\}$、$\chi \left( {u}_{2}\right)  = \left\{  {{A}_{2},{B}_{2}}\right\}  ,\chi \left( {u}_{3}\right)  = \left\{  {{A}_{3},{B}_{2}}\right\}$和$\chi \left( {u}_{4}\right)  = \left\{  {{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3}}\right\}$的$\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。此外，宽度$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \#$子宽$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \frac{3}{2}$与$\#$函数子宽$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$函数fhtw$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathcal{T}}\right)  = 3$。我们的新算法可在$O\left( {{N}^{\frac{3}{2}} \cdot  \max \left\{  {1,{\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{2}{3}}}\right\}   + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间内完成计算，显著改进了先前结果$O\left( {N}^{3}\right)$或$O\left( {{N}^{\frac{3}{2}} \cdot  \max \{ 1,\mathrm{{OUT}}\} }\right)$。
