## 7 LOWER BOUND

## 7 下界证明

In this section, we will show our lower bound for semi-ring algorithms computing acyclic join-aggregate queries. Recall that semiring algorithms work with semiring elements as an abstract type and can only copy them from existing semiring elements or combine them using $\oplus$ or $\otimes$ . No other operations on semi-ring elements are allowed, such as division, subtraction, or equality check.

本节将展示半环算法处理无环连接聚合查询的下界。需注意半环算法将半环元素作为抽象类型处理，仅能通过复制现有元素或使用$\oplus$与$\otimes$进行组合。不允许对半环元素执行其他操作，如除法、减法或相等性检验。

THEOREM 7.1. For an arbitrary self-join-free acyclic query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,given any parameters $1 \leq  N$ and OUT $\leq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime } \in  \mathfrak{R}\left( N\right) }}\left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|$ ,there exists an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT

定理7.1. 对于任意无自连接的无环查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，给定参数$1 \leq  N$与输出量OUT$\leq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{{\mathcal{R}}^{\prime } \in  \mathfrak{R}\left( N\right) }}\left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|$，总存在输入规模为$N$且输出规模为OUT的实例$\mathcal{R}$

such that any semi-ring algorithm computing $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ requires at least $\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time,where $\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ is the free-connex fractional hypertree width of $\mathcal{Q}$ ,and $\mathfrak{R}\left( N\right)$ is the set of all instances for $Q$ of input size $N$ .

使得任何计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的半环算法至少需要$\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间，其中$\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$表示$\mathcal{Q}$的自由连接分数超树宽度，$\mathfrak{R}\left( N\right)$是输入规模为$N$时$Q$的所有实例集合。

In [7],it has been shown that for the matrix query ${Q}_{\text{matrix }} = {\bigoplus }_{B}{R}_{1}\left( {A,B}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {B,C}\right)$ ,and parameters $1 \leq  N$ and OUT $\leq  {N}^{2}$ ,there exists an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT such that any semiring algorithm for computing ${Q}_{\text{matrix }}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ requires $\Omega \left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$ time. A similar argument has been extended to star queries:

文献[7]已证明：对于矩阵查询${Q}_{\text{matrix }} = {\bigoplus }_{B}{R}_{1}\left( {A,B}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {B,C}\right)$、参数$1 \leq  N$及输出量OUT$\leq  {N}^{2}$，存在输入规模为$N$且输出量为OUT的实例$\mathcal{R}$，使得任何计算${Q}_{\text{matrix }}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的半环算法都需要$\Omega \left( {N \cdot  \sqrt{\mathrm{{OUT}}}}\right)$时间。类似论证已被推广至星型查询：

$$
{Q}_{\text{star }} = {\bigoplus }_{B}{R}_{1}\left( {{A}_{1},B}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{A}_{2},B}\right)  \bowtie  \cdots  \bowtie  {R}_{k}\left( {{A}_{k},B}\right) 
$$

which will used to establish our new lower bound for general acyclic queries.

这将用于建立我们针对一般无环查询的新下界。

LEMMA 7.2. For ${Q}_{\text{star }}$ of $k$ relations,any parameters $1 \leq  N$ and OUT $\leq  {N}^{k}$ ,there exists an instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT such that any semiring algorithm for computing ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$ requires at least $\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$ time.

引理7.2. 对于包含$k$个关系的${Q}_{\text{star }}$，任意参数$1 \leq  N$和输出量OUT$\leq  {N}^{k}$，总存在输入规模为$N$且输出量为OUT的实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}$，使得任何计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$的半环算法至少需要$\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$时间。

Note that Lemma 7.2 has been proved for ${Q}_{\text{matrix }}$ when $k = 2$ by Pagh and Stockel [51]. Below, we simply generalized their proof to ${Q}_{\text{star }}$ .

注意当$k = 2$时，引理7.2已由Pagh和Stockel[51]针对${Q}_{\text{matrix }}$完成证明。下文我们将其证明过程推广至${Q}_{\text{star }}$。

Proof of Lemma 7.2. We construct a hard instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT for ${Q}_{\text{star }}$ as follows. There are ${\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1}{k}}$ distinct values in each attribute ${A}_{i}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . There are $\frac{N}{k \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUTT}}}^{1/k}}$ distinct values in attribute $B$ . Each relation ${R}_{i}$ is a Cartesian product between ${A}_{i}$ and $B$ . It can be checked that each relation contains exactly $\frac{N}{k}$ tuples,so the input size of $\mathcal{R}$ is exactly $N$ . The query result of ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$ is all combinations of values in attributes ${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{k}$ ,and the output size is exactly OUT. On this hard instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}$ ,for each query result $\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},\cdots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in$ $\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{2}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{k}\right)$ ,its annotation is defined as $\bigoplus \bigotimes w\left( {{a}_{i},b}\right)$ ,which takes $b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$

引理7.2的证明。我们构建一个输入规模为$N$、输出规模为OUT的困难实例<b0></b2>如下：每个属性${A}_{i}$中存在${\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1}{k}}$个不同取值，属性$B$中存在$\frac{N}{k \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUTT}}}^{1/k}}$个不同取值。每个关系${R}_{i}$是${A}_{i}$与$B$的笛卡尔积。可验证每个关系恰好包含$\frac{N}{k}$个元组，因此$\mathcal{R}$的输入规模精确等于$N$。查询${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$的结果是属性${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{k}$中所有值的组合，其输出规模精确等于OUT。在此困难实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}$上，每个查询结果$\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},\cdots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in$$\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{2}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{k}\right)$的注释定义为$\bigoplus \bigotimes w\left( {{a}_{i},b}\right)$，这需要消耗$b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$

at least $\left| {\operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) }\right|$ operations to compute $w\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{k},b}\right)  = {\bigotimes }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }w\left( {{a}_{i},b}\right)$ for each $b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)$ . Note that every pair of query results do not share any common operations.

至少$\left| {\operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) }\right|$次运算才能为每个$b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)$计算出$w\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},\cdots ,{w}_{k},b}\right)  = {\bigotimes }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }w\left( {{a}_{i},b}\right)$。注意任意两个查询结果之间不存在共享运算。

Similar to [51] we require the algorithm to work over fields of infinite size such as real numbers. We consider each output value as a polynomial over nonzero entries of the input matrices. Again, by the Schwartz-Zippel theorem [44], two polynomials agree on all inputs if and only if they are identical. Since we are working in the semiring model, the only way to get the annotation for the query result $\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},\cdots ,{a}_{k}}\right)$ in an output polynomial is to directly multiply these input entries. That means that to compute a query result $\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},\cdots ,{a}_{k}}\right)$ ,one needs to compute a polynomial that is identical to the sum of elementary products $\bigoplus \bigotimes w\left( {{a}_{i},b}\right)$ . Summing over all query $b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$

类似[51]的研究，我们要求算法在无限域（如实数域）上运行。将每个输出值视作输入矩阵非零项的多项式。根据Schwartz-Zippel定理[44]，两个多项式在所有输入上等价当且仅当它们完全相同。由于我们在半环模型中运算，在输出多项式里获取查询结果$\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},\cdots ,{a}_{k}}\right)$注释的唯一方式是直接相乘这些输入项。这意味着要计算查询结果$\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},\cdots ,{a}_{k}}\right)$，必须构造出与初等积和式$\bigoplus \bigotimes w\left( {{a}_{i},b}\right)$完全相同的多项式。对所有查询$b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$

results,the number of operations required is at least $N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}$ . Hence,any semiring algorithm requires at least $\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$ time for computing ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$ .

结果进行求和时，所需运算次数至少为$N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}$。因此，任何半环算法计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$至少需要$\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$时间。

LEMMA 7.3. For any 3-connected acyclic query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,there exists a subset $S \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$ of fn-fhtw(Q)attributes such that no pair of them appear in the same relation from $\mathcal{E}$ .

引理7.3. 对于任何3-连通无环查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，都存在一个fn-fhtw(Q)属性的子集$S \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$，使得其中任意两个属性不会同时出现在$\mathcal{E}$的同一关系中。

Proof of Lemma 7.3. Note that $q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack$ is also acyclic. Initially,we set $S = \varnothing$ . As shown in [29], the following greedy strategy leads to an optimal fractional edge covering for $q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack$ that is also integral. It iteratively performs the following two procedures: (i) removes a relation $e$ if there exists another relation ${e}^{\prime }$ such that $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ; (ii) if there exists a relation $e$ containing some unique attribute, we remove relation $e$ as well as all attributes in $e$ ,and add an arbitrary attribute in $e$ to $S$ . It can be easily checked that $\left| S\right|  =$ fn-fhtw,and no pair of them appears in the same relation from $\mathcal{E}$ .

引理7.3的证明. 注意到$q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack$也是无环的。初始时，我们设定$S = \varnothing$。如文献[29]所示，下述贪心策略可为$q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack$生成最优的分数边覆盖（该覆盖同时也是整数形式的）。该策略迭代执行以下两个步骤：(i) 当存在另一关系${e}^{\prime }$满足$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$时，移除关系$e$；(ii) 若存在包含唯一属性的关系$e$，则移除该关系及其所有属性，并任意选取$e$中的一个属性加入$S$。易验证$\left| S\right|  =$ fn-fhtw，且这些属性不会成对出现在$\mathcal{E}$的同一关系中。

Proof of Theorem 1.2. First, $\Omega \left( {N + \text{OUT}}\right)$ is a trivial lower bound. Hence,it suffices to show the lower bound $\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$ when $\mathrm{{OUT}} < {N}^{\mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }$ . We will prove it via three steps:

定理1.2的证明. 首先，$\Omega \left( {N + \text{OUT}}\right)$是一个平凡下界。因此，只需证明当$\mathrm{{OUT}} < {N}^{\mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }$时下界$\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$成立。我们将通过三个步骤进行证明：

Step 1. Consider any $\exists$ -connected and cleansed query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ . Suppose we are given parameters $1 \leq  N$ and OUT $\leq  {N}^{\text{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }$ . We show a reduction from $\mathcal{Q}$ to ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}$ with fn-fhtw(Q) relations. Implied by Lemma 7.3,let $S \subseteq  \mathrm{y}$ be the set of output attributes such that no pair appears in the same relation,and $\left| S\right|  =$ fn-fhtw(Q). Suppose we are given a hard instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}$ for ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}$ with fn-fhtw(Q)relations,which has input size $N$ and output size OUT. We next construct an instance $\mathcal{R}$ for $Q$ as follows.

步骤1. 考虑任意$\exists$-连通且净化后的查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$。假设给定参数$1 \leq  N$和输出OUT$\leq  {N}^{\text{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }$。我们展示从$\mathcal{Q}$到具有fn-fhtw(Q)个关系的${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}$的归约。根据引理7.3，设$S \subseteq  \mathrm{y}$为输出属性集，其中任意两个属性不出现在同一关系中，且$\left| S\right|  =$ fn-fhtw(Q)。假设给定针对${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}$的硬实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}$（具有fn-fhtw(Q)个关系），其输入规模为$N$且输出规模为OUT。接下来我们按如下方式构造$Q$的实例$\mathcal{R}$。

Each output attribute $A \in  \mathbf{y} - S$ contains one distinct value $\{  * \}$ . Each output attribute $A \in  S$ contains OUT ${}^{\frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}$ distinct values. Each non-output attribute $B \in  \left( {\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}\right)$ contains $\frac{N}{\left| \mathcal{E}\right|  \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}$ distinct values. For each relation ${R}_{e},\left| {e \cap  S}\right|  \leq  1$ . The projection of ${R}_{e}$ onto all non-output attributes contains tuples in a form of $\left( {{b}_{i},{b}_{i},\cdots ,{b}_{i}}\right)$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right\rbrack$ . The projection of ${R}_{e}$ onto all output attributes is the full Cartesian product. Moreover, ${R}_{e} = \left( {{\pi }_{\mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}}\right)  \times  \left( {{\pi }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}}\right)$ . For each output attribute $A \in  S$ ,we choose an arbitrary relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that $A \in  e$ . Note that all chosen relations are also distinct. Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{S}$ be the set of chosen relations. We specify an arbitrary one-to-one mapping from relations in ${Q}_{\text{star }}$ and relations in ${\mathcal{E}}_{S}$ ,say ${R}_{i}$ corresponds to ${S}_{i}$ . From our construction above,there is also a one-to-one mapping between tuples in ${R}_{i}$ and ${S}_{i}$ . We just set the annotation of a tuple $t \in  {S}_{i}$ as the same as ${t}^{\prime } \in  {R}_{i}$ ,if $t$ corresponds to ${t}^{\prime }$ . For every remaining relation ${R}_{e}$ in $\mathcal{R}$ , we simply set the annotation of each tuple as 1 . As $\left| {e \cap  S}\right|  \leq  1$ for each relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,it can be checked that each relation contains at most $N$ tuples. The input size of the constructed instance is $N$ ,and the output size is exactly OUT.

每个输出属性$A \in  \mathbf{y} - S$包含一个唯一值$\{  * \}$。每个输出属性$A \in  S$包含OUT${}^{\frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}$个不同值。每个非输出属性$B \in  \left( {\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}\right)$包含$\frac{N}{\left| \mathcal{E}\right|  \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}$个不同值。对于每个关系${R}_{e},\left| {e \cap  S}\right|  \leq  1$，将其${R}_{e}$投影到所有非输出属性上时，元组形式为$\left( {{b}_{i},{b}_{i},\cdots ,{b}_{i}}\right)$（其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right\rbrack$）。而投影到所有输出属性上则是完整的笛卡尔积。此外，${R}_{e} = \left( {{\pi }_{\mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}}\right)  \times  \left( {{\pi }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}}\right)$。针对每个输出属性$A \in  S$，我们任意选择一个满足$A \in  e$的关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$。注意所有被选关系也互不相同。设${\mathcal{E}}_{S}$为所选关系集合。我们建立${Q}_{\text{star }}$与${\mathcal{E}}_{S}$之间任意的一一映射，例如令${R}_{i}$对应${S}_{i}$。根据前述构造，${R}_{i}$与${S}_{i}$的元组间也存在一一映射。当$t$对应${t}^{\prime }$时，直接将元组$t \in  {S}_{i}$的注解设为与${t}^{\prime } \in  {R}_{i}$相同。对于$\mathcal{R}$中剩余的关系${R}_{e}$，将每个元组的注解设为1。由于每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$满足$\left| {e \cap  S}\right|  \leq  1$，可验证每个关系最多包含$N$个元组。构造实例的输入规模为$N$，输出规模恰好为OUT。

It is not hard to see that ${\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - S}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$ . Any semiring algorithm that compute $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ within $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn}\operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$ time,can also compute ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$ within $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn}\operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$ time. Hence, this automatically follows Lemma 7.2.

不难看出${\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - S}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = {\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$。任何能在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn}\operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$时间内计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的半环算法，同样能在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn}\operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$时间内计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{star }}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\text{star }}\right)$。因此，这直接遵循引理7.2。

Step 2. Consider any 3-connected but not cleansed query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ . Let ${Q}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },\mathbf{y}}\right)$ be the cleansed version of $Q$ ,and ${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$ be the hard instance for ${Q}^{\prime }$ as constructed above. Each non-output attribute $B \in  \mathcal{V} - {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }$ contains one distinct value $\{  * \}$ . For each ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ ,there must exist a relation $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ such that ${e}^{\prime } \subseteq  e$ . The relation ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$ is just a projection of ${R}_{e}$ onto attribute ${e}^{\prime }$ ,where each tuple has its annotation as 1 . It is not hard to see ${\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - {\mathbf{v}}^{\prime }}{\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{v}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ . Any semiring algorithm that can compute $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ within $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$ time,can also compute ${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$ within $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$ time. Hence,this automatically follows Step 1.

步骤二：考虑任意一个三连通但未净化的查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$。令${Q}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },\mathbf{y}}\right)$表示$Q$的净化版本，${\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }$为如上构建的${Q}^{\prime }$的困难实例。每个非输出属性$B \in  \mathcal{V} - {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }$包含一个独立值$\{  * \}$。对于每个${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$，必然存在一个关系$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$使得${e}^{\prime } \subseteq  e$。该关系${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$仅是${R}_{e}$在属性${e}^{\prime }$上的投影，其中每个元组的注释值为1。不难看出${\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - {\mathbf{v}}^{\prime }}{\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{v}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$。任何能在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$时间内计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的半环算法，同样能在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }}}\right)$时间内计算${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{R}}^{\prime }\right)$。因此，这自然承接步骤一的结论。

Step 3. Consider any 3-disconnected acyclic query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ . Let ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{Q}}_{h}$ be the $\exists$ - connected subqueries of $\mathcal{Q}$ . Wlog,assume fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)  =$ fn-fhtw(Q). Suppose we are given any parameters $1 \leq  N$ and OUT $\leq  {N}^{\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }$ . There exists an instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{1}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT such that any semiring algorithm computing ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)$ requires $\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time. We can extend the sub-instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{1}$ to an instance $\mathcal{R}$ to $Q$ as follows. Each attribute in $\mathcal{V} - {\mathcal{V}}_{1}$ contains one distinct value $\{  * \}$ . Consider an arbitrary relation $e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ . If $e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{1} = \varnothing$ , relation ${R}_{e}$ only contains one tuple in a form of $\left( {*,*,\cdots , * }\right)$ . Otherwise, $e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{1} \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$ . In this case, ${R}_{e} = \left( {{\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}{R}_{e}}\right)  \times  \left( {{\pi }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}{R}_{e}}\right)$ . Note that each tuple in ${R}_{e}$ has its annotation as 1 . It is not hard to see ${\bigoplus }_{{\mathcal{V}}_{1} - {\mathbf{y}}_{1}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ . Any semiring algorithm that can compute $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ within $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time,also computes ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)$ within $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time. Hence, this automatically follows Step 2.

步骤3. 考虑任意3-非连通无环查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$。令${\mathcal{Q}}_{1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{Q}}_{h}$作为$\mathcal{Q}$的$\exists$-连通子查询。不失一般性，假设函数嵌套分层树宽$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)  =$ ≤ 函数嵌套分层树宽(Q)。给定任意参数$1 \leq  N$和输出大小OUT$\leq  {N}^{\operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }$时，存在输入规模为$N$、输出规模为OUT的实例${\mathcal{R}}_{1}$，使得任何计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)$的半环算法都需要$\Omega \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间。我们可以将子实例${\mathcal{R}}_{1}$按如下方式扩展为$Q$的实例$\mathcal{R}$：$\mathcal{V} - {\mathcal{V}}_{1}$中的每个属性包含一个唯一值$\{  * \}$。考虑任意关系$e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$，若$e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{1} = \varnothing$，则该关系${R}_{e}$仅包含一个形如$\left( {*,*,\cdots , * }\right)$的元组；否则$e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{1} \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$。此时${R}_{e} = \left( {{\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}{R}_{e}}\right)  \times  \left( {{\pi }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}{R}_{e}}\right)$。注意${R}_{e}$中每个元组的标注均为1，易证${\bigoplus }_{{\mathcal{V}}_{1} - {\mathbf{y}}_{1}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$。任何能在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间内计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的半环算法，同样能在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间内计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)$。因此该结论可直接由步骤2推出。

## 8 RELATED WORK

## 8 相关工作

Efficient evaluation of join-aggregate queries. Olteanu and Závodný [49] investigate the problem of representing and computing results of conjunctive queries in a factorized form. They introduce the width ${s}^{ \uparrow  }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ ,which captures both the time complexity for computation and the size of factorized representations for conjunctive queries. They then establish a mapping between this notion of width and the free-connex fractional hypertree width. Subsequent work further explores the computation of aggregates over factorized databases [53]. In particular, aggregate functions such as SUM, PROD, MIN, and MAX can be evaluated on these factorized representations within $O\left( {N}^{{s}^{ \uparrow  }\left( Q\right) }\right)$ time. Later,Abo Khamis et al. [3] extended these results by generalizing the approach to arbitrary semirings. Specifically, they define the FAQ query that considers multiple semirings simultaneously,and show that it can be computed in $O\left( {{N}^{\operatorname{faqw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time,where $\operatorname{faqw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ denotes the FAQ-width of the query.

连接聚合查询的高效评估。Olteanu和Závodný [49]研究了以因子化形式表示和计算合取查询结果的问题。他们引入了宽度${s}^{ \uparrow  }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$这一概念，该概念同时捕捉了计算的时间复杂度与合取查询因子化表示的大小。随后，他们建立了这种宽度概念与自由连接分数超树宽度之间的映射关系。后续研究进一步探索了因子化数据库上的聚合计算[53]。特别是，SUM、PROD、MIN和MAX等聚合函数可以在$O\left( {N}^{{s}^{ \uparrow  }\left( Q\right) }\right)$时间内对这些因子化表示进行评估。后来，Abo Khamis等人[3]通过将该方法推广到任意半环，扩展了这些结果。具体而言，他们定义了同时考虑多个半环的FAQ查询，并证明其可在$O\left( {{N}^{\operatorname{faqw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间内完成计算，其中$\operatorname{faqw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$表示查询的FAQ宽度。

Extension of the Yannakakis Algorithm. Recent studies extend the Yannakakis algorithm to support a variety of operators and scenarios, including projections [9], unions [18], differences [32], comparisons [57], dynamic query processing [35, 58], massively parallel query processing [4, 33] and secure query processing $\left\lbrack  {{59},{60}}\right\rbrack$ . Moreover,several works focus on efficiently implementing the Yannakakis algorithm within practical data systems [13, 15, 62, 64].

Yannakakis算法的扩展。近期研究将该算法扩展至支持多种运算符和场景，包括投影[9]、并集[18]、差集[32]、比较[57]、动态查询处理[35,58]、大规模并行查询处理[4,33]以及安全查询处理$\left\lbrack  {{59},{60}}\right\rbrack$。此外，多项研究专注于在实际数据系统中高效实现Yannakakis算法[13,15,62,64]。

Fast matrix multiplication for conjunctive queries. As mentioned in Section 1, the semi-ring lower bound can be surpassed on specific semirings when certain conditions are met, as is the case for conjunctive queries defined over the Boolean ring. Recently, fast matrix multiplication has been widely adopted to accelerate the processing of conjunctive queries. Suppose that computing two rectangular matrices of size ${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$ and ${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$ can be done in $O\left( {n}^{\omega \left( {a,b,c}\right)  + o\left( 1\right) }\right)$ time. For simplicity,we use $\omega$ to denote $\omega \left( {1,1,1}\right)$ . There are some important constants related to rectangular matrix multiplication,such as, $\alpha  \leq  1$ defined as the largest constant such that $\omega \left( {1,\alpha ,1}\right)  = 2$ ,and $\mu$ is the (unique) solution to the equation $\omega \left( {\mu ,1,1}\right)  = {2\mu } + 1$ . Note that $\alpha  = 1$ if and only if $\omega  = 2$ , and the current best bounds on $\alpha$ are ${0.321334} < \alpha  \leq  1$ [61]. Moreover, $\mu  = \frac{1}{2}$ if $\omega  = 2$ ,and the current best bounds on $\mu$ are $\frac{1}{2} \leq  \mu  < {0.527661}$ [61].

合取查询的快速矩阵乘法。如第1节所述，在特定半环满足条件时（如定义在布尔环上的合取查询），可突破半环下界。近年来，快速矩阵乘法被广泛用于加速合取查询处理。假设尺寸为${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$和${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$的两个矩形矩阵乘法可在$O\left( {n}^{\omega \left( {a,b,c}\right)  + o\left( 1\right) }\right)$时间内完成。为简化表示，我们用$\omega$指代$\omega \left( {1,1,1}\right)$。矩形矩阵乘法涉及若干重要常数，例如$\alpha  \leq  1$定义为满足$\omega \left( {1,\alpha ,1}\right)  = 2$的最大常数，而$\mu$是方程$\omega \left( {\mu ,1,1}\right)  = {2\mu } + 1$的唯一解。注意$\alpha  = 1$当且仅当$\omega  = 2$，当前对$\alpha$的最佳边界是${0.321334} < \alpha  \leq  1$[61]。此外，若$\omega  = 2$则$\mu  = \frac{1}{2}$，当前对$\mu$的最佳边界为$\frac{1}{2} \leq  \mu  < {0.527661}$[61]。

Amossen and Pagh [7] first proposed an algorithm for the Boolean matrix multiplication by using fast matrix multiplication,which runs in $\widetilde{O}\left( {{N}^{\frac{2}{3}} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{2}{3}} + {N}^{\frac{\left( {2 - \alpha }\right) \omega  - 2}{\left( {1 + \omega }\right) \left( {1 - \alpha }\right) }} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{2 - {\alpha \omega }}{\left( {1 + \omega }\right) \left( {1 - \alpha }\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time when OUT $\geq  N$ ,and $\widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}}}\right)$ time when OUT $< N$ . Very recently,Abboud et al. [1] have completely improved this to $\widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{\mu }{1 + \mu }} + \mathrm{{OUT}} + {N}^{\frac{\left( {2 + \alpha }\right) \mu }{1 + \mu }} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1 - {\alpha \mu }}{1 + \mu }}}\right)$ . Improving these results further for any value of OUT remains a challenging task, given the hardness of the all-edge-triangle problem [1]. Several algorithms for Boolean matrix multiplication also measure complexity by the domain size of attributes; readers are referred to [1] for additional details. Deep et al. [24] and Huang and Chen [34] have further investigated the efficient practical implementation of these algorithms for sparse matrix multiplication.

Amossen与Pagh[7]首次提出利用快速矩阵乘法实现布尔矩阵乘法的算法，当输出$\widetilde{O}\left( {{N}^{\frac{2}{3}} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{2}{3}} + {N}^{\frac{\left( {2 - \alpha }\right) \omega  - 2}{\left( {1 + \omega }\right) \left( {1 - \alpha }\right) }} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{2 - {\alpha \omega }}{\left( {1 + \omega }\right) \left( {1 - \alpha }\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时时间复杂度为$\geq  N$，当输出$\widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}}}\right)$时则为$< N$。最近，Abboud等人[1]将其全面优化至$\widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{\mu }{1 + \mu }} + \mathrm{{OUT}} + {N}^{\frac{\left( {2 + \alpha }\right) \mu }{1 + \mu }} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1 - {\alpha \mu }}{1 + \mu }}}\right)$。鉴于全边三角问题的计算难度[1]，针对任意输出值进一步改进这些结果仍是极具挑战性的课题。部分布尔矩阵乘法算法还通过属性域大小衡量复杂度，详见文献[1]。Deep等人[24]与Huang和Chen[34]则深入研究了这些算法在稀疏矩阵乘法中的高效实践应用。

Finally, Hu [31] applied fast matrix multiplication to speed up acyclic join-project queries and showed a polynomial improvement over the combinatorial Yannakakis algorithm. Independently, the algorithm community has extensively utilized fast matrix multiplication to speed up detecting, counting,and listing subgraph patterns,such as cliques $\left\lbrack  {6,{22},{52}}\right\rbrack$ and cycles $\left\lbrack  {36}\right\rbrack$ . In addition,this technique has been used to approximately count cycles [19, 55], k-centering in graphs [37], etc.

最终，Hu[31]运用快速矩阵乘法加速无环连接-投影查询，相较组合式Yannakakis算法实现了多项式级提升。与此同时，算法学界广泛采用快速矩阵乘法来加速检测、计数及枚举子图模式（如团$\left\lbrack  {6,{22},{52}}\right\rbrack$和环$\left\lbrack  {36}\right\rbrack$）。此外，该技术还被用于近似计数环结构[19,55]、图中k中心问题[37]等。

## 9 CONCLUSION

## 9 结论

In this paper, we established matching lower and upper bounds for computing general acyclic join-aggregate queries in an output-optimal manner, characterized by the free-connex fractional hypertree width of the queries. This result generalizes and improves upon all previously known upper and lower bounds. As a by-product, it also implies new output-sensitive algorithms for certain cyclic queries, but their optimality is still unknown. Hence, achieving output-optimal algorithms for cyclic join-aggregate queries remains an interesting open question. In addition, it is interesting to investigate join-aggregate queries defined over multiple semi-rings [3] and to explore if one can apply our new output-optimal algorithm to improve processing multiple semi-rings.

本文通过查询的自由连接分数超树宽度特征，建立了以输出最优方式计算通用无环连接-聚合查询的匹配上下界。该结果概括并改进了所有已知上下界。作为副产品，它还为某些循环查询提供了新的输出敏感算法，但其最优性仍有待验证。因此，实现循环连接-聚合查询的输出最优算法仍是一个值得探索的开放性问题。此外，研究基于多重半环[3]定义的连接-聚合查询，以及探索如何应用我们新的输出最优算法来改进多重半环处理也颇具意义。

## A MISSING PROOFS IN SECTION 3

## A 第3节缺失证明

### A.1 Helper Lemmas

### A.1 辅助引理

LEMMA A.1. For any query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,any optimal fractional edge covering ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \cdot \right)$ of $Q$ satisfies:

引理A.1. 对于任意查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，其最优分数边覆盖${\rho }^{ * }\left( \cdot \right)$满足：

- If there exists a unique attribute $A \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,say ${\mathcal{E}}_{A} = \{ e\}$ ,then ${\rho }^{ * }\left( q\right)  = 1 + {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{V} - e}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

- 若存在唯一属性$A \in  \mathcal{V}$（记为${\mathcal{E}}_{A} = \{ e\}$），则${\rho }^{ * }\left( q\right)  = 1 + {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{V} - e}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。

- For any subsets ${S}_{1} \subseteq  {S}_{2} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ of attributes, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {S}_{1}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {S}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

- 对于属性的任意子集 ${S}_{1} \subseteq  {S}_{2} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$，${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {S}_{1}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {S}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。

LEMMA A.2. For any $\exists$ -connected query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and any $\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ ,there exists a node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ such that $\mathbf{y} = \chi \left( u\right)$ .

引理 A.2. 对于任意$\exists$连通查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$及任意$\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$，存在节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$使得$\mathbf{y} = \chi \left( u\right)$。

Proof of Lemma A.2. As $\mathcal{Q}$ is $\exists$ -connected, $e - \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ holds for every relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ; otherwise, $e$ becomes a singular $\exists$ -connected subquery of $\mathcal{Q}$ . Let $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ be an arbitrary free-connex TD of $\mathcal{Q}$ , with $S \subseteq  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ as the connex of $\mathcal{T}$ . Let ${\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{\ell }$ be the set of connected subtrees for some $\ell  \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$ ,after removing $S$ from $\mathcal{T}$ . For simplicity,we define ${\mathcal{E}}_{i} = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \exists u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) ,e \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right) }\right\}$ . It is easy to see that $\left( {{\mathcal{E}}_{1},{\mathcal{E}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{E}}_{\ell }}\right)$ forms a partition of $\mathcal{E}$ . On other hand, $\mathcal{E} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{E}}_{i}$ . On the other hand, ${\mathcal{E}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{j} = \varnothing$ for any $i,j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ with $i \neq  j$ . Suppose not,let $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{j}$ . As $e - \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ and ${e}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathrm{y}$ for every node ${e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right)$ ,the set of nodes containing non-output attributes in $e - \mathrm{y}$ does not form a connected subtree,contradicting the fact that $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is a valid TD. For any $i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ , relations in ${\mathcal{E}}_{i}$ must be $\exists$ -disconnected from remaining relations. As $Q$ is $\exists$ -connected,there must exist some $i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ with ${\mathcal{E}}_{i} = \mathcal{E}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{j} = \varnothing$ for any other $j$ . Let $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right)$ be the unique node sharing an edge with some node in ${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$ . As $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{{u}_{1} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right) }}\chi \left( u\right)  = \mathbf{y}$ ,and $\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \chi \left( {u}_{2}\right)  \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$ for ever pairs of nodes ${u}_{1} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right) ,{u}_{2} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) ,\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$ . Together with $\chi \left( u\right)  \subseteq  \mathbf{y},\mathbf{y} = \chi \left( u\right)$ .

引理A.2的证明。由于$\mathcal{Q}$是$\exists$-连通的，对于每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，$e - \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$都成立；否则，$e$将成为$\mathcal{Q}$的一个奇异$\exists$-连通子查询。设$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$为$\mathcal{Q}$的任意自由连通树分解（TD），其中$S \subseteq  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$作为$\mathcal{T}$的连通部分。设${\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{\ell }$为从$\mathcal{T}$中移除$S$后，某些$\ell  \in  {\mathbb{Z}}^{ + }$的连通子树集合。为简化起见，我们定义${\mathcal{E}}_{i} = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \exists u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) ,e \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right) }\right\}$。显然，$\left( {{\mathcal{E}}_{1},{\mathcal{E}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{E}}_{\ell }}\right)$构成了$\mathcal{E}$的一个划分。另一方面，$\mathcal{E} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{E}}_{i}$。此外，对于任何满足$i \neq  j$的$i,j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$，都有${\mathcal{E}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{j} = \varnothing$。假设不成立，令$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{j}$。由于对于每个节点${e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right)$，$e - \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$和${e}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathrm{y}$成立，包含$e - \mathrm{y}$中非输出属性的节点集合无法形成连通子树，这与$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$是有效TD的事实相矛盾。对于任何$i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$，${\mathcal{E}}_{i}$中的关系必须与其余关系$\exists$-断开。由于$Q$是$\exists$-连通的，必然存在某个$i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$满足${\mathcal{E}}_{i} = \mathcal{E}$，且对于任何其他$j$有${\mathcal{E}}_{j} = \varnothing$。设$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right)$为与${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$中某节点共享边的唯一节点。由于$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{{u}_{1} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right) }}\chi \left( u\right)  = \mathbf{y}$，且对于每对节点${u}_{1} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( S\right) ,{u}_{2} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) ,\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$都有$\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \chi \left( {u}_{2}\right)  \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$。结合$\chi \left( u\right)  \subseteq  \mathbf{y},\mathbf{y} = \chi \left( u\right)$。

Proof of Lemma 3.2. Direction fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ . It suffices to prove that for each TD $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ ,we can construct a $\mathrm{{TD}}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ with the same width. Consider the post-ordering of relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{\ell }$ removed by the cleanse process. Suppose ${e}_{1}$ is removed due to some relation $e$ . Let $u \in$ nodes $\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$ be a node such that $e \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$ . We add a new node ${u}_{1}$ as a child of $u$ and set $\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  = {e}_{1}$ . Note that $\left( {{e}_{1} - e}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$ . We move to the next relation and apply this procedure recursively. As ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{i}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  = 1$ for each node ${u}_{i} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  - \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$ , width $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  = \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ . Let ${S}^{\prime }$ be the connex of ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ . It can be easily checked that ${S}^{\prime }$ is also a connex of $\mathcal{T}$ . Hence, $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

引理3.2的证明。方向函数fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$。只需证明对于每个树分解TD $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$，我们都能构造一个具有相同宽度的$\mathrm{{TD}}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。考虑被清理过程移除的关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{\ell }$的后序遍历。假设${e}_{1}$由于某个关系$e$而被移除。令$u \in$节点$\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$为满足$e \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$的节点。我们添加一个新节点${u}_{1}$作为$u$的子节点，并设置$\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  = {e}_{1}$。注意$\left( {{e}_{1} - e}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$。我们移至下一个关系并递归应用此过程。由于对于每个节点${u}_{i} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  - \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }\right)$都有${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{i}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  = 1$，故宽度$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  = \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。设${S}^{\prime }$为${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$的连通集。容易验证${S}^{\prime }$也是$\mathcal{T}$的连通集。因此$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

Direction fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ . It suffices to prove that for each $\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ ,we can construct a $\operatorname{TD}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ with smaller (or at least no larger) width. For simplicity, assume $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and ${Q}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },\mathbf{y}}\right)$ . Let $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ and ${q}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right)$ . Implied by the cleanse procedure, ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \left\{  {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } : e \in  \mathcal{E}}\right\}$ . Moreover, $\left( {\mathcal{V} - {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$ . We construct another function ${\chi }^{\prime } : \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }}$ as follows. For each node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) ,{\chi }^{\prime }\left( u\right)  = \chi \left( u\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }$ . It can be easily checked that $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ . Moreover,for each node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) ,{\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  =$ ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {{q}^{\prime }\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  \geq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {{q}^{\prime }\left\lbrack  {{\chi }^{\prime }\left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$ ,where the first inequality follows that one can always assign weight 0 to all relations in $\mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ without losing the optimality. Hence,width $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \geq  \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$ .

方向函数fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$。只需证明对于每个$\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$，我们都能构造一个宽度更小（或至少不更大）的$\operatorname{TD}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$。为简化起见，假设$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$且${Q}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },\mathbf{y}}\right)$。令$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$和${q}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right)$。根据清洗过程可知，${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \left\{  {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } : e \in  \mathcal{E}}\right\}$。此外，$\left( {\mathcal{V} - {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$。我们按如下方式构造另一个函数${\chi }^{\prime } : \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }}$：对于每个节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) ,{\chi }^{\prime }\left( u\right)  = \chi \left( u\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }$，可以轻易验证$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$。再者，对于每个节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) ,{\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  =$${\rho }^{ * }\left( {{q}^{\prime }\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  \geq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {{q}^{\prime }\left\lbrack  {{\chi }^{\prime }\left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$，其中第一个不等式成立的原因是总能为$\mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$中所有关系分配权重0而不损失最优性。因此，宽度$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \geq  \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$。

LEMMA A.3. For any query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,there exists a $\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ with the smallest width while satisfying the following property: each unique attribute $A \in  \mathcal{V}$ only appears in one bag of $\mathcal{T}$ .

引理A.3。对于任意查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，存在一个满足以下性质且具有最小宽度的$\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$：每个唯一属性$A \in  \mathcal{V}$仅出现在$\mathcal{T}$的一个包中。

Proof of Lemma A.3. Let $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ be an arbitrary TD of $Q$ with the smallest width. We perform the following step if a unique attribute $A \in  \mathcal{V}$ appears in more than one bag of $\mathcal{T}$ . Consider an arbitrary node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ with $A \in  \chi \left( u\right)$ . We construct another mapping ${\chi }^{\prime } : \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$ as follows: ${\chi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  = \chi \left( v\right)  - \{ A\}$ if $v \neq  u$ and ${\chi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  = \chi \left( v\right)$ otherwise. It can be easily checked that $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$ is a valid TD if $\mathcal{Q}$ with width $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ . We iteratively apply this argument until each unique attribute only appears in one bag of $\mathcal{T}$ .

引理A.3的证明。设$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$为$Q$的任意最小宽度的树分解。若唯一属性$A \in  \mathcal{V}$在$\mathcal{T}$的多个包中出现，则执行以下步骤：任选一个包含$A \in  \chi \left( u\right)$的节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$，构造新映射${\chi }^{\prime } : \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$：当$v \neq  u$时令${\chi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  = \chi \left( v\right)  - \{ A\}$，否则保持原值。易证当$\mathcal{Q}$时，$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$构成有效树分解且宽度为$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。重复此操作直至每个唯一属性仅出现在$\mathcal{T}$的一个包中。

### A.2 Proof of Corollary 3.5

### A.2 推论3.5的证明

LEMMA A.4. For an acyclic query $Q$ ,if $Q$ is $\exists$ -disconnected with $\exists$ -connected subqueries ${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\ldots ,{Q}_{h}$ , fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$ .

引理A.4。对于无环查询$Q$，若$Q$是$\exists$不连通且具有$\exists$连通子查询${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\ldots ,{Q}_{h}$，则fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}$ fn-fhtw$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$。

Proof of Lemma A.4. Let $\left( {{\mathcal{E}}_{1},{\mathcal{E}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{E}}_{h}}\right)$ be the partition of $\mathcal{E}$ defined by the $\exists$ -connectivity of $Q$ . For each $i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  ,{Q}_{i} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i},{\mathcal{E}}_{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{i}}\right)$ with ${\mathcal{V}}_{i} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}e$ and ${\mathbf{y}}_{i} = \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{i}$ . We first note an equivalent expression of that right-hand-side of the equation above:

引理A.4的证明。设$\left( {{\mathcal{E}}_{1},{\mathcal{E}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{E}}_{h}}\right)$为由$Q$的$\exists$连通性定义的$\mathcal{E}$划分。对于每个满足${\mathcal{V}}_{i} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}e$和${\mathbf{y}}_{i} = \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{i}$的$i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  ,{Q}_{i} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i},{\mathcal{E}}_{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{i}}\right)$，我们首先给出上述等式右侧的等价表达式：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right) }}\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{\substack{{\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{h},{\chi }_{h}}\right)  \in  \epsilon } \\  {\operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{h}\right) } }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)  \tag{2}
$$

Direction (1) $\leq  \left( 2\right)$ . Consider an arbitrary combination $\left\{  {\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{h},{\chi }_{h}}\right) }\right\}   \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times$ FTD $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{h}\right)$ . We next construct a TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ for $\mathcal{Q}$ . From Lemma A.2,there exists a node ${u}_{i} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$ with ${\mathbf{y}}_{i} = \chi \left( {u}_{i}\right)$ . We root ${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$ at node ${u}_{i}$ . We next build a width-1 TD $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$ for the sub-query ${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime } =$ $\left( {\mathbf{y},\left\{  {{\mathbf{y}}_{i} : i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }\right\}  }\right)$ that there is a one-to-one correspondence between bags in ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ and relations in $\left\{  {\mathbf{y}}_{i}\right.$ : $i \in  \lbrack h\rbrack \}$ . For each $i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack$ ,let $u \in$ nodes $\left( {T}^{\prime }\right)$ be the node with $\chi \left( u\right)  = {\mathbf{y}}_{i}$ . We add ${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$ as a child of $u$ . It can be checked that width $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \max \left\{  {\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right) ,\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right) }\right\}   = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ .

方向(1) $\leq  \left( 2\right)$。考虑任意组合$\left\{  {\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{h},{\chi }_{h}}\right) }\right\}   \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times$ FTD（全树分解）$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{h}\right)$。接下来我们为$\mathcal{Q}$构造一个树分解(TD)$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。根据引理A.2，存在一个节点${u}_{i} \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$满足${\mathbf{y}}_{i} = \chi \left( {u}_{i}\right)$。我们将${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$的根节点设为${u}_{i}$。接着为子查询${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime } =$$\left( {\mathbf{y},\left\{  {{\mathbf{y}}_{i} : i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }\right\}  }\right)$构建宽度为1的树分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$，使得${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$中的包与$\left\{  {\mathbf{y}}_{i}\right.$中的关系存在一一对应：$i \in  \lbrack h\rbrack \}$。对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack$，令$u \in$节点$\left( {T}^{\prime }\right)$成为具有$\chi \left( u\right)  = {\mathbf{y}}_{i}$特性的节点。我们将${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$添加为$u$的子节点。可以验证该分解的宽度为$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \max \left\{  {\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right) ,\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right) }\right\}   = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{width}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$。

Direction (1) $\geq$ (2). Consider an arbitrary TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ . For each $i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack$ ,we construct a mapping ${\chi }_{i} :$ nodes $\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{{\mathcal{V}}_{i}}$ as follows. For each node $t \in$ nodes(T),we set ${\chi }_{i}\left( t\right)  = \chi \left( u\right)  \cap$ ${\mathcal{V}}_{i}.\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ is a valid TD for ${Q}_{i}$ . Moreover,width $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{i}}\right)  \leq$ width $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ . For the combination $\left\{  {\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{h}}\right) }\right\}$ ,width $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \geq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ . Hence, $\left( 1\right)  \geq  \left( 2\right)$ follows.

方向 (1) $\geq$ (2). 考虑任意树分解(TD) $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack$，我们按如下方式构建映射${\chi }_{i} :$节点$\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{{\mathcal{V}}_{i}}$：对树分解(T)中的每个节点$t \in$，设定${\chi }_{i}\left( t\right)  = \chi \left( u\right)  \cap$${\mathcal{V}}_{i}.\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$构成${Q}_{i}$的有效树分解。此外，宽度$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{i}}\right)  \leq$不超过宽度$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。对于组合$\left\{  {\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{h}}\right) }\right\}$，其宽度$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \geq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  h\right\rbrack  }}\operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$。因此，$\left( 1\right)  \geq  \left( 2\right)$得证。

LEMMA A.5. For an acyclic query $Q$ ,if $Q$ is $\exists$ -connected, $\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ ,i.e.,the fractional edge covering number of the sub-query induced by output attributes.

引理 A.5. 对于无环查询$Q$，若$Q$是$\exists$-连通的，则$\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$（即由输出属性导出的子查询的分数边覆盖数）。

Proof of Lemma A.5. From Lemma 3.2,if $\mathcal{Q}$ is not cleansed,we have fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ , where ${Q}^{\prime }$ is the cleansed version of $Q$ . Then,it suffices to show that for any a 3-connected and cleansed query $Q$ ,we have $\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

引理A.5的证明. 由引理3.2可知，若$\mathcal{Q}$未净化，则fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$不小于fn-fhtw$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$，其中${Q}^{\prime }$是$Q$的净化版本。因此只需证明：对于任何3-连通且净化后的查询$Q$，都有$\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$成立。

Direction fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . Consider an arbitrary TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ . By Lemma A.2, there is a node $u \in$ nodes(T)with $\mathbf{y} = \chi \left( u\right)$ . Then, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ . Applying this argument to all possible TDs in FTD(Q),we have ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

方向函数fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。考虑任意树分解TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。根据引理A.2，存在节点$u \in$ ∈ nodes(T)满足$\mathbf{y} = \chi \left( u\right)$。于是，${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。将此论证应用于FTD(Q)中所有可能的TD，可得${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

Direction fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . It suffices to identify a $\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ with width $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  =$ ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . Let $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ be a width-1 TD of $Q$ ,where each unique (output) attribute only appears in one bag of $\mathcal{T}$ . As $Q$ is cleansed,a unique output attribute $A \in  \mathbf{y}$ must exist and only appear in one bag,say $u$ . We root $\mathcal{T}$ at node $u$ ,and construct another mapping ${\chi }^{\prime } :$ nodes $\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$ as follows. Consider an arbitrary node ${u}_{1} \in$ nodes(T). Let ${\mathcal{T}}_{{u}_{1}}$ the subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at ${u}_{1}$ . We set ${\chi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{1}\right)  = \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathbf{y} \cap  \bigcup \chi \left( {u}_{2}\right) }\right)$ ,i.e.,augmenting ${u}_{1}$ with all output attributes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{{u}_{1}}$ .

方向函数fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。只需构造一个宽度为$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  =$的$\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。令$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$为$Q$的宽度-1树分解，其中每个唯一（输出）属性仅出现在$\mathcal{T}$的一个包中。由于$Q$是清洁的，必然存在唯一输出属性$A \in  \mathbf{y}$且仅出现在某个包（设为$u$）中。我们将$\mathcal{T}$的根节点设为$u$，并构建另一个映射${\chi }^{\prime } :$：nodes$\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$如下：任取节点${u}_{1} \in$ ∈ nodes(T)，令${\mathcal{T}}_{{u}_{1}}$表示以${u}_{1}$为根的$\mathcal{T}$子树。定义${\chi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{1}\right)  = \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathbf{y} \cap  \bigcup \chi \left( {u}_{2}\right) }\right)$，即将${u}_{1}$增强为包含${\mathcal{T}}_{{u}_{1}}$中所有输出属性。

We next show fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ by distinguishing two more cases:

接下来通过区分两种情形证明fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$：

- If ${u}_{1}$ is assigned with weight 1 by some optimal fractional edge covering of $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$ ,we have

- 若${u}_{1}$被$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$的某个最优分数边覆盖赋予权重1，则有

$$
{\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  = 1 + {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {{\chi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{1}\right)  - \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  1 + {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\mathbf{y} - \chi \left( u\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right) 
$$

where the inequality is implied by ${\chi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{1}\right)  - \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \subseteq  \mathbf{y} - \chi \left( u\right)$ . Suppose not,let $A \in  \left( {{\chi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{1}\right)  - \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right)  -$ $\left( {\mathbf{y} - \chi \left( u\right) }\right)$ be an arbitrary attribute. Note that $A \in  \chi \left( u\right) ,A \in  \chi \left( {u}_{2}\right)$ for some node ${u}_{2} \in$ nodes $\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{{u}_{1}}\right)$ ,but $A \notin  \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)$ . As ${u}_{1}$ lies on the path between $t$ and ${u}_{2},\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is not a valid TD for $Q$ ,coming to a contradiction.

该不等式由${\chi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{1}\right)  - \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \subseteq  \mathbf{y} - \chi \left( u\right)$隐含。假设不成立，令$A \in  \left( {{\chi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{1}\right)  - \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right)  -$$\left( {\mathbf{y} - \chi \left( u\right) }\right)$为任意属性。注意到对于某些节点${u}_{2} \in$存在$A \in  \chi \left( u\right) ,A \in  \chi \left( {u}_{2}\right)$，但$A \notin  \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)$。由于${u}_{1}$位于$t$和${u}_{2},\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$之间的路径上，这不符合$Q$的有效TD定义，导致矛盾。

- Otherwise, ${u}_{1}$ is not assigned with weight 1 by any optimal fractional edge covering of $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$ . We next prove ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . Suppose not,as $\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} \subseteq  \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)$ ,we must have ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)  < {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . As both $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack$ and $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$ are acyclic, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ and ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$ are integers. Meanwhile,we observe that any fractional edge covering for $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathrm{y}}\right\rbrack$ together with assigning weight 1 to relation ${e}_{1}$ is a valid fractional edge covering for $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$ ,hence ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)  + 1$ . Then,the optimal fractional edge covering for $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack$ together with assigning weight 1 to relation ${e}_{1}$ is also an optimal fractional edge covering for $q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$ ,coming to a contradiction. Hence,we have:

- 否则，${u}_{1}$不会被任何$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$的最优分数边覆盖赋予权重1。接下来我们证明${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$。假设不成立，由于$\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} \subseteq  \chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)$，必然存在${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)  < {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$。由于$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack$和$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$都是无环的，${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)$和${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$为整数。同时我们观察到，任何对$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathrm{y}}\right\rbrack$的分数边覆盖方案，若给关系${e}_{1}$赋予权重1，都将构成$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$的有效分数边覆盖，因此${\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)  + 1$。于是，$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack$的最优分数边覆盖方案结合对关系${e}_{1}$赋予权重1的操作，也将成为$q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack$的最优分数边覆盖，导致矛盾。由此可得：

$$
{\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  {\chi \left( {u}_{1}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right) .
$$

Applying this argument to every node in $\mathcal{T}$ ,we obtain fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

将此论证应用于$\mathcal{T}$中的每个节点，我们得到fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。

### A.3 Free-connex Submodular Width and #Free-connex Submodular Width

### A.3 自由连接子模宽度与#自由连接子模宽度

Definition A. 6 (The set of polymatroids). Given a hypergraph $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,a function $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ is called a polymatroid if it satisfies the following properties:

定义A.6（多拟阵集）. 给定超图$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，若函数$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$满足以下性质，则称为多拟阵：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{Y}\right)  \geq  h\left( {\mathbf{X} \cup  \mathbf{Y}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{Y}}\right) \;\forall \mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V},\;\text{ (submodularity) } \tag{3}
$$

$$
h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  h\left( \mathbf{Y}\right) \;\forall \mathbf{X} \subseteq  \mathbf{Y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}\;\text{ (monotonicity) } \tag{4}
$$

$$
h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0\;\text{(strictness)} \tag{5}
$$

The above properties are also known as Shannon inequalities. We use ${\Gamma }_{\mathcal{V}}$ to denote the set of all polymatroids over $\mathcal{V}$ . When $\mathcal{V}$ is clear from the context,we drop $\mathcal{V}$ and write $\Gamma$ .

上述性质亦称为香农不等式(Shannon inequalities)。我们用${\Gamma }_{\mathcal{V}}$表示定义在$\mathcal{V}$上的所有多拟阵(polymatroids)集合。当上下文明确时，省略$\mathcal{V}$直接记作$\Gamma$。

Definition A. 7 (The set of #polymatroids). Given a hypergraph $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,a function $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow$ ${\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ is called a #polymatroid if it satisfies the following properties:

定义A.7（#多拟阵集合）。给定超图$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，若函数$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow$${\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$满足下列性质，则称之为#多拟阵(#polymatroid)：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{Y}\right)  \geq  h\left( {\mathbf{X} \cup  \mathbf{Y}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{Y}}\right) \;\forall \mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V},\exists e \in  \mathcal{E},\mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{Y} = e\;\text{ (submodularity) }\;\left( 6\right)  \tag{6}
$$

$$
h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  h\left( \mathbf{Y}\right) \;\forall \mathbf{X} \subseteq  \mathbf{Y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}\;\text{ (monotonicity) } \tag{7}
$$

$$
h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0\;\text{(strictness)} \tag{8}
$$

We use ${\Gamma }_{\mathcal{V}}^{ * }$ to denote the set of all #polymatroids over $\mathcal{V}$ . When $\mathcal{V}$ is clear from the context,we drop $\mathcal{V}$ and write ${\Gamma }^{\# }$ instead of ${\Gamma }_{\mathcal{V}}^{\# }$ .

我们使用${\Gamma }_{\mathcal{V}}^{ * }$表示在$\mathcal{V}$上所有#多拟阵的集合。当上下文已明确$\mathcal{V}$时，省略$\mathcal{V}$并记作${\Gamma }^{\# }$替代${\Gamma }_{\mathcal{V}}^{\# }$。

Definition A. 8 (The set of edge-dominated functions). Given a hypergraph $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,a function $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ is called edge-dominated if it satisfies the following property: $h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  1,\forall \mathbf{X} \in  \mathcal{E}$ . We use ${\mathrm{{ED}}}_{q}$ to denote the set of all edge-dominated functions over $q$ . When $q$ is clear from the context, we drop $q$ and simply write ED.

定义A.8（边支配函数集）。给定超图$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，若函数$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$满足以下性质：$h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  1,\forall \mathbf{X} \in  \mathcal{E}$，则称其为边支配函数。我们用${\mathrm{{ED}}}_{q}$表示$q$上所有边支配函数的集合。当上下文明确$q$时，省略$q$简记为ED。

Definition A. 9 (Free-connex Submodular Width). For any query $Q$ ,its free-connex submodular width of $Q$ denoted as fn-subw(Q),is defined as:

定义A.9（自由连接子模宽度）。对于任意查询$Q$，其自由连接子模宽度记作fn-subw(Q)，定义为：

$$
\text{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}h\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right)  \tag{9}
$$

Definition A. 10 (#Free-connex Submodular Width). For any query $Q$ ,its free-connex submodular width of $Q$ denoted as fn-subw(Q),is defined as:

定义A.10（#自由连接子模宽度）。对于任意查询$Q$，其自由连接子模宽度记作fn-subw(Q)，定义为：

$$
\# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathbf{h} \in  {\Gamma }^{\# } \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}h\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right)  \tag{10}
$$

LEMMA A.11. Given any query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,for a subset $S \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ of attributes,

引理A.11。给定任意查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，对于属性子集$S \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$，

$$
{\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  {ED}}}h\left( S\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathbf{h} \in  {\Gamma }^{\# } \cap  {ED}}}h\left( S\right) .
$$

Proof of Lemma 3.9. It suffices to show that for any query $Q$ , $\# \mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)$ . For

引理3.9的证明。只需证明对任意查询$Q$，$\# \mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{{fn}} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right)$成立。对于

fn-fhtw(Q),we first rewrite (1) as below:

fn-fhtw(Q)，我们首先将(1)式改写如下：

$$
\text{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}\operatorname{width}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right) 
$$

By comparing the above and (10), it immediately follows from the min-max inequality that $\# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

通过比较上述式与(10)式，根据最小最大不等式可直接推出$\# \operatorname{fn-subw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。
