### A.4 Proof of Lemma 3.10

### A.4 引理3.10的证明

Implied by Lemma 3.9,it suffices to show that for any acyclic query $\mathcal{Q}$ ,fn-subw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ holds. Recall the procedural definition of fn-fhtw(Q)in Corollary 3.5. We prove it via two steps:

由引理3.9可知，只需证明对任意无环查询$\mathcal{Q}$，fn-subw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$成立。回顾推论3.5中fn-fhtw(Q)的过程定义，我们分两步证明：

Step 1: $Q$ is $\exists$ -connected. In this case,we will identify a polymatroid $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ such that for any $\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ ,there always exists a node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ with $h\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . Note that fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . From Lemma 7.3,let $S \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$ be a set of fn-fhtw(Q)output attributes such that no pairs of them appears in the same relation from $\mathcal{E}$ . We identify a modular function $h$ as follows. Let each attribute $A \in  S$ represent an independently random variable with $h\left( A\right)  = 1$ . Let each remaining attribute $B \in  \mathcal{V} - S$ represent a constant with $h\left( B\right)  = 0$ . It can be easily checked that $h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ . Consider an arbitrary $\mathrm{{TD}}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ . From Lemma A.2,there must exist a node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ such that $\chi \left( u\right)  = \mathbf{y}$ . Hence, $h\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{A \in  S}}h\left( A\right)  = \left| S\right|  = \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

步骤1：$Q$是$\exists$连通的。在此情况下，我们将识别一个拟阵$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$，使得对于任意$\operatorname{TD}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{FTD}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$，总存在一个节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$满足$h\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。注意函数嵌套-超树宽（fn-fhtw）$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。根据引理7.3，设$S \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$为函数嵌套-超树宽(Q)输出属性集合，且其中任意两个属性不会同时出现在$\mathcal{E}$的同一关系中。我们按如下方式定义模函数$h$：令每个属性$A \in  S$表示具有$h\left( A\right)  = 1$的独立随机变量，其余属性$B \in  \mathcal{V} - S$表示具有$h\left( B\right)  = 0$的常量。可轻易验证$h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$。考虑任意$\mathrm{{TD}}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathrm{{FTD}}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$，根据引理A.2，必存在节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$使得$\chi \left( u\right)  = \mathbf{y}$，因此$h\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{A \in  S}}h\left( A\right)  = \left| S\right|  = \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。

Step 2: $Q$ is $\exists$ -disconnected. Let ${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\cdots ,{Q}_{h}$ be the $\exists$ -connected subqueries of $Q$ . Wlog, assume fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$ fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)$ . Let ${\mathcal{Q}}_{i} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i},{\mathcal{E}}_{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{i}}\right)$ where ${\mathcal{V}}_{i} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}e$ and ${\mathbf{y}}_{i} = {\mathcal{V}}_{i} \cap  y$ . Similarly,let $S \subseteq  {\mathbf{y}}_{1}$ be a set of fn-fhtw $\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)$ output attributes such that no pair of them appears in the same relation from ${\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ . We next argue that no pair appears in the same relation from $\mathcal{E}$ . Suppose not,assume $A,{A}^{\prime } \in  S$ are two output attributes that appear together in some relation $e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ . In ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}$ ,let ${A}_{0}\left( { = A}\right) ,{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell },{A}_{\ell  + 1}\left( { = {A}^{\prime }}\right)$ be a sequence of attributes such that (1) ${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell } \in  \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}$ ; (2) there exists $\ell  + 1$ relations ${e}_{0},{e}_{1},\cdots ,{e}_{\ell } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ such that for each $i \in  \{ 0,1,\cdots ,\ell \}$ ,we have ${A}_{i},{A}_{i + 1} \in  {e}_{i}$ ; (3) for every pair of non-consecutive attributes ${A}_{i},{A}_{j}$ for some $i,j$ with $\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$ ,there exists no relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ containing both ${A}_{i},{A}_{j}$ . Note that for any other relation $e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{1},e \cap  \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell }}\right\}   = \varnothing$ ,which is implied by the definition of $\exists$ -connectivity. Hence,(3) is equivalent to for every pair of non-consecutive attributes ${A}_{i},{A}_{j}$ for some $I,j$ with $\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$ ,there exists no relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ containing both ${A}_{i},{A}_{j}$ . If $\ell  \geq  1$ , we have identified a cycle ${A}_{0}\left( { = A}\right) ,{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell },{A}_{\ell  + 1}\left( { = {A}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,contradicting the fact that $Q$ is acyclic. Hence, $\ell  < 1$ ,which means ${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell }$ do not exist; in other words, $A,{A}^{\prime }$ appear in some common relation from ${\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ . As $Q$ is acyclic,there must exist a relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that $A,{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell },{A}^{\prime } \in  {e}^{\prime }$ ; otherwise,we have identified a cycle,contradicting the fact that $Q$ is not acyclic. As ${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell } \in  {e}^{\prime },{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ . This comes to a contradiction that $A,{A}^{\prime }$ does not appear in the same relation of ${\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ . Again,let each attribute $A \in  S$ represent an independent random variable with $h\left( A\right)  = 1$ . Let each remaining attribute $B \in  \mathcal{V} - S$ represent a constant with $h\left( B\right)  = 0$ . It can be easily checked that $h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ . We claim that a node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ must exist with ${\mathbf{y}}_{1} \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$ . Consider a TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$ with ${\chi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  = \chi \left( v\right)  - \left( {\mathbf{y} - {\mathbf{y}}_{1}}\right)$ for each node $v \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ . Note that $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$ is also a valid free-connex TD for ${Q}_{1}$ ; hence there must exist some node $u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ with ${\chi }^{\prime }\left( u\right)  = {\mathbf{y}}_{1}$ . Correspondingly, ${\mathbf{y}}_{1} \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$ . Hence, $h\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{A \in  S}}h\left( A\right)  = \left| S\right|  = \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

步骤2：$Q$是$\exists$非连通的。令${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\cdots ,{Q}_{h}$表示$Q$的$\exists$连通子查询。不失一般性，假设函数-超树宽$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$≤函数-超树宽$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)$。设${\mathcal{Q}}_{i} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i},{\mathcal{E}}_{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{i}}\right)$，其中${\mathcal{V}}_{i} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}e$且${\mathbf{y}}_{i} = {\mathcal{V}}_{i} \cap  y$。类似地，令$S \subseteq  {\mathbf{y}}_{1}$为一组函数-超树宽$\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\right)$输出属性，且这些属性在${\mathcal{E}}_{1}$中不存在于同一关系内。接下来论证这些属性在$\mathcal{E}$中也不会共现于同一关系。假设不成立，设$A,{A}^{\prime } \in  S$为两个共现于某关系$e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$的输出属性。在${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}$中，设${A}_{0}\left( { = A}\right) ,{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell },{A}_{\ell  + 1}\left( { = {A}^{\prime }}\right)$为满足以下条件的属性序列：(1)${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell } \in  \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}$；(2)存在$\ell  + 1$个关系${e}_{0},{e}_{1},\cdots ,{e}_{\ell } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$使得对每个$i \in  \{ 0,1,\cdots ,\ell \}$，都有${A}_{i},{A}_{i + 1} \in  {e}_{i}$；(3)对于任意非连续属性对${A}_{i},{A}_{j}$（其中$i,j$满足$\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$），不存在同时包含${A}_{i},{A}_{j}$的关系${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$。注意到根据$\exists$连通性定义，对于其他任何关系$e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{1},e \cap  \left\{  {{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell }}\right\}   = \varnothing$也成立。因此，(3)等价于：对于任意非连续属性对${A}_{i},{A}_{j}$（其中$I,j$满足$\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$），不存在同时包含${A}_{i},{A}_{j}$的关系${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$。若$\ell  \geq  1$，则我们已识别出环${A}_{0}\left( { = A}\right) ,{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell },{A}_{\ell  + 1}\left( { = {A}^{\prime }}\right)$，这与$Q$无环的性质矛盾。故$\ell  < 1$，这意味着${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell }$不存在；换言之，$A,{A}^{\prime }$在${\mathcal{E}}_{1}$的某个共同关系中存在。由于$Q$无环，必存在关系${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$使得$A,{A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell },{A}^{\prime } \in  {e}^{\prime }$；否则将形成环，与$Q$非无环矛盾。由于${A}_{1},{A}_{2},\cdots ,{A}_{\ell } \in  {e}^{\prime },{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{1}$，这与$A,{A}^{\prime }$不在${\mathcal{E}}_{1}$同一关系中的前提矛盾。再次地，令每个属性$A \in  S$表示具有$h\left( A\right)  = 1$的独立随机变量。令其余属性$B \in  \mathcal{V} - S$表示具有$h\left( B\right)  = 0$的常量。易证$h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$。我们断言必存在满足${\mathbf{y}}_{1} \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$的节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$。考虑树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$，其中每个节点$v \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$满足${\chi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  = \chi \left( v\right)  - \left( {\mathbf{y} - {\mathbf{y}}_{1}}\right)$。注意到$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$也是${Q}_{1}$的有效自由连接树分解，因此必存在某节点$u \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$满足${\chi }^{\prime }\left( u\right)  = {\mathbf{y}}_{1}$。相应地，${\mathbf{y}}_{1} \subseteq  \chi \left( u\right)$。故$h\left( {\chi \left( u\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{A \in  S}}h\left( A\right)  = \left| S\right|  = \operatorname{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

## B YANNAKAKIS ALGORITHM REVISITED

## 重探杨纳卡基斯算法

### B.1 Worst-case Optimality

### B.1 最坏情况最优性

Next, we will give a new analysis of the Yannakakis algorithm by a more fine-grained inspection of its execution. Similar to Algorithm 3, we first remove all dangling tuples, decompose the query into a set of $\exists$ -connected subqueries,compute their query results separately and finally combine their results via joins. Below, we focus on 3-connected acyclic queries.

接下来，我们将通过更精细的执行过程分析，对杨纳卡基斯算法进行新的研究。与算法3类似，我们首先移除所有悬空元组，将查询分解为一组$\exists$连通子查询，分别计算它们的查询结果，最后通过连接操作合并结果。下文重点讨论3-连通无环查询。

In the proof of Lemma A.5,we have identified a width-1 TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ such that its corresponding free-connex TD $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$ defined by the Yannakakis algorithm has width $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  =$ fn-fhtw(Q). Recall that for each node $u \in$ nodes $\left( \mathcal{T}\right) ,{\chi }^{\prime }\left( u\right)$ is the set of attributes on which the intermediate join results will be materialized by the Yannakakis algorithm. Hence, the intermediate join size can be bounded by $O\left( {N}^{\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }\right)$ . We obtain:

在引理A.5的证明中，我们确定了一个宽度为1的树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，使得杨纳卡基斯算法定义的对应自由连接树分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)$的宽度为$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\chi }^{\prime }}\right)  =$fn-fhtw(Q)。注意对于每个节点$u \in$，节点集$\left( \mathcal{T}\right) ,{\chi }^{\prime }\left( u\right)$是杨纳卡基斯算法物化中间连接结果的属性集合。因此，中间连接结果大小可被$O\left( {N}^{\operatorname{fn} - \operatorname{fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }\right)$限定。我们得到：

THEOREM B.1. For any acyclic query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT,the Yannakakis algorithm can compute $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ within $O\left( {{N}^{\text{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } + \text{OUT}}\right)$ time.

定理B.1. 对于任意无环查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$和输入规模为$\mathcal{R}$、输出规模为OUT的实例，杨纳卡基斯算法可在$O\left( {{N}^{\text{fn-fhtw}\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } + \text{OUT}}\right)$时间内计算出$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$。

For any acyclic query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ with $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,fn-fhtw $\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . Hence,we can show that ${N}^{\text{fn-fhtw }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } + \mathrm{{OUT}} = O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack  }\right) }\right)$ . Therefore,the Yannakakis algorithm is worst-case optimal for acyclic queries.

对于满足$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的任意无环查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，fn-fhtw$\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathbf{y}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。因此可证${N}^{\text{fn-fhtw }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } + \mathrm{{OUT}} = O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( {q\left\lbrack  \mathrm{y}\right\rbrack  }\right) }\right)$。由此可见，杨纳卡基斯算法对无环查询具有最坏情况最优性。

### B.2 a-hierarchical Query

### B.2 层次化查询

THEOREM B.2. For any a-hierarchical query $Q$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ of input size $N$ and output size OUT,the Yannakakis algorithm computes $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ in $O\left( {N \cdot  {\operatorname{OUT}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time.

定理B.2. 对于任意层次化查询$Q$和输入规模为$\mathcal{R}$、输出规模为OUT的实例，杨纳卡基斯算法可在$O\left( {N \cdot  {\operatorname{OUT}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\operatorname{fn} \cdot  \operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间内计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$。

We also resort to the same outline as Algorithm 3, except that we invoke the Yannakakis algorithm at lines 4-5. It suffices to assume that the input join-query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is acyclic, cleansed, and existentially connected. By definition, $Q$ is hierarchical. Moreover, $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ has an attribute tree $\mathcal{H}$ ,such that (i) there is a one-to-one correspondence between attributes in $\mathcal{V}$ and nodes in $\mathcal{H}$ ; (ii) each relation corresponds to a leaf-to-root path; (iii) every leaf node (that corresponds to a unique attribute) must be an output attribute. Note that $\left| \mathcal{E}\right|  =$ fn-fhtw. In $\mathcal{H}$ ,let ${\mathcal{H}}_{x}$ be the subtree of $\mathcal{H}$ rooted at attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ . Let ${Q}_{x}$ be the sub-query derived by relations that contain attribute $x$ , i.e., ${\mathcal{Q}}_{x} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{x},{\mathcal{E}}_{x},{\mathbf{y}}_{x}}\right)$ ,where ${\mathcal{V}}_{x} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{x}}}e$ and ${\mathbf{y}}_{x} = \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{x}$ . Let path $\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ denote the set of attributes lying on the path between ${x}_{1}$ and ${x}_{2}$ in $\mathcal{H}$ .

我们同样采用算法3的框架，区别在于第4-5行调用了Yannakakis算法。只需假设输入的连接查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$是无环、净化且存在性连接的。根据定义，$Q$具有层次性。此外，$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$存在属性树$\mathcal{H}$，满足：(i)属性与树节点一一对应；(ii)每个关系对应一条叶到根的路径；(iii)每个叶节点（对应唯一属性）必须是输出属性。注意$\left| \mathcal{E}\right|  =$满足fn-fhtw性质。在$\mathcal{H}$中，令${\mathcal{H}}_{x}$表示以属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$为根的子树。设${Q}_{x}$为由包含属性$x$的关系导出的子查询，即${\mathcal{Q}}_{x} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{x},{\mathcal{E}}_{x},{\mathbf{y}}_{x}}\right)$，其中${\mathcal{V}}_{x} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{x}}}e$且${\mathbf{y}}_{x} = \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{x}$。路径$\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$表示$\mathcal{H}$中${x}_{1}$与${x}_{2}$之间的属性集合。

Fig. 12. An illustration of the attribute tree (left) and width- 1 TD (right) of a cleansed, 3-connected, and hierarchical join-aggregate query $Q = { \oplus  }_{A,C,D,E}{R}_{1}\left( {A,B,D,H}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {A,B,D,I}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{3}\left( {A,B,E,J}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {A,B,E,K}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {A,C,F}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{6}\left( {A,C,G}\right) .$

图12. 净化、3-连接且层次化的连接-聚合查询$Q = { \oplus  }_{A,C,D,E}{R}_{1}\left( {A,B,D,H}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {A,B,D,I}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{3}\left( {A,B,E,J}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {A,B,E,K}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {A,C,F}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{6}\left( {A,C,G}\right) .$的属性树示意图（左）与宽度1树分解（右）

<!-- Media -->

We build a width-1 TD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ for $Q$ as follows. Consider the children of the root attribute in $\mathcal{H}$ . Let $\left\{  {{\mathcal{E}}_{1},{\mathcal{E}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{E}}_{j}}\right\}$ be a partition of $\mathcal{E}$ such that all relations in ${\mathcal{E}}_{i}$ shares one common child attribute. We build a width-1 TD $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ for each group ${\mathcal{E}}_{i}$ ,and then add ${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$ for every $i < j$ as the last(j - 1)child nodes of the root of ${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$ . See an example in Figure 12. We apply the Yannakakis algorithm along such a width-1 TD by traversing nodes in a post order.

我们按如下方式构建$Q$的宽度1树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$：考虑$\mathcal{H}$中根属性的子属性。设$\left\{  {{\mathcal{E}}_{1},{\mathcal{E}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{E}}_{j}}\right\}$是$\mathcal{E}$的划分，使得${\mathcal{E}}_{i}$中所有关系共享一个子属性。为每个分组${\mathcal{E}}_{i}$构建宽度1树分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$，然后将每个$i < j$对应的${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$添加为${\mathcal{T}}_{j}$根节点的最后(j-1)个子节点。示例见图12。我们通过后序遍历沿该宽度1树分解应用Yannakakis算法。

Below, we aim to bound the number of intermediate join results materialized by the Yannakakis algorithm. Let $q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ be the corresponding join query of $\mathcal{Q}$ . For simplicity,we define

接下来，我们将界定Yannakakis算法物化的中间连接结果数量。设$q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$为$\mathcal{Q}$对应的连接查询。为简化表述，定义

$$
{\lambda }_{q,\mathbf{y},\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right)  = \left| {{\pi }_{\operatorname{path}\left( {x,r}\right)  \cup  {\mathbf{y}}_{x}}q\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right| 
$$

for attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ . The number of intermediate join result that materialized is exactly $O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{x \in  \mathcal{H}}}{\lambda }_{\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right) }\right)$ . Let ${\mathfrak{R}}_{Q}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ denote the class of all input instances of input size $N$ and output size $\mathrm{{OUT}}$ for $Q$ . We can further rewrite the size bound above as:

对于属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$，物化的中间连接结果数量恰好为$O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{x \in  \mathcal{H}}}{\lambda }_{\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right) }\right)$。设${\mathfrak{R}}_{Q}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$表示所有输入规模为$N$、输出规模为$\mathrm{{OUT}}$的$Q$输入实例类。我们可以将上述规模界限改写为：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\mathfrak{R}}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{x \in  \mathcal{H}}}{\lambda }_{\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right)  \leq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{x \in  \mathcal{H}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\mathfrak{R}}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) }}{\lambda }_{\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right) 
$$

Then,it suffices to prove for an arbitrary attribute $x \in  \mathcal{H} : \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\Re }_{\mathcal{Q}}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) }}{\lambda }_{\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right)  \leq  N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{X}\right| }}$ .

因此，只需证明任意属性$x \in  \mathcal{H} : \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\Re }_{\mathcal{Q}}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) }}{\lambda }_{\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right)  \leq  N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{X}\right| }}$成立即可。

As $\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{x}\right|  \leq$ fn-fhtw,we come to the desired result. Let’s take a closer look at the sub-query ${\lambda }_{\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right)$ derived, which is captured by the class of generalized star query below. Implied by Lemma B.4, we complete the whole proof.

由于$\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{x}\right|  \leq$满足fn-fhtw条件，我们得出目标结论。下面仔细分析推导出的子查询${\lambda }_{\mathcal{R}}\left( x\right)$，它由以下广义星型查询类所刻画。根据引理B.4的推论，我们完成整个证明。

Definition B.3 (Generalized Star). A cleansed, $\exists$ -connected and hierarchical query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is a generalized star if $\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}e$ .

定义B.3（广义星型）。一个经过净化、$\exists$连通且层次化的查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，当满足$\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}e$时称为广义星型查询。

LEMMA B.4. For a generalized star query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ of $k$ relations,given any parameter $1 \leq  N$ and $\mathrm{{OUT}} \leq  {N}^{k}$ ,we have $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{Q}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) }}\left| {{ \bowtie  }_{e \in  \mathcal{E}}{R}_{e}}\right|  = O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$ .

引理B.4。对于包含$k$个关系的广义星型查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，给定任意参数$1 \leq  N$和$\mathrm{{OUT}} \leq  {N}^{k}$，我们有$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{Q}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) }}\left| {{ \bowtie  }_{e \in  \mathcal{E}}{R}_{e}}\right|  = O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$。

Proof. We distinguish two more cases on $Q$ . If $\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}e \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$ . In this case, $\left| {{ \bowtie  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}}{R}_{{e}^{\prime }}}\right|  =$ OUT holds for an arbitrary instance $\mathcal{R} \in  {\mathfrak{R}}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ . As $\mathrm{{OUT}} \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\left| {R}_{e}\right|$ ,this result automatically holds. In the remaining,we focus on the case when $\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}e - \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ . Let $\mathbf{z} = \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}e \cap  \mathbf{y}$ . Our proof consists of two steps:

证明。我们针对$Q$分两种情况讨论。若$\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}e \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$成立，则对于任意实例$\mathcal{R} \in  {\mathfrak{R}}_{\mathcal{Q}}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$，$\left| {{ \bowtie  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}}{R}_{{e}^{\prime }}}\right|  =$输出条件成立。由于$\mathrm{{OUT}} \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\left| {R}_{e}\right|$，该结果自然成立。余下我们重点讨论$\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}e - \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$情形。设$\mathbf{z} = \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}e \cap  \mathbf{y}$，证明分为两步：

Step 1. Consider a derived sub-query ${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime }}\right)$ where ${\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } = \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{z},{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } : e \in  \mathcal{E}}\right\}$ and ${\mathbf{y}}^{\prime } = \mathbf{y} - \mathbf{z}$ . Note that $\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right|  = \left| \mathcal{E}\right|$ . We will prove

步骤1. 考虑一个派生子查询${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime }}\right)$，其中${\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } = \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{z},{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } : e \in  \mathcal{E}}\right\}$且${\mathbf{y}}^{\prime } = \mathbf{y} - \mathbf{z}$。注意$\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right|  = \left| \mathcal{E}\right|$。我们将证明

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\Re }_{{Q}^{\prime }}\left( {N,\mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) }}\left| {{ \bowtie  }_{e \in  \mathcal{E}}{R}_{e}}\right|  = O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}}\right) 
$$

with a similar argument made in [7]. Let $\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}e = \{ B\}$ be the unique non-output attribute appearing in all relations from ${Q}^{\prime }$ . Suppose $\operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)  = \left\{  {{b}_{1},{b}_{2},\cdots ,{b}_{\ell }}\right\}$ . We introduce a variable ${\Delta }_{i}^{e}$ for each value ${b}_{i}$ to denote the number of input tuples that display ${b}_{i}$ in attribute $B$ in relation ${R}_{e}$ . The largest number of full join result can be produced is captured as follows:

采用与文献[7]类似的论证方法。设$\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}e = \{ B\}$为${Q}^{\prime }$中所有关系共有的唯一非输出属性。假设$\operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)  = \left\{  {{b}_{1},{b}_{2},\cdots ,{b}_{\ell }}\right\}$。我们为每个值${b}_{i}$引入变量${\Delta }_{i}^{e}$，用于表示关系${R}_{e}$中属性$B$上显示${b}_{i}$的输入元组数量。全连接结果的最大生成量可通过下式表示：

$$
\max .\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}{\Delta }_{i}^{e}\text{ subject to. }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{\Delta }_{i}^{e} \leq  N,\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}{\Delta }_{i}^{e} \leq  \mathrm{{OUT}},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  
$$

The optimal solution $\Theta \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}}\right)$ is achieved when ${\Delta }_{i}^{e} = {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}$ for any $i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ and $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ , and $\ell  = \frac{N}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right|  \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}}$ .

当满足以下条件时获得最优解$\Theta \left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}}\right)$：对任意$i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$和$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$有${\Delta }_{i}^{e} = {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}$，且$\ell  = \frac{N}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right|  \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{\frac{1}{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}}$。

Step 2. Consider an arbitrary instance $\mathcal{R}$ of $\mathcal{Q}$ . Let ${z}_{1},{z}_{2},\cdots ,{z}_{\ell }$ be the values in the effective domain of $\mathbf{z}$ in $\mathcal{R}$ . Let ${N}_{i} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{z} = {z}_{i}}{R}_{j}}\right|$ and ${\operatorname{OUT}}_{i} = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{z} = {z}_{i}}Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$ . Implied by Step 1,the largest number of full join result can be produced is at most $O\left( {{N}_{i} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{i}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }}}\right)$ . Given the following two constraints $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{N}_{i} = N$ ,and $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{i} = \mathrm{{OUT}}$ ,the largest number of full join result produced can be bounded by $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{N}_{i} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{i}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{N}_{i} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}} \leq  N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }}$ . As $\left| \mathcal{E}\right|  = k$ ,this is exactly $O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$ .

步骤二：考虑$\mathcal{R}$的任意实例$\mathcal{Q}$。设${z}_{1},{z}_{2},\cdots ,{z}_{\ell }$为$\mathcal{R}$中$\mathbf{z}$有效域内的值。令${N}_{i} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{z} = {z}_{i}}{R}_{j}}\right|$且${\operatorname{OUT}}_{i} = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{z} = {z}_{i}}Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$。根据步骤一可知，能产生的完整连接结果最大数量不超过$O\left( {{N}_{i} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{i}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }}}\right)$。在给定以下两个约束条件$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{N}_{i} = N$和$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{i} = \mathrm{{OUT}}$时，产生的完整连接结果最大数量可被限定为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{N}_{i} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{i}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}{N}_{i} \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}} \leq  N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }}$。由于$\left| \mathcal{E}\right|  = k$，该值恰好等于$O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}}\right)$。

Combining these two steps, we complete the whole proof.

结合这两个步骤，我们完成了整个证明。

### B.3 Non-a-hierarchical Query

### B.3 非层次化查询

As shown in [31],Yannakakis algorithm indeed incurs $\Theta (N$ . OUT) time for line queries. We first revisit the hard instance constructed for line-3 query as shown in Figure 13. Consider an arbitrary acyclic but non-a-hierarchical join-aggregate query $Q$ . Let ${Q}^{\prime }$ be its cleansed version. Let ${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\cdots ,{Q}_{h}$ be the connected sub-queries in ${G}_{{Q}^{\prime }}^{\exists }$ . As $Q$ is acyclic,every sub-query ${Q}_{i}$ is also acyclic. As $Q$ is non-a-hierarchical,at least one sub-query is not hierarchical,say ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}$ . There must exist a pair of attributes $x,{x}^{\prime }$ and three relations $e,{e}^{\prime },{e}^{\prime \prime }$ such that $x \in  e \cap  {e}^{\prime } - {e}^{\prime \prime }$ and ${x}^{\prime } \in  {e}^{\prime } \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime } - e$ . Let $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ be a width-1 TD of ${Q}_{1}$ . As ${Q}_{1}$ is cleansed,every leaf node must contain some unique attribute.

如文献[31]所示，Yannakakis算法在处理线性查询时确实会产生$\Theta (N$时间开销。我们首先重新审视图13中为三路线性查询构建的困难实例。考虑任意一个无环但非层次化的连接-聚合查询$Q$，设${Q}^{\prime }$为其净化后的版本，${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\cdots ,{Q}_{h}$表示${G}_{{Q}^{\prime }}^{\exists }$中的连通子查询。由于$Q$是无环查询，每个子查询${Q}_{i}$也必然无环。又因$Q$是非层次化查询，至少存在一个非层次化的子查询（例如${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}$），其中必然存在属性对$x,{x}^{\prime }$和三个关系$e,{e}^{\prime },{e}^{\prime \prime }$满足$x \in  e \cap  {e}^{\prime } - {e}^{\prime \prime }$且${x}^{\prime } \in  {e}^{\prime } \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime } - e$。设$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$是${Q}_{1}$的宽度为1的树分解，由于${Q}_{1}$是净化后的查询，每个叶节点必须包含某些唯一属性。

Fig. 13. An illustration of hard instance for Yannakakis algorithm on line query with $k = 3$ [31].

图13. Yannakakis算法在$k = 3$线性查询上的困难实例示意图[31]。

<!-- Media -->

It is always feasible to find a pair of leaf nodes ${e}_{1},{e}_{n}$ in $\mathcal{T}$ such that $e,{e}^{\prime \prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{n}}\right) ,e \in$ path $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}^{\prime \prime }}\right)$ and ${e}^{\prime \prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}^{\prime },{e}_{n}}\right)$ . It could be possible that $e = {e}_{1}$ or ${e}^{\prime \prime } = {e}_{n}$ . Let ${A}_{1},{A}_{n + 1}$ be an arbitrary unique output attribute in ${e}_{1},{e}_{n}$ separately. As ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}$ is 3-connected,there must exists a subset $S$ of $m$ relations in path $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{n}}\right)$ (including ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{n}$ ) and a subset of non-output attributes ${B}_{1},{B}_{2},\cdots ,{B}_{m}$ such that ${B}_{1} \in  {e}_{1},{B}_{n} \in  {e}_{n},{B}_{i},{B}_{i + 1} \in  {e}_{i}$ for some relation ${e}_{i} \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{n}}\right)$ ,and there exists no relation ${e}^{\prime }$ such that ${B}_{i},{B}_{j} \in  {e}^{\prime }$ for any $\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$ . Otherwise, ${Q}_{1}$ is not $\exists$ -connected.

总能在$\mathcal{T}$中找到一对叶节点${e}_{1},{e}_{n}$，使得$e,{e}^{\prime \prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{n}}\right) ,e \in$路径$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}^{\prime \prime }}\right)$与${e}^{\prime \prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}^{\prime },{e}_{n}}\right)$成立。可能存在$e = {e}_{1}$或${e}^{\prime \prime } = {e}_{n}$的情况。设${A}_{1},{A}_{n + 1}$为${e}_{1},{e}_{n}$中任意唯一的独立输出属性。由于${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}$是3-连通的，必然存在路径$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{n}}\right)$中$m$关系的子集$S$（包含${e}_{1}$和${e}_{n}$）以及非输出属性子集${B}_{1},{B}_{2},\cdots ,{B}_{m}$，使得对于某个关系${e}_{i} \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{n}}\right)$满足${B}_{1} \in  {e}_{1},{B}_{n} \in  {e}_{n},{B}_{i},{B}_{i + 1} \in  {e}_{i}$，且不存在任何关系${e}^{\prime }$能对任意$\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$实现${B}_{i},{B}_{j} \in  {e}^{\prime }$。否则，${Q}_{1}$不具备$\exists$-连通性。

Give an instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{line }}$ of ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{line }}$ with $k = 3$ ,we construct an instance $\mathcal{R}$ for $Q$ as follows. We set $\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)  = \{  * \}$ for every attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V} - \left\{  {{C}_{1},{C}_{2},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},\cdots ,{B}_{m}}\right\}$ . We use ${C}_{1}$ to simulate ${A}_{1}$ , use ${C}_{2}$ to simulate ${A}_{4},{B}_{1}$ to simulate ${A}_{2}$ and all remaining attributes ${B}_{2},{B}_{3},\cdots ,{B}_{m}$ to simulate ${A}_{3}$ . For any relation $e$ ,such that $\left| {e \cap  \left\{  {{B}_{2},{B}_{3},\cdots ,{B}_{m}}\right\}  }\right|  > 1$ ,the projection of ${R}_{e}$ onto $\left\{  {{B}_{2},{B}_{3},\cdots ,{B}_{m}}\right\}$ should be a one-to-one mapping. The argument for line-3 query can be applied for $Q$ similarly,i.e., any query plan of the Yannakakis algorithm requires $\Theta \left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$ time.

给定${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{line }}$的实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\text{line }}$（含$k = 3$），我们按如下方式构建$Q$的实例$\mathcal{R}$：为每个属性$x \in  \mathcal{V} - \left\{  {{C}_{1},{C}_{2},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},\cdots ,{B}_{m}}\right\}$设置$\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)  = \{  * \}$，用${C}_{1}$模拟${A}_{1}$，${C}_{2}$模拟${A}_{4},{B}_{1}$，${A}_{2}$模拟剩余所有属性${B}_{2},{B}_{3},\cdots ,{B}_{m}$以再现${A}_{3}$。对于满足$\left| {e \cap  \left\{  {{B}_{2},{B}_{3},\cdots ,{B}_{m}}\right\}  }\right|  > 1$的关系$e$，其${R}_{e}$在$\left\{  {{B}_{2},{B}_{3},\cdots ,{B}_{m}}\right\}$上的投影应为一对一映射。线-3查询的论证可类推至$Q$，即Yannakakis算法的任何查询方案均需$\Theta \left( {N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right)$时间。

## C OUTPUT SIZE ESTIMATION

## C 输出规模估算

### C.1 Line Queries [20]

### C.1 线性查询 [20]

It has been shown that how to obtain a constant-factor approximation of OUT for line queries in near-linear time [20]. We borrow the technique of $k$ minimum values (KMV) [10,14],which is more commonly used to estimate the number of distinct elements in the streaming model. KMV works by applying a hash function to the input items,and keeping the $k$ minimum hash values,denoted as ${v}_{1},{v}_{2},\cdots ,{v}_{k}$ . It has been shown that,with $k = O\left( \frac{1}{{\epsilon }^{2}}\right)$ ,the estimator $\frac{k - 1}{{v}_{k}}$ is an $\left( {1 + \epsilon }\right)$ -approximation of the number of distinct items in the data stream, with at least constant probability. Moreover, given the KMVs of two sets, the KMV of the union of the two sets can be computed by simply "merging" the two KMVs,i.e.,keeping the $k$ minimum values of the ${2k}$ values from the two KMVs, provided that they use the same hash function.

已有研究表明，如何在近线性时间内获取线查询OUT值的常数因子近似解[20]。我们借鉴了最小哈希值(KMV)技术[10,14]，该技术更常用于估算流模型中的唯一元素数量。KMV通过对输入项应用哈希函数，保留$k$个最小哈希值（记为${v}_{1},{v}_{2},\cdots ,{v}_{k}$）实现。当$k = O\left( \frac{1}{{\epsilon }^{2}}\right)$时，估算器$\frac{k - 1}{{v}_{k}}$能以至少恒定概率给出数据流中唯一元素数量的$\left( {1 + \epsilon }\right)$近似值。此外，给定两个集合的KMV，只需"合并"这两个KMV（即从${2k}$个哈希值中保留$k$个最小值）即可计算其并集的KMV，前提是它们使用相同的哈希函数。

On a line query,for each $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)$ ,we will obtain a constant-factor approximation of ${\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a} =$ $\left| {{\pi }_{{A}_{n + 1}}{R}_{1}\left( {a,{A}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  \cdots  \bowtie  {R}_{n}\left( {{A}_{n},{A}_{n + 1}}\right) }\right|$ . Note that $\mathrm{{OUT}} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right) }}{\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a}$ . We compute a hash value for each distinct value in $\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{n + 1}\right)$ to do so. Then it suffices to compute,for each $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)$ ,a KMV (with a constant $k$ ) over all distinct values in $\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{n + 1}\right)$ that can join with $a$ . This can be done by using the merge operation above to compute the min. More precisely, for $i = n,n - 1,\ldots ,1$ ,we compute the KMV for each $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i}\right)$ ,by merging all the KMVs for $b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i + 1}\right)$ such that $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{i + 1}}\right)$ .

在线查询中，对于每个$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)$，我们将获得${\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a} =$$\left| {{\pi }_{{A}_{n + 1}}{R}_{1}\left( {a,{A}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{A}_{2},{A}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  \cdots  \bowtie  {R}_{n}\left( {{A}_{n},{A}_{n + 1}}\right) }\right|$的常数因子近似值。注意$\mathrm{{OUT}} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right) }}{\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a}$。为此，我们为$\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{n + 1}\right)$中每个唯一值计算哈希值。然后只需为每个$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)$计算能与$a$连接的$\operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{n + 1}\right)$中所有唯一值的KMV（采用常数$k$）。这可通过上述合并操作计算最小值实现。具体而言，对于$i = n,n - 1,\ldots ,1$，我们通过合并满足$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{i + 1}}\right)$条件的所有$b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i + 1}\right)$的KMV，为每个$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{i}\right)$计算KMV。

The KMV obtained from each $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)$ gives us a constant-factor approximation of ${\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a}$ only with constant probability. To boost the success probability,we run $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ instances of this algorithm in parallel using independent random hash functions and return the median estimator for each ${\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a}$ . This boosts the success probability to $1 - 1/{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }$ for each ${\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a}$ . By a union bound, the probability that all estimators are constant-factor approximations is also $1 - 1/{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }$ . Then, we also have a constant-factor approximation of OUT. The runtime of this algorithm is $\widetilde{O}\left( N\right)$ .

从每个$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {A}_{1}\right)$获取的KMV仅以恒定概率给出${\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a}$的常数因子近似值。为提高成功率，我们使用独立随机哈希函数并行运行$O\left( {\log N}\right)$个算法实例，并返回每个${\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a}$的中位数估算值。这将每个${\mathrm{{OUT}}}_{a}$的成功概率提升至$1 - 1/{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }$。根据联合边界原理，所有估算值均为常数因子近似的概率同样为$1 - 1/{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }$。由此我们也得到了OUT的常数因子近似值。该算法运行时长为$\widetilde{O}\left( N\right)$。

### C.2 Estimate OUT on the fly [31]

### C.2 动态估算OUT值[31]

However, it is unknown how to extend the KMV-based techniques to general acyclic queries. In [31], Hu showed a method that can obtain a 2-approximation of OUT on the fly. As shown in Algorithm 8, it iteratively compresses the input instances and invokes Algorithm 6 as a primitive, which has been described in Section 5.

然而，如何将基于KMV的技术推广至一般无环查询仍是未知领域。在[31]中，胡提出了一种动态获取OUT值2-近似解的方法。如算法8所示，该方法通过迭代压缩输入实例并调用第5节所述的算法6作为基础操作实现。

### C.3 Double Guess of OUT [25]

### C.3 对OUT值的双重猜测 [25]

We can doubly guess the value of OUT starting from $1,2,4,\cdots$ . For the $i$ -th trial,we run the algorithm for $c \cdot  N \cdot  {\left( {2}^{i}\right) }^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}$ time before terminating the simulation if the execution is not completed,for some sufficiently large constant $c$ . The overall time complexity is $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left\lbrack  {\log \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right\rbrack  \right\rbrack  }}c$ . $\left( {N \cdot  {\left( {2}^{i}\right) }^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \mathrm{{fhtw}}\left( Q\right) }} + {2}^{i}}\right)  = O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \mathrm{{fhtw}}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) .$

我们可以从$1,2,4,\cdots$开始对OUT值进行双重猜测。在第$i$次试验中，若执行未完成，我们会在终止模拟前运行算法$c \cdot  N \cdot  {\left( {2}^{i}\right) }^{1 - \frac{1}{k}}$次（设$c$为足够大的常数）。总体时间复杂度为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left\lbrack  {\log \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right\rbrack  \right\rbrack  }}c$。$\left( {N \cdot  {\left( {2}^{i}\right) }^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \mathrm{{fhtw}}\left( Q\right) }} + {2}^{i}}\right)  = O\left( {N \cdot  {\mathrm{{OUT}}}^{1 - \frac{1}{\mathrm{{fn}} - \mathrm{{fhtw}}\left( Q\right) }} + \mathrm{{OUT}}}\right) .$

算法8：无OUT混合Yannakakis算法 $\left( {\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right) ,\mathcal{R}}\right)$

if $\left| \mathcal{E}\right|  = 1$ ,say $\mathcal{E} = \{ e\}$ then return $\oplus  {\mathcal{V}}_{-\mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}$ ;

若$\left| \mathcal{E}\right|  = 1$（设为$\mathcal{E} = \{ e\}$）则返回$\oplus  {\mathcal{V}}_{-\mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}$；

${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \leftarrow  \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : e \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing \}$

$e \leftarrow$ an arbitrary relation in $\in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ ;

$e \leftarrow$为$\in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$中的任意关系；

Put an ordering on elements in ${\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}$ as ${a}_{1},{a}_{2},{a}_{3},\cdots$ ;

将${\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}{R}_{e}$中元素按${a}_{1},{a}_{2},{a}_{3},\cdots$排序；

${S}_{e}^{\left( 0\right) } \leftarrow  {R}_{e},i \leftarrow  1$

while true do

循环执行

		${S}_{e}^{\left( i\right) } \leftarrow  \varnothing$

		foreach $t \in  {S}_{e}^{\left( i - 1\right) }$ do

		遍历$t \in  {S}_{e}^{\left( i - 1\right) }$：

			Suppose ${\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}t = {a}_{j}$ ;

			假设${\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}t = {a}_{j}$；

			${t}^{\prime } \leftarrow$ a tuple with ${\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}{t}^{\prime } = {a}_{\left\lfloor  \frac{j + 1}{2}\right\rfloor  }$ and ${\pi }_{A}{t}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{A}t$ for any attribute $A \in  e - \mathrm{y}$ ;

			${t}^{\prime } \leftarrow$为包含${\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}{t}^{\prime } = {a}_{\left\lfloor  \frac{j + 1}{2}\right\rfloor  }$的元组，且对任意属性$A \in  e - \mathrm{y}$满足${\pi }_{A}{t}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{A}t$；

			${S}_{e}^{\left( i\right) } \leftarrow  {S}_{e}^{\left( i\right) } \cup  \left\{  {t}^{\prime }\right\}$

		if $\left| {{\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}{S}_{e}^{\left( i\right) }}\right|  = 1$ then break;

		若$\left| {{\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}{S}_{e}^{\left( i\right) }}\right|  = 1$则跳出循环；

		$i \leftarrow  i + 1$

${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \leftarrow  {R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {S}_{e}^{\left( i\right) };$

	${Q}^{\prime } \leftarrow  \left( {\mathcal{V} - e \cap  \mathbf{y},\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} ,\mathbf{y} - e}\right) ;$

${\mathcal{J}}^{\left( i\right) } \leftarrow  \left( {{\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}{S}_{e}^{\left( i\right) }}\right)  \times$ HYBRIDYANNAKAKISWITHOUTOUT $\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime },\mathcal{R} - \left\{  {R}_{e}\right\}  }\right)$ ;

${\mathcal{J}}^{\left( i\right) } \leftarrow  \left( {{\pi }_{e \cap  \mathrm{y}}{S}_{e}^{\left( i\right) }}\right)  \times$ 无OUT混合Yannakakis算法 $\left( {{\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime },\mathcal{R} - \left\{  {R}_{e}\right\}  }\right)$；

while $i > 0$ do

当$i > 0$时执行

		${\mathcal{J}}^{\left( i - 1\right) } \leftarrow$ HYBRIDYANNAKAKIS $\left( {\mathcal{Q},\mathcal{R} - \left\{  {R}_{e}\right\}   + \left\{  {S}_{e}^{\left( i - 1\right) }\right\}  ,2 \cdot  \left| {\mathcal{J}}^{\left( i\right) }\right| }\right)$ ; - Algorithm 6;

		${\mathcal{J}}^{\left( i - 1\right) } \leftarrow$ 混合Yannakakis算法 $\left( {\mathcal{Q},\mathcal{R} - \left\{  {R}_{e}\right\}   + \left\{  {S}_{e}^{\left( i - 1\right) }\right\}  ,2 \cdot  \left| {\mathcal{J}}^{\left( i\right) }\right| }\right)$； - 算法6；

		$i \leftarrow  i - 1$

return ${\mathcal{J}}^{\left( 0\right) }$ ;
